  797 ## Card
  798 
  799 | Field | Contents |
  800 |---|---|
  801 | State | Stage 1, slice 1a. Objective: two authorised claimants contend for one shared resource unit, settled atomically and observed immutably. Consumer: the Stage 1b reservation slice, which needs a trustworthy availability boundary and settlement path before it can add two-tick actions. Prerequisites: none. Review status: no independent review requested or held; Stage 1 exit remains unclaimed. |
  802 | Scope | In: immutable world state, per-actor read-only views, proposal validation, declared deterministic ordering, all-or-nothing settlement of balanced effects, immutable tick records with explicit outcome reasons, canonical serialization and digests, an inert diagnostics channel, focused deterministic tests. Owning modules: `kernel/`. Out: reservations, multi-tick or cancellable actions, replay and recovery, evidence streaming to disk, needs, movement, perception rules, memory, social behaviour, construction, viewers, plugins, a general scheduler, randomness, the six-person world and the 50-actor capacity workload. |
  803 | Identity | Adoption revision `2bf08e9e4629022adb508b5c6db53a144bb355b4` on branch `codex/kernel-first-slice`, containing `AGENTS.md`, `DOCTRINE.md` and `ROADMAP.md` together.   804 | Claim | Essential: one contested source unit yields exactly one credited unit and exactly one winner; an invalid or unauthorised transaction commits no part of itself; an indivisible transaction against insufficient stock is rejected without partial credit; results are independent of input-map and proposal-collection order; the declared rotation gives no permanent winner over equivalent contention at successive ticks; the roadmap's A to B transfer/consume fixture honours the next-tick availability boundary and prevents double spending; observers cannot mutate live or previously observed state; diagnostics on and off, and repeated identical execution, give identical canonical results. Integrity rails on every tick: no negative balance, conserved declared total, every committed transaction's effects sum to zero, every submitted proposal carries exactly one explicit outcome reason.   806 | Instrument | The tests read the engine's own state objects and tick records through its public accessors; there is no second scorer or reconstructed ledger. Ordering is exercised by generating every permutation of the declared input maps and proposal collections, not by sampling. Null controls: an empty proposal collection, and an empty roster for the rotation rule. Known-negative controls: unauthorised claimant, insufficient source, insufficient balance, duplicate proposal identity, duplicate actor sequence, unbalanced effects, non-integer and non-positive amounts.   807 | Budget and attempt | Focused deterministic fixtures only. Whole-world executions authorised: 0. Used: 0. Remaining: 0. Capacity runs authorised: 0. No exploration levers are declared, because this slice answers a correctness question, not a design question. |

  809 
  810 ## Declarations
  811 
  812 These are fixed before implementation. Changing any of them after a recorded
  813 result creates a new attempt; it does not revise an existing one.
  814 
  815 ### Units and numeric rules
  816 
  817 - One resource kind exists in this slice, named `unit`. It is abstract; it
  818   acquires meaning in Stage 2, not here.
  819 - All quantities are Python integers. No floating-point value may enter state,
  820   a proposal, a tick record, or the canonical form; canonical serialization
  821   rejects them rather than formatting them.
  822 - `bool` is rejected as a quantity even though Python treats it as an integer.
  823 - Balances and source stocks are integers greater than or equal to zero, with no
  824   upper bound.
  825 - A transaction amount is an integer greater than or equal to one. Zero and
  826   negative amounts are malformed, not no-ops.
  827 - Units are indivisible: a transaction moves its exact declared amounts or
  828   nothing. There is no fractional or partial credit.
  829 - Conserved total, asserted after every tick:
  830   `sum(source stocks) + sum(actor balances) + consumption sink`.
  831 
  832 ### Accounts and ownership
  833 
  834 Three account kinds, addressed as `source:<id>`, `actor:<id>` and `sink:consumed`.
  835 
  836 - `source:<id>` is a shared source holding a stock, with a fixed set of
  837   authorised claimant actor identities.
  838 - `actor:<id>` is an actor's exclusive holding.
  839 - `sink:consumed` is the single declared consumption sink. It is the explicit
  840   rule for loss: consumed units leave circulation but stay in the conserved
  841   total, so consumption can never be confused with leakage.
  842 
  843 Authority is checked per debited account, for the whole transaction:
  844 
  845 - debiting `actor:X` requires the proposing actor to be `X`;
  846 - debiting `source:S` requires the proposing actor to be in `S`'s authorised set;
  847 - debiting `sink:consumed` is never permitted;
  848 - crediting requires no authority; an actor may be given units unasked.
  849 
  850 No module outside `kernel/settlement.py` writes a balance. There is no public
  851 mutator on any state object.
  852 
  853 ### Operations and balanced effects
  854 
  855 A transaction is a list of balanced effects whose signed amounts sum to zero.
  856 An operation is a named constructor for such a list. Three exist in this slice:
  857 
  858 | Operation | Parameters | Effects |
  859 |---|---|---|
  860 | `claim` | `sources`: a mapping of one or more source identities to positive integer amounts | debit each named source by its amount; credit the proposing actor the total |
  861 | `transfer` | `to`: an actor identity other than the proposer; `amount` | debit the proposer; credit `to` |
  862 | `consume` | `amount` | debit the proposer; credit `sink:consumed` |
  863 
  864 `claim` accepts several sources because a single-debit transaction cannot
  865 demonstrate that a rejected transaction commits none of its parts. A two-source
  866 claim is the smallest transaction that can prove all-or-nothing settlement, and
  867 the roadmap already requires one global order for multi-resource transactions.
  868 No other multi-effect operation is built.
  869 
  870 An effect list is rejected before commit if its amounts do not sum to zero, so a
  871 malformed operation cannot create or destroy units even if it passes every other
  872 check.
  873 
  874 ### Proposal identity and the actor-local sequence
  875 
  876 A proposal is `(proposal_id, actor, sequence, operation, params)`.
  877 
  878 - `proposal_id` is a non-empty string, unique across the tick. If two or more
  879   proposals share an identity, all of them are rejected with
  880   `denied_duplicate_proposal_id`, regardless of which arrived first.
  881 - `actor` must be present in the tick-start roster.
  882 - `sequence` is the actor-local proposal sequence: a non-negative integer by
  883   which the actor declares the order of its own proposals within the tick. It is
  884   carried on the proposal rather than assigned from arrival order, because the
  885   roadmap's fixture depends on "A to B is A's first proposal" and ordering may
  886   not depend on collection order. The engine validates it: if one actor submits
  887   two proposals with the same sequence, all proposals sharing that `(actor,
  888   sequence)` are rejected with `denied_duplicate_actor_sequence`.
  889 
  890 ### Ordering and rotation
  891 
  892 Resolution order is derived only from tick-start state and proposal content.
  893 
  894 1. `roster` is the tick-start actor identities sorted by Unicode code point.
  895 2. `k = tick mod len(roster)`; `rotated = roster[k:] + roster[:k]`. An empty
  896    roster resolves no actor transactions.
  897 3. `actor_rank` is the actor's index in `rotated`.
  898 4. The total order key is `(actor_rank, sequence)`.
  899 
  900 The roadmap's key is `(phase, rotated actor rank, actor-local sequence)`. The
  901 phase term is constant in this slice because reserved completions do not exist
  902 until slice 1b; the key is therefore implemented without it, and 1b restores it.
  903 
  904 Rotation is deterministic priority, not a fairness guarantee. It only removes a
  905 permanent winner among claimants that are otherwise equivalent.
  906 
  907 ### The availability boundary
  908 
  909 - At tick start, each account's available amount equals its tick-start balance.
  910 - A transaction is validated against remaining availability for every account it
  911   debits, as one unit of work.
  912 - Acceptance reduces that availability immediately. Rejection leaves it
  913   unchanged.
  914 - Credits never raise availability within the tick. A unit credited at tick `T`
  915   becomes spendable at tick `T+1`.
  916 - Accepted effects are staged and applied together at the end of the tick.
  917 
  918 ### Outcome reasons
  919 
  920 Every submitted proposal appears in the tick record with exactly one reason.
  921 Nothing disappears from the record.
  922 
  923 `accepted`, `denied_unknown_actor`, `denied_unknown_source`,
  924 `denied_unknown_operation`, `denied_malformed_params`,
  925 `denied_non_integer_amount`, `denied_non_positive_amount`,
  926 `denied_duplicate_proposal_id`, `denied_duplicate_actor_sequence`,
  927 `denied_unauthorised`, `denied_insufficient_source`,
  928 `denied_insufficient_balance`, `denied_unbalanced_effects`,
  929 `denied_self_transfer`.
  930 
  931 ### Canonical form
  932 
  933 Canonical bytes are UTF-8 JSON with sorted keys, `(",", ":")` separators and
  934 escaped non-ASCII. Integers only; floats and booleans are refused at
  935 serialization rather than formatted, so no platform number formatting can reach
  936 an identity. A digest is the SHA-256 hexadecimal of those bytes. State identity
  937 and tick-record identity are digests of the canonical form, so no incidental
  938 object order, insertion order or wall-clock value can enter them.
  939 
  940 ### Diagnostics
  941 
  942 The diagnostics channel receives already-canonical copies after each decision is
  943 fixed. It returns nothing the engine reads and allocates no identity. This slice
  944 allocates no identities at all, so the roadmap's stronger clause about
  945 diagnostics never perturbing identity allocation is only partly exercised here;
  946 that is recorded as a limitation, not a pass.
  947 
  948 ## Assertions
  949

 1041 ### Instrument validation
 1042 
 1043 The suite passed on its first complete run. That is not evidence that it checks
 1044 anything, so `evidence/stage-01/instrument/mutation_check.py` breaks one declared
 1045 rule in the kernel at a time and records which tests notice. Results:
 1046 
 1047 | Mutation | Tests that noticed |
 1048 |---|---:|
 1049 | rotation always starts at the sorted roster | 8 |
 1050 | a unit credited this tick becomes spendable this tick | 4 |
 1051 | authority over a debited account is never checked | 6 |
 1052 | resolution follows proposal arrival order | 7 |
 1053 | the record lists outcomes in arrival order | 3 |
 1054 | canonical bytes follow mapping insertion order | 1 |
 1055 | state hands out a live mutable mapping | 3 |
 1056 | a view points at the state's own mapping | 2 |
 1057 | only the first leg is checked against availability | 1 |
 1058 | proposals sharing an identity are resolved | 3 |
 1059 | one actor may reuse a sequence | 2 |
 1060 | a transaction may create or destroy units | 1 |
 1061 | an amount need not be a whole unit | 3 |
 1062 | the negative-balance rail is off | 2 |
 1063 | the conservation rail is off | 0, unreachable |
 1064 
 1065 The conservation rail cannot be reached by any input: a committed transaction
 1066 must already have balanced effects and touch only accounts inside the world, and
 1067 those two together conserve the total by construction. It is defence in depth
 1068 against a future slice, and the harness records it as declared-unreachable
 1069 rather than as a pass.
 1070 
 1071 ### Corrections made during attempt 1, preserved
 1072 
 1073 1. **The instrument was wrong before the kernel was.** The first mutation harness
 1074    reported the `authority-not-checked` failures again for `order-by-arrival`.
 1075    Both mutations change `kernel/settlement.py` by exactly 38 bytes, and both
 1076    runs fell inside one filesystem mtime second, so CPython accepted the previous
 1077    mutation's cached bytecode as current and the second run tested the first
 1078    mutation's code. The harness now disables bytecode writing, removes every
 1079    `__pycache__` before each run, and checks the mutated text is the text on
 1080    disk. Caught by running one mutation by hand and reading pytest's own output
 1081    instead of the harness summary.
 1082 
 1083 2. **The instrument altered the repository.** The same harness wrote files in
 1084    text mode, which converts LF to CRLF on Windows, so its "restore" was not byte
 1085    for byte and four kernel files were left with changed line endings. The
 1086    harness now reads and writes bytes and verifies the restore. The affected
 1087    files were normalised back to LF before the implementation commit.
 1088 
 1089 3. **Two real blind spots in the suite**, both found by the corrected harness and
 1090    both fixed by adding tests rather than by dropping the mutation:
 1091    `view-shares-the-live-mapping` was noticed by nothing, because handing an
 1092    observer the world's own mapping is unobservable while state is immutable;
 1093    `negative-balance-rail-off` was noticed by nothing, because the ordinary
 1094    validation path never reaches that rail. The first is now checked as a
 1095    containment property, the second by calling the commit step directly with the
 1096    staged effects that trip it.
 1097 
 1098 4. **A missing declared rule.** The original nine assertions never checked the
 1099    duplicate-identity and duplicate-sequence rules, although the declarations
 1100    state them and the roadmap requires the first. Added as A10.
 1101 
 1102 No assertion was weakened and no expected result was replaced at any point.
 1103 
 1104 ### Guarantees demonstrated
 1105 
 1106 - One contested unit produces exactly one winner and exactly one credit; the
 1107   loser carries an explicit reason; balances stay at or above zero; the declared
 1108   total is conserved.
 1109 - A transaction with an unauthorised, unknown or unaffordable leg commits none of
 1110   its legs, including the legs that would have succeeded alone.
 1111 - An indivisible transaction against insufficient stock is denied outright. Zero,
 1112   negative, fractional and boolean amounts are denied rather than rounded,
 1113   reversed or treated as no-ops.
 1114 - Every permutation of the genesis balance map, source map, authorised set,
 1115   claim source map and proposal collection gives one canonical result: 576
 1116   orderings, one digest.
 1117 - The rotation gives no permanent winner over equivalent contention at
 1118   successive ticks, for two and for three claimants, and a high-sorting identity
 1119   wins as often as a low-sorting one.
 1120 - The roadmap's credit fixture holds at both resolver orders, and a unit credited
 1121   this tick, whether transferred or claimed, is only spendable at the next tick.
 1122 - Views, states, sources, records, outcomes and effects all refuse mutation; a
 1123   view or record taken earlier is unchanged after later ticks; the maps handed to
 1124   the constructor and to a proposal can be edited afterwards with no effect.
 1125 - Diagnostics on and off give identical canonical results, a hostile sink that
 1126   corrupts its payload and raises changes nothing and is counted, and settlement
 1127   cannot import the diagnostics channel at all.
 1128 
 1129 ### Limitations
 1130 
 1131 - **Stage 1 is not complete and its exit gate is not claimed.** Slices 1b, 1c and
 1132   1d are not built: no reservations, no two-tick actions, no cancellation, no
 1133   sealed evidence stream, no replay, no recovery, no instance isolation, no
 1134   capacity benchmark.
 1135 - **No independent review has been requested, supplied or held.**
 1136 - The roadmap's clause that optional capture must not perturb identity allocation
 1137   is only partly exercised, because this slice allocates no identity of its own:
 1138   proposal identities are supplied by the caller. Slice 1c must satisfy it
 1139   properly.
 1140 - The tick record is in memory only. It is not the sealed evidence schema, and it
 1141   does not yet carry immutable copies of the inputs and alternatives a decision
 1142   used. It records identity, reason and committed effects. Slice 1c specifies the
 1143   schema and writes it.
 1144 - Contention over successive ticks is shown by sweeping the genesis tick, because
 1145   nothing in this slice produces resources, so a depleted source cannot be
 1146   refilled to contend over again inside one run. The engine's own tick-over-tick
 1147   rotation is checked separately across four consecutive ticks.
 1148 - Rotation is deterministic priority. It is not a fairness result, and it says
 1149   nothing about claimants whose opportunity or eligibility differ.
 1150 - `WorldView` gives every actor the whole boundary. Bounded perception is Stage 2.
 1151 - The conservation rail is unreachable by any current input, as recorded above.
 1152 - Amounts are unbounded Python integers. No overflow behaviour is defined,
 1153   because none exists at this size.
 1154 - Nothing here is a world, a person or a behaviour. No claim is made about six
 1155   people, fifty actors, performance, or any social or emergent property.
 1156 
 1157 ## Review
 1158 
 1159 No independent review has been requested, supplied or held. Stage 1 has three
 1160 further slices and its exit gate is not claimed. Under `AGENTS.md`, progression
 1161 past Stage 1 additionally requires a separate reviewer using a different model,
 1162 which has not happened.
 1163 
 1164 ## Rollback
 1165 
 1166 The work sits in four commits on `codex/kernel-first-slice` in a repository with
 1167 no remote. Nothing outside `C:\dev\03-Living-World-V3` was changed.
 1168 
 1169 To undo the slice but keep the adopted documents, revert or reset to
 1170 `bbbf142f1399c1642c567d3d9c78792610b2df0b`. To undo everything including the
 1171 adoption, reset to before `2bf08e9e4629022adb508b5c6db53a144bb355b4`, or delete
 1172 the repository directory.
 1173 
 1174 Exact files introduced by the slice, all of which may be deleted to remove it:

 1177   `ordering.py`, `outcomes.py`, `proposals.py`, `reasons.py`, `settlement.py`,
 1178   `state.py`, `units.py`, `version.py`
 1179 - `tests/__init__.py`, `support.py`, `test_atomicity.py`, `test_contention.py`,
 1180   `test_credit_boundary.py`, `test_dependency_direction.py`,
 1181   `test_determinism.py`, `test_indivisibility.py`, `test_integrity_rails.py`,
 1182   `test_observation.py`, `test_order_independence.py`,
 1183   `test_proposal_identity.py`, `test_rotation.py`
 1184 - `evidence/stage-01/RECORD.md` and `evidence/stage-01/instrument/`
 1185 - `pyproject.toml`, `.gitignore`, `.gitattributes`
 1186 
 1187 Configuration to revert: `pyproject.toml` holds the only configuration, the
 1188 pytest `pythonpath` and `testpaths` settings. There is no other configuration
 1189 file, no environment variable and no installed package.

 1191 ## Repair 1 — declared before implementation, 2026-09-20
 1192 
 1193 Base: `fa8be6083b31ead3c7e6aae844c0fd749927e6d0`. Authority: OD-002.
 1194 Scope: R1–R5 only. No reservations, worlds, new domains or stage progression.
 1195 Whole-world/capacity runs: zero. Original results and instrument outputs stay intact.
 1196 
 1197 - R1: reject reentry for the complete engine tick, including collection and
 1198   publication; release the guard on every exit. Verify both review triggers,
 1199   ordinary iterators, independent engines and recovery after exceptions.
 1200 - R2: deterministically order rejected outcomes from distinct unknown actors while
 1201   retaining every denial and preserving valid-actor allocation priority.
 1202 - R3: a mutant counts as detected only on an executed failing test, never runner
 1203   failure or an empty failure list. Retain UNKNOWN output and test negative controls.
 1204 - R4: retain unbounded Python integers and the existing JSON byte representation.
 1205   Encode integer digits locally without changing the interpreter's global limit;
 1206   test large positive/negative values, ordinary JSON equivalence and global inertness.
 1207 - R5: input proposals carry an actor-local **order**, expressing intention such as
 1208   giving before consuming. Settlement assigns dense engine-owned **sequence** values
 1209   from sorted distinct orders within each actor at the boundary. Collection order
 1210   is irrelevant. Duplicate input orders are all denied; they retain the existing
 1211   `denied_duplicate_actor_sequence` reason for compatibility. Rejected proposals
 1212   also receive deterministic sequence values. The caller cannot supply the output
 1213   sequence. This implements the roadmap rather than amending its requirement.
 1214 
 1215 Proposal helper positional calls retain their shape; the input keyword/attribute
 1216 `sequence` becomes `order`. There are no person/domain callers in Stage 1a. Outcomes
 1217 keep their existing `sequence` field. Proposal IDs remain caller-supplied in this
 1218 slice; sequence ownership is not a claim of authenticated actor provenance.
 1219 
 1220 Code version advances to `0.1.1-stage1a`; output schema remains `v3.kernel.1a.1`.
 1221 Ordinary state identities must remain identical. Original fixture record payloads

 1278 the first direct suite attempt had 137 passes and 12 fixture/teardown errors
 1279 because pytest's default temporary directory was inaccessible (terminal summary
 1280 only retained). An explicit writable temp directory gave 148 passes. The first
 1281 mutation-wrapper attempt then had 137 passes and 11 fixture errors because
 1282 `PYTEST_ADDOPTS` consumed Windows backslashes. No mutation ran. Its raw output
 1283 is in `repair-1/invalid-mutation-wrapper-attempt/`. One wrapper correction to a
 1284 quoted forward-slash path produced the valid mutation run above. Neither invalid
 1285 attempt supports a pass, failure of kernel behaviour, or mutation detection.
 1286 
 1287 The surviving conservation mutation remains an assertion in the kernel. Its
 1288 redundancy follows from balanced effects and account containment; survival alone
 1289 does not prove this. The negative-balance mutant still fails because constructors
 1290 reject the negative state with a different exception when that rail is removed.
 1291 That kill distinguishes the rail/error path; it does not demonstrate that removing
 1292 the rail alone permits negative state to commit.

 1315 ### Reproduction
 1316 
 1317 Use a disposable copy containing these repaired files and a separate clean copy
 1318 of the base revision. The mutation script deliberately edits/restores files and
 1319 writes scratch outputs, so run it in the disposable copy, preserving this evidence.
 1320 No dependencies beyond Python and pytest are required.
 1321 
 1322 The actual interpreter used was:
 1323 `C:/dev/02-Simulation-Sandbox/.worktrees/request-food-outstanding/sandbox/f01_validation_venv/Scripts/python.exe`.
 1324 
 1325 From the disposable repaired copy, with Python available as `python` and a new
 1326 explicit temporary directory (the names below are placeholders):
 1327 
 1328 ```text
 1329 python -B -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider --tb=short --basetemp="<fresh-suite-temp>"
 1330 python -B evidence/stage-01/repair-1/run_mutations.py "<repair-copy>" "<fresh-mutation-temp>"
 1331 ```
 1332 
 1333 The second command sets `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1`, `PYTHONHASHSEED=0` and
 1334 a correctly quoted `PYTEST_ADDOPTS` temp path; it invokes the actual instrument,
 1335 retains every raw output and verifies restoration hashes.
 1336 
 1337 Run `capture_fixtures.py "<base-copy>" "<output>/fixtures-base.json"` and
 1338 `capture_fixtures.py "<repair-copy>" "<output>/fixtures-repaired.json"` under
 1339 hash seed 0; repeat the repaired capture with seed 17 and the filename
 1340 `fixtures-repaired-seed17.json`. Then run `compare_fixtures.py "<output>"`.
 1341 Capture calls the existing native fixture functions and records public engine
 1342 state and tick records. The comparison strips only the declared engine-version
 1343 field; it does not reconstruct decisions or alter engine outputs.

 1489 ### Slice 1c (re-scoped) — checkpoint 1 — 2026-09-21
 1490 
 1491 Record stream and viewer landed under OD-009. `stream/run_file.py` writes an
 1492 append-only JSONL run (`v3.stream.1`): a header, one canonical content line per
 1493 tick (record, next-state digest, chained), a separate timing line per tick,
 1494 and an end line carrying the trail digest; `read_run` recomputes every digest
 1495 and chain link and lists problems. `stream/scenario.py` is a seeded
 1496 contention-and-holds proposal generator over the 1b kernel; `stream/run.py`
 1497 runs it (`--twice` re-runs and byte-compares); `stream/viewer.py` renders a
 1498 self-contained HTML page (inline CSS/JS, no external resources) and a text
 1499 render from the run file alone. `tests/test_stream.py` adds ten tests
 1500 (determinism, verification, overwrite refusal, tamper detection, viewer
 1501 self-containment, kernel→stream import direction). Whole tree: 264 passed.
 1502 
 1503 Checkpoint run: `py -3 -B -m stream.run --seed 7 --ticks 200 --twice --html`
 1504 → 200 ticks, trail
 1505 `094e03795b77c6574c82baca33d3a0fe8d8f9daae5e24585e894f047bc90689b`, final
 1506 state `00076b2d…`, second run byte-identical; tick_ms mean 0.233 / p95 0.333 /
 1507 max 0.424. Run files live under `runs/` (gitignored); checkpoint output is
 1508 exploration, not evidence.
 1509 
 1510 Observations from the viewer: reservations never expire (a 1b declaration),
 1511 so holds accumulate over a long run and availability drains even where stock
 1512 remains; the economy has no production, so it is a pure depletion picture.
 1513 Both are inputs to Stage 2 scoping, not defects.
 1514 
 1515 Tooling notes: `automation/preflight.py` recognises the milestone line by
 1516 fixed phrases, so the updated `SIM3_STATE.md` snapshot reports
 1517 `sim3_state_milestone_mismatch` (and `sim3_state_head_mismatch`, because the
 1518 head line carries an annotation rather than a bare hash) as informational
 1519 inconsistencies alongside the two pre-existing ones; per OD-009 this is
 1520 recorded rather than fixed. The copied review evidence files under
 1521 `review-2026-09-21-slice-1a/` and `-1b/` were CRLF-normalised to LF on
 1522 commit, so their `artifact-sha256.json` hashes describe the workspace copies,

 1526 ### Stage 2, first step — checkpoint 2, a map over time — 2026-09-21
 1527 
 1528 Position, movement, one renewable food source and hunger, under OD-009 point
 1529 4. New package `world/` composes the 1b kernel without changing it: the kernel
 1530 remains the only place food units move (claims, eating, contention); `world/`
 1531 holds what the kernel does not know (homes, positions, hunger, deaths) as an
 1532 immutable overlay beside the kernel state. One tick: observe (bounded
 1533 tick-start view: own position, hunger, free food, the source's place and free
 1534 stock) → decide (one live selection rule with the eligible candidates recorded
 1535 beside the choice) → kernel settles eat/claim proposals → world processes in a
 1536 fixed order: movement, hunger, death, renewal. Renewal is the only production
 1537 rule; it constructs the next kernel state through the kernel's validated
 1538 constructor and is written to the tick line, and the run reader recomputes it
 1539 from the stored state rather than trusting the digest. Stream format is now
 1540 `v3.stream.2` (optional `world`, `decisions`, `production` blocks; v3.stream.1
 1541 files still read). Genesis uses one named seeded generator
 1542 (`homes-uniform-v1`); nothing else in a run is random.
 1543 
 1544 Declared levers (all in the run header; CLI flags on `world.run`): grid
 1545 12×12, six people, source at centre, starting food 1, source stock 4, cap 8,
 1546 renewal +2 every 3 ticks, claim up to 2, hunger +1 per tick, satiation 6 per
 1547 unit, hungry at 5, emergency at 10, death at 16. Movement one cell per tick
 1548 along the longer axis (x on ties), co-location allowed. Decision priority:
 1549 eat if hungry and holding; claim if hungry at the source; wait if hungry at an
 1550 empty source; walk to the source if hungry; else walk home and rest.
 1551 
 1552 Checkpoint run: `py -3 -B -m world.run --seed 7 --ticks 300 --twice --html`
 1553 → trail `d9f78325601a9a1f7745843f0517d7a6452a6f8d0eb261c9f3f1253596115f02`,
 1554 second run byte-identical; survivors 3, deaths 3 (p04 t22, p06 t46, p03
 1555 t58); 79 claims accepted, 6 denied (`denied_insufficient_source`), 160 units
 1556 eaten, 144 emergency person-ticks; tick_ms mean 0.099 / p95 0.143 / max
 1557 0.212. Viewer: `world/viewer.py` (map, people table with decision and kernel
 1558 outcome, hunger and stock over time). `tests/test_world.py` adds 22 tests;
 1559 whole tree 286 passed.
 1560 
 1561 Observations, inputs to the next leg, not results:
 1562 - The world found a carrying capacity: three die in the first 60 ticks, then
 1563   the remaining three hold a stable cycle (stock 2–6, hunger sawtooth to ~10).
 1564   With these levers the source cannot feed six.
 1565 - Two of the three deaths happened at the source holding food: claim and eat
 1566   are separate ticks, so a claim at hunger 15 cannot beat death at 16. That
 1567   is the declared rule, and it is what "needs never pause" costs.
 1568 - The kernel's own contention rail is what decides who eats when several
 1569   arrive together; the world layer never overrides a kernel outcome.
 1570 - Nobody sees anyone else yet; perception is the next checkpoint (OD-009).
 1571 
 1572 Tooling note: `tests/test_preflight.py` now sets `PYTHONIOENCODING=utf-8`
 1573 for the preflight child process; the previous commit's em dash in a subject
 1574 line made the pipe's ANSI code page decode fail on Windows. No orchestrator,
 1575 no frozen hashes, no acceptance claimed. Stage 1 exit remains unclaimed.
 1576 
 1577 ### Stage 2, second step — checkpoint 3, perception radius and bounded views — 2026-09-21
 1578 
 1579 Perception radius and bounded views, under OD-009 point 4. Each living person
 1580 now observes the tick-start world inside a Chebyshev radius: other living
 1581 people as identity, position and free food (not hunger, home or decision);
 1582 the source position remains a known landmark; source stock is present only
 1583 when the source cell is in view, otherwise absent, never stale and never
 1584 zero-as-unknown. Self is always in view. Observations are recorded per
 1585 person on the tick line in an optional `observations` block, verified the
 1586 same way as `decisions` (canonical bytes, in the trail). STREAM_FORMAT stays
 1587 `v3.stream.2`: adding that optional block does not change the reader's rules.
 1588 `world/process.py` is unchanged. CLAIM still requires standing on the source
 1589 (hence in view) and uses the observed stock, asserted rather than defaulted;
 1590 no new candidate kinds.
 1591 
 1592 Declared values (in the run header via `WorldConfig.describe()`): distance
 1593 metric Chebyshev; default perception radius 3 (`--perception-radius`);
 1594 boundary inclusive (`distance <= radius`); others leak identity, position,
 1595 free food only; source stock only when the source cell is in view.
 1596 
 1597 Checkpoint runs, seed 7, 300 ticks (`py -3 -B -m world.run`):
 1598 - radius 3 (default, `--twice --html`): trail
 1599   `ac95126572ed0ce787042df116c4ec8715ad62d3939ff53af8995043bf89c43d`,
 1600   second run byte-identical; survivors 3, deaths 3 (p04 t22, p06 t46, p03
 1601   t58); tick_ms mean 0.127 / p95 0.201 / max 0.301; person-ticks with
 1602   another in view 861, with source in view 836.
 1603 - radius 12 (whole grid): trail
 1604   `1608c71acba9227310ba973f214a6c670e29ada8de986be9679460f14a3dc045`;
 1605   survivors 3, deaths 3; tick_ms mean 0.135 / p95 0.221 / max 0.334;
 1606   1026 / 1026 (every living person-tick).
 1607 - radius 1: trail
 1608   `76886afdb38868f05d417ca823342d15cdd111bd4d637e2d34a52d78c8e96e38`;
 1609   survivors 3, deaths 3; tick_ms mean 0.115 / p95 0.179 / max 0.263;
 1610   433 / 481.
 1611 
 1612 Radius-12 decisions matched HEAD `b305783` byte-for-byte on the 300-tick
 1613 seed-7 run (and on the 80-tick fixture in `tests/fixtures/`). Radius 1 and 3
 1614 produced the same decisions and the same deaths: the selection rule still
 1615 does not act on other people, and CLAIM only happens on the source cell,

 1620 Observations, inputs to whatever comes next, not results:
 1621 - Bounded views are now native records, but they do not change who walks,
 1622   who claims, or who dies. The world still looks like checkpoint 2 until
 1623   someone can act on a person they can see.
 1624 - At radius 3 a person approaching from a home on the rim often cannot
 1625   yet see source stock; they still walk to the landmark. At the source
 1626   they see the crowd and still only eat, claim or wait.

 1628   is unchanged; it remains the owner decision pending from checkpoint 2.
 1629

===== stream/run_file.py =====
    1 """Append-only run files: one JSON line per event, canonical bytes for content.
    2 
    3 Line kinds:
    4   header  run identity, engine/schema versions, scenario, genesis state
    5   tick    the tick record, the committed state, the engine's own availability
    6   timing  wall-clock cost of that tick (outside every digest and seal)
    7   end     tick count and the trail digest over all tick lines
    8 
    9 Content lines are canonical JSON (sorted keys, integers and strings only), so
   10 two runs of the same scenario produce byte-identical content lines. Timing
   11 lines are the only non-deterministic bytes and are kept separate on purpose.
   12 
   13 Format v3.stream.2 added optional blocks a world run needs, each verified by
   14 the reader: a `world` overlay (positions, needs) with its own digest on the
   15 header and on every tick; `decisions` (what each actor chose and why);
   16 `observations` (what each living person saw at tick start); and `production`,
   17 the renewal applied to sources after the tick, with the digest of the state
   18 that results. When production is present the next tick chains from that
   19 produced state, and the reader recomputes it from the stored state rather
   20 than trusting the digest.
   21 
   22 Format v3.stream.3 is the sealed evidence stream of slice 1c, declared in
   23 evidence/stage-01/RECORD.md before this code. The header adds `horizon` (the
   24 declared tick count), `code_identity` (sha256 of every simulation source file)
   25 and `config_identity` (digest of the scenario description). Every tick line
   26 adds `inputs`: the proposals submitted to the kernel that tick, in submission
   27 order. Seals chain the whole file:
   28 
   29     header seal = digest(header without "seal")
   30     tick seal   = digest({"prev": previous seal, "tick": digest(tick without "seal")})
   31 
   32 and the end line carries the final seal. The reader verifies the chain, names
   33 the first line that fails any check, and reports `last_sealed_tick`: the last
   34 tick that its own line and every line before it vouch for. Timing lines stay
   35 outside the chain. v3.stream.1 and v3.stream.2 files remain readable, without
   36 seal checks.
   37 """
   38 
   39 from __future__ import annotations
   40 
   41 import hashlib
   42 import json
   43 import os
   44 from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping
   45 from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   46 from pathlib import Path
   47 from typing import Any
   48 
   49 from kernel import (ENGINE_VERSION, SCHEMA_VERSION, CanonicalError, Proposal, TickRecord, WorldState,
   50                     canonical_bytes, canonicalise, digest)
   51 
   52 STREAM_FORMAT = "v3.stream.3"
   53 SEALED_FORMATS = frozenset({STREAM_FORMAT})
   54 READABLE_FORMATS = frozenset({"v3.stream.1", "v3.stream.2", STREAM_FORMAT})
   55 CODE_PACKAGES = ("kernel", "stream", "world")
   56 REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
   57 INPUT_FIELDS = ("proposal_id", "actor", "order", "operation")
   58 
   59 
   60 class RunFileError(Exception):
   61     """A run file is missing, malformed, or does not verify."""
   62 
   63 
   64 def _line(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> bytes:
   65     return canonical_bytes(payload) + b"\n"
   66 
   67 
   68 def _safe_digest(value: Any) -> str | None:
   69     """The digest of untrusted file content, or None when it has no canonical form."""
   70     try:
   71         return digest(value)
   72     except CanonicalError:
   73         return None
   74 
   75 
   76 # --- identities and seals --------------------------------------------------------
   77 
   78 def code_identity(root: Path = REPO_ROOT) -> dict[str, Any]:
   79     """What produced a run, independent of git: the sha256 of every Python file
   80     under kernel/, stream/ and world/ (bytes read with CRLF as LF, so a checkout's
   81     line endings do not change it) and the digest of that map."""
   82     files: dict[str, str] = {}
   83     for package in CODE_PACKAGES:
   84         for path in sorted((root / package).rglob("*.py")):
   85             if "__pycache__" in path.parts:
   86                 continue
   87             files[path.relative_to(root).as_posix()] = hashlib.sha256(
   88                 path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")).hexdigest()
   89     return {"files": files, "digest": digest(files)}
   90 
   91 
   92 def without_seal(payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   93     return {key: value for key, value in payload.items() if key != "seal"}
   94 
   95 
   96 def header_seal(header: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
   97     return digest(without_seal(header))
   98 
   99 
  100 def tick_seal(previous: str | None, payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
  101     return digest({"prev": previous, "tick": digest(without_seal(payload))})
  102 
  103 
  104 # --- inputs ------------------------------------------------------------------------
  105 
  106 def _typed(value: Any) -> list[Any]:
  107     """The declared encoding for a submitted value with no canonical form."""
  108     if value is None:
  109         return ["none", None]
  110     if isinstance(value, bool):
  111         return ["bool", 1 if value else 0]
  112     if isinstance(value, int):
  113         return ["int", value]
  114     if isinstance(value, float):
  115         return ["float", value.hex()]
  116     if isinstance(value, str):
  117         return ["str", value]
  118     if isinstance(value, Mapping):
  119         pairs = [[_typed(key), _typed(item)] for key, item in value.items()]
  120         return ["map", sorted(pairs, key=lambda pair: canonical_bytes(pair[0]))]
  121     if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
  122         return ["seq", [_typed(item) for item in value]]
  123     raise RunFileError(f"a submitted {type(value).__name__} has no declared input encoding")
  124 
  125 
  126 def _untyped(encoded: Any) -> Any:
  127     if not (isinstance(encoded, list) and len(encoded) == 2 and isinstance(encoded[0], str)):
  128         raise RunFileError(f"not a typed input value: {encoded!r}")
  129     tag, payload = encoded
  130     if tag == "none" and payload is None:
  131         return None
  132     if tag == "bool" and type(payload) is int and payload in (0, 1):
  133         return payload == 1
  134     if tag == "int" and type(payload) is int:
  135         return payload
  136     if tag == "str" and isinstance(payload, str):
  137         return payload
  138     if tag == "float" and isinstance(payload, str):
  139         try:
  140             return float.fromhex(payload)
  141         except ValueError as exc:
  142             raise RunFileError(f"not a typed float: {payload!r}") from exc
  143     if tag == "seq" and isinstance(payload, list):
  144         return tuple(_untyped(item) for item in payload)
  145     if tag == "map" and isinstance(payload, list) and all(isinstance(pair, list) and len(pair) == 2 for pair in payload):
  146         try:
  147             return {_untyped(key): _untyped(item) for key, item in payload}
  148         except TypeError as exc:
  149             raise RunFileError(f"a typed map key is not hashable: {exc}") from exc
  150     raise RunFileError(f"not a typed input value: {encoded!r}")
  151 
  152 
  153 def encode_input(proposal: Proposal) -> dict[str, Any]:
  154     """One submitted proposal as its tick line records it: `params` in canonical
  155     form when the params have one, else `params_typed` in the declared encoding."""
  156     if not isinstance(proposal, Proposal):
  157         raise RunFileError(f"an input must be a Proposal, got {type(proposal).__name__}")
  158     entry: dict[str, Any] = {name: getattr(proposal, name) for name in INPUT_FIELDS}
  159     try:
  160         entry["params"] = canonicalise(proposal.params)
  161     except CanonicalError:
  162         entry["params_typed"] = _typed(proposal.params)
  163     return entry
  164 
  165 
  166 def decode_input(entry: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Proposal:
  167     """The Proposal an input entry records; it equals the one that was submitted."""
  168     if not isinstance(entry, Mapping):
  169         raise RunFileError("an input entry must be a mapping")
  170     base = set(INPUT_FIELDS)
  171     if set(entry) == base | {"params"}:
  172         params = entry["params"]
  173     elif set(entry) == base | {"params_typed"}:
  174         params = _untyped(entry["params_typed"])
  175     else:
  176         raise RunFileError(f"an input entry needs {sorted(base)} and params or params_typed, got {sorted(entry)}")
  177     try:
  178         return Proposal(entry["proposal_id"], entry["actor"], entry["order"], entry["operation"], params)
  179     except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
  180         raise RunFileError(f"an input entry does not make a proposal: {exc}") from exc
  181 
  182 
  183 # --- production --------------------------------------------------------------------
  184 
  185 def apply_production(state: dict[str, Any], production: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
  186     """The production rule on a stored canonical state: each entry adds
  187     `amount` (a positive integer) to the stock of an existing source and
  188     nothing else changes. Pure, on plain JSON, so a reader can recompute the
  189     produced state without the kernel. Raises RunFileError on a bad entry."""
  190     produced = json.loads(json.dumps(state))
  191     sources = produced.get("sources")
  192     if not isinstance(sources, dict):
  193         raise RunFileError("production needs a state with sources")
  194     seen: set[str] = set()
  195     for entry in production:
  196         source = entry.get("source") if isinstance(entry, dict) else None
  197         amount = entry.get("amount") if isinstance(entry, dict) else None
  198         if not isinstance(source, str) or source not in sources:
  199             raise RunFileError(f"production names an unknown source {source!r}")
  200         if source in seen:
  201             raise RunFileError(f"production names {source!r} twice")
  202         if type(amount) is not int or amount <= 0:
  203             raise RunFileError(f"production of {source!r} must be a positive integer, got {amount!r}")
  204         seen.add(source)
  205         sources[source]["stock"] = int(sources[source]["stock"]) + amount
  206     return produced
  207 
  208 
  209 # --- writing -----------------------------------------------------------------------
  210 
  211 def tick_payload(record: TickRecord, next_state: WorldState, *, inputs: Iterable[Proposal],
  212                  world: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None, decisions: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
  213                  observations: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None, production: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
  214                  produced_state: WorldState | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
  215     """The content of one tick line, without its seal.
  216 
  217     The writer seals and writes it; replay rebuilds it with the live code and
  218     compares it with the file. `next_state` is what settlement committed and
  219     `inputs` are the proposals that record settled. When a world process then
  220     produced stock, pass `production` (the rule's entries) and `produced_state`
  221     (the kernel state that resulted): the pure rule on the committed state must
  222     give exactly that state, so a file never records production that did not
  223     happen."""
  224     if record.next_state_digest != next_state.digest():
  225         raise RunFileError("committed state does not match the record's next_state_digest")
  226     encoded = [encode_input(proposal) for proposal in inputs]
  227     if sorted(entry["proposal_id"] for entry in encoded) != sorted(outcome.proposal_id for outcome in record.outcomes):
  228         raise RunFileError("inputs are not the proposals this record settled")
  229     payload: dict[str, Any] = {
  230         "kind": "tick",
  231         "tick": record.tick,
  232         "record": record.canonical(),
  233         "record_digest": record.digest(),
  234         "state": next_state.canonical(),
  235         "state_digest": next_state.digest(),
  236         "availability": next_state.availability(),
  237         "inputs": encoded,
  238     }
  239     if world is not None:
  240         payload["world"] = world
  241         payload["world_digest"] = digest(world)
  242     if decisions is not None:
  243         payload["decisions"] = decisions
  244     if observations is not None:
  245         payload["observations"] = observations
  246     if production:
  247         if produced_state is None:
  248             raise RunFileError("production needs the produced state")
  249         expected = digest(apply_production(next_state.canonical(), production))
  250         if expected != produced_state.digest():
  251             raise RunFileError("produced state is not the production rule applied to the committed state")
  252         payload["production"] = production
  253         payload["produced_state_digest"] = produced_state.digest()
  254     elif produced_state is not None and produced_state.digest() != next_state.digest():
  255         raise RunFileError("state changed after settlement without production")
  256     return payload
  257 
  258 
  259 class RunWriter:
  260     """Write one sealed run. Refuses to overwrite; every line is flushed as written.
  261 
  262     The end line is written only when `horizon` ticks were recorded and the
  263     writer is closed normally. A run stopped early (an exception inside the
  264     `with` block, or `abort()`) is left cut, with no end line, so recovery can
  265     resume it from its last sealed tick."""
  266 
  267     def __init__(self, path: Path, *, run_id: str, genesis: WorldState, scenario: dict[str, Any],
  268                  horizon: int, world: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> None:
  269         if type(horizon) is not int or horizon < 0:
  270             raise RunFileError(f"a run horizon must be an integer of zero or more, got {horizon!r}")
  271         header: dict[str, Any] = {
  272             "kind": "header",
  273             "format": STREAM_FORMAT,
  274             "run_id": run_id,
  275             "engine_version": ENGINE_VERSION,
  276             "schema_version": SCHEMA_VERSION,
  277             "scenario": scenario,
  278             "config_identity": digest(scenario),
  279             "code_identity": code_identity(),
  280             "horizon": horizon,
  281             "genesis": genesis.canonical(),
  282             "genesis_digest": genesis.digest(),
  283         }
  284         if world is not None:
  285             header["world"] = world
  286             header["world_digest"] = digest(world)
  287         header["seal"] = header_seal(header)
  288         self.header = header
  289         self.path = Path(path)
  290         self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  291         self._handle = self.path.open("xb")
  292         self._closed = False
  293         self._seal = header["seal"]
  294         self._ticks = 0
  295         self._next_tick = genesis.tick
  296         self._trail = hashlib.sha256()
  297         self._write(header)
  298 
  299     @classmethod
  300     def resume(cls, path: Path, *, header: dict[str, Any], prefix: bytes, tick_lines: Iterable[bytes],
  301                seal: str, next_tick: int) -> "RunWriter":
  302         """Continue a sealed run in a new file from its sealed prefix (slice 1c
  303         recovery). The prefix bytes are written unchanged, and the seal chain,
  304         trail digest and tick count carry on from them, so a faithful
  305         continuation is byte-identical to an uninterrupted run, timing aside.
  306         The caller vouches for the prefix; stream/recover.py builds it from
  307         what the reader verified."""
  308         if header.get("format") not in SEALED_FORMATS:
  309             raise RunFileError("only a sealed run can be resumed")
  310         writer = cls.__new__(cls)
  311         writer.header = header
  312         writer._trail = hashlib.sha256()
  313         writer._ticks = 0
  314         for raw in tick_lines:
  315             writer._trail.update(raw)
  316             writer._ticks += 1
  317         if writer._ticks > header["horizon"]:
  318             raise RunFileError("the sealed prefix is longer than the run's horizon")
  319         writer._seal = seal
  320         writer._next_tick = next_tick
  321         writer.path = Path(path)
  322         writer.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  323         writer._handle = writer.path.open("xb")
  324         writer._closed = False
  325         writer._handle.write(prefix)
  326         writer._handle.flush()
  327         return writer
  328 
  329     def _write(self, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> bytes:
  330         raw = _line(payload)
  331         self._handle.write(raw)
  332         self._handle.flush()
  333         return raw
  334 
  335     @property
  336     def seal(self) -> str:
  337         """The seal the next tick line will chain from."""
  338         return self._seal
  339 
  340     def record(self, record: TickRecord, next_state: WorldState, *, inputs: Iterable[Proposal],
  341                elapsed_ns: int | None = None, world: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
  342                decisions: dict[str, Any] | None = None, observations: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
  343                production: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None, produced_state: WorldState | None = None) -> None:
  344         """Append one sealed tick. See `tick_payload` for what is checked."""
  345         if self._closed:
  346             raise RunFileError("run file already closed")
  347         if self._ticks >= self.header["horizon"]:
  348             raise RunFileError(f"the run's horizon of {self.header['horizon']} ticks is already written")
  349         if record.tick != self._next_tick:
  350             raise RunFileError(f"expected a record for tick {self._next_tick}, got tick {record.tick}")
  351         payload = tick_payload(record, next_state, inputs=inputs, world=world, decisions=decisions,
  352                                observations=observations, production=production, produced_state=produced_state)
  353         payload["seal"] = tick_seal(self._seal, payload)
  354         raw = self._write(payload)
  355         self._seal = payload["seal"]
  356         self._trail.update(raw)
  357         self._ticks += 1
  358         self._next_tick += 1
  359         if elapsed_ns is not None:
  360             self._write({"kind": "timing", "tick": record.tick, "elapsed_ns": int(elapsed_ns)})
  361 
  362     def close(self) -> str:
  363         """Write the end line and return the trail digest. Refuses, leaving the
  364         file cut, when fewer than `horizon` ticks were recorded."""
  365         if self._closed:
  366             return self._trail.hexdigest()
  367         if self._ticks != self.header["horizon"]:
  368             self.abort()
  369             raise RunFileError(f"closed after {self._ticks} of {self.header['horizon']} ticks; "
  370                                "the file is left without an end line")
  371         trail = self._trail.hexdigest()
  372         self._write({"kind": "end", "ticks": self._ticks, "trail_digest": trail, "final_seal": self._seal})
  373         self._handle.flush()
  374         os.fsync(self._handle.fileno())
  375         self._handle.close()
  376         self._closed = True
  377         return trail
  378 
  379     def abort(self) -> None:
  380         """Stop without an end line: the file stays a cut run."""
  381         if not self._closed:
  382             self._handle.flush()
  383             self._handle.close()
  384             self._closed = True
  385 
  386     def __enter__(self) -> "RunWriter":
  387         return self
  388 
  389     def __exit__(self, exc_type: object, *exc: object) -> None:
  390         if exc_type is None:
  391             self.close()
  392         else:
  393             self.abort()
  394 
  395 
  396 # --- reading -----------------------------------------------------------------------
  397 
  398 @dataclass(frozen=True)
  399 class Run:
  400     path: Path
  401     header: dict[str, Any]
  402     ticks: tuple[dict[str, Any], ...]
  403     timings: dict[int, int]
  404     end: dict[str, Any] | None
  405     trail_digest: str
  406     problems: tuple[str, ...] = field(default_factory=tuple)
  407     # v3.stream.3: the last tick the seal chain and every check vouch for
  408     # (-1 when none does); None for unsealed formats.
  409     last_sealed_tick: int | None = None
  410     # the first line that failed any check, or None
  411     first_break_line: int | None = None
  412 
  413     @property
  414     def run_id(self) -> str:
  415         return str(self.header.get("run_id", ""))
  416 
  417     @property
  418     def complete(self) -> bool:
  419         return self.end is not None and not self.problems
  420 
  421     @property
  422     def has_world(self) -> bool:
  423         return "world" in self.header
  424 
  425     @property
  426     def sealed(self) -> bool:
  427         return self.header.get("format") in SEALED_FORMATS
  428 
  429 
  430 def chained_from(tick: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
  431     """The state digest the next tick must start from: the produced state
  432     when a world process produced stock after this tick, else the committed one."""
  433     if "production" in tick:
  434         return tick.get("produced_state_digest")
  435     return tick.get("state_digest")
  436 
  437 
  438 def _sealed_header_problems(header: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
  439     problems: list[str] = []
  440     horizon = header.get("horizon")
  441     if type(horizon) is not int or horizon < 0:
  442         problems.append("header horizon is not an integer of zero or more")
  443     identity = header.get("code_identity")
  444     if (not isinstance(identity, dict) or not isinstance(identity.get("files"), dict)
  445             or identity.get("digest") != _safe_digest(identity["files"])):
  446         problems.append("header code identity does not match its file map")
  447     if header.get("config_identity") != _safe_digest(header.get("scenario")):
  448         problems.append("header config identity is not the digest of its scenario")
  449     if header.get("seal") != _safe_digest(without_seal(header)):
  450         problems.append("header seal does not verify")
  451     return problems
  452 
  453 
  454 def _input_problems(payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
  455     inputs = payload.get("inputs")
  456     if not isinstance(inputs, list):
  457         return ["tick has no inputs list"]
  458     try:
  459         ids = sorted(decode_input(entry).proposal_id for entry in inputs)
  460     except RunFileError as exc:
  461         return [f"inputs do not decode ({exc})"]
  462     record = payload.get("record")
  463     outcomes = record.get("outcomes") if isinstance(record, dict) else None
  464     if not isinstance(outcomes, list) or not all(isinstance(o, dict) and isinstance(o.get("proposal_id"), str)
  465                                                  for o in outcomes):
  466         return ["tick record has no readable outcomes to match its inputs"]
  467     if ids != sorted(o["proposal_id"] for o in outcomes):
  468         return ["inputs are not the proposals the record settled"]
  469     return []
  470 
  471 
  472 def read_run(path: Path) -> Run:
  473     """Read and verify a run file. Every stored digest is recomputed from the
  474     stored content and, for sealed formats, every seal; a run with problems is
  475     still returned so a viewer can show what exists, but `complete` is false."""
  476     path = Path(path)
  477     if not path.is_file():
  478         raise RunFileError(f"no run file at {path}")
  479     header: dict[str, Any] | None = None
  480     ticks: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  481     timings: dict[int, int] = {}
  482     end: dict[str, Any] | None = None
  483     problems: list[str] = []
  484     trail = hashlib.sha256()
  485     expected_tick = None
  486     sealed = False
  487     seal: str | None = None       # the stored seal the next tick line chains from
  488     last_sealed: int | None = None
  489     first_break: int | None = None
  490     intact = True                 # no line so far has failed any check
  491     with path.open("rb") as handle:
  492         for number, raw in enumerate(handle, start=1):
  493             before = len(problems)
  494             try:
  495                 if not raw.endswith(b"\n"):
  496                     problems.append(f"line {number}: incomplete (no newline)")
  497                     break
  498                 try:
  499                     payload = json.loads(raw)
  500                 except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
  501                     problems.append(f"line {number}: not JSON ({exc.msg})")
  502                     continue
  503                 if not isinstance(payload, dict) or "kind" not in payload:
  504                     problems.append(f"line {number}: not an event")
  505                     continue
  506                 if end is not None:
  507                     problems.append(f"line {number}: event after end")
  508                 kind = payload["kind"]
  509                 if kind == "header":
  510                     first_header = header is None
  511                     if not first_header:
  512                         problems.append(f"line {number}: second header")
  513                     header = payload
  514                     if payload.get("format") not in READABLE_FORMATS:
  515                         problems.append(f"line {number}: unsupported format {payload.get('format')!r}")
  516                     elif digest(payload.get("genesis")) != payload.get("genesis_digest"):
  517                         problems.append(f"line {number}: genesis digest does not match content")
  518                     else:
  519                         expected_tick = int(payload["genesis"]["tick"])
  520                     if "world" in payload and digest(payload.get("world")) != payload.get("world_digest"):
  521                         problems.append(f"line {number}: world genesis digest does not match content")
  522                     if first_header and payload.get("format") in SEALED_FORMATS:
  523                         sealed, last_sealed, seal = True, -1, payload.get("seal")
  524                         problems.extend(f"line {number}: {p}" for p in _sealed_header_problems(payload))
  525                 elif kind == "tick":
  526                     if header is None:
  527                         problems.append(f"line {number}: tick before header")
  528                     if canonical_bytes(payload) + b"\n" != raw:
  529                         problems.append(f"line {number}: tick line is not canonical bytes")
  530                     if digest(payload.get("record")) != payload.get("record_digest"):
  531                         problems.append(f"line {number}: record digest does not match content")
  532                     if digest(payload.get("state")) != payload.get("state_digest"):
  533                         problems.append(f"line {number}: state digest does not match content")
  534                     record = payload.get("record") or {}
  535                     if record.get("next_state_digest") != payload.get("state_digest"):
  536                         problems.append(f"line {number}: record and state disagree")
  537                     if expected_tick is not None and payload.get("tick") != expected_tick:
  538                         problems.append(f"line {number}: expected tick {expected_tick}, found {payload.get('tick')}")
  539                     if ticks and chained_from(ticks[-1]) != record.get("prior_state_digest"):
  540                         problems.append(f"line {number}: prior state digest does not chain")
  541                     if not ticks and header is not None and header.get("genesis_digest") != record.get("prior_state_digest"):
  542                         problems.append(f"line {number}: first tick does not start from genesis")
  543                     if "world" in payload and digest(payload.get("world")) != payload.get("world_digest"):
  544                         problems.append(f"line {number}: world digest does not match content")
  545                     if "production" in payload:
  546                         try:
  547                             produced = digest(apply_production(payload.get("state") or {}, payload["production"]))
  548                         except RunFileError as exc:
  549                             problems.append(f"line {number}: {exc}")
  550                         else:
  551                             if produced != payload.get("produced_state_digest"):
  552                                 problems.append(f"line {number}: produced state digest is not the production rule on the committed state")
  553                     elif "produced_state_digest" in payload:
  554                         problems.append(f"line {number}: produced state without production")
  555                     if sealed:
  556                         problems.extend(f"line {number}: {p}" for p in _input_problems(payload))
  557                         try:
  558                             expected_seal = tick_seal(seal, payload)
  559                         except CanonicalError:
  560                             expected_seal = None
  561                         if expected_seal is None or payload.get("seal") != expected_seal:
  562                             problems.append(f"line {number}: seal does not verify")
  563                         seal = payload.get("seal")
  564                     trail.update(raw)
  565                     ticks.append(payload)
  566                     expected_tick = int(payload.get("tick", -1)) + 1
  567                     if sealed and intact and len(problems) == before:
  568                         last_sealed = payload.get("tick")
  569                 elif kind == "timing":
  570                     timings[int(payload.get("tick", -1))] = int(payload.get("elapsed_ns", 0))
  571                 elif kind == "end":
  572                     end = payload
  573                     if payload.get("ticks") != len(ticks):
  574                         problems.append(f"line {number}: end declares {payload.get('ticks')} ticks, file has {len(ticks)}")
  575                     if payload.get("trail_digest") != trail.hexdigest():
  576                         problems.append(f"line {number}: trail digest does not match the tick lines")
  577                     if sealed:
  578                         if payload.get("final_seal") != seal:
  579                             problems.append(f"line {number}: final seal is not the last tick's seal")
  580                         if header is not None and payload.get("ticks") != header.get("horizon"):
  581                             problems.append(f"line {number}: end declares {payload.get('ticks')} ticks, "
  582                                             f"horizon is {header.get('horizon')}")
  583                 else:
  584                     problems.append(f"line {number}: unknown event kind {kind!r}")
  585             finally:
  586                 if len(problems) > before:
  587                     intact = False
  588                     if first_break is None:
  589                         first_break = number
  590     if header is None:
  591         problems.append("no header")
  592         header = {}
  593     if end is None:
  594         problems.append("no end record (run did not finish, or the file was cut)")
  595     return Run(path=path, header=header, ticks=tuple(ticks), timings=timings, end=end,
  596                trail_digest=trail.hexdigest(), problems=tuple(problems),
  597                last_sealed_tick=last_sealed if sealed else None, first_break_line=first_break)

===== stream/replay.py =====
    1 """Replay a sealed run against its own file (slice 1c, declaration item 6).
    2 
    3 Replay is the independent check ROADMAP asks for: it re-executes from the
    4 header's genesis with the declared inputs and compares every tick line the
    5 file holds, byte for byte without its seal, with what the live code produces.
    6 It is not `--twice`, which compares a second run with the first; it checks
    7 the file against the engine.
    8 
    9 Kernel-only (scenario) runs are replayed here by resubmitting each tick's
   10 recorded inputs. World runs recompute their decisions from their own state and
   11 are replayed by world/replay.py with the comparison below, because the stream
   12 never imports the world.
   13 """
   14 
   15 from __future__ import annotations
   16 
   17 from collections.abc import Iterator
   18 from dataclasses import dataclass
   19 from pathlib import Path
   20 from typing import Any
   21 
   22 from kernel import Engine, WorldState, canonical_bytes
   23 
   24 from stream.run_file import Run, code_identity, decode_input, read_run, tick_payload, without_seal
   25 from stream.scenario import Scenario
   26 
   27 
   28 class ReplayError(Exception):
   29     """A run file cannot be replayed: incomplete, unsealed, or the wrong kind of run."""
   30 
   31 
   32 @dataclass(frozen=True)
   33 class ReplayResult:
   34     path: Path
   35     ticks: int                      # tick lines compared
   36     identical: bool
   37     divergent_tick: int | None      # the first tick whose replayed line differs
   38     divergent_field: str | None     # its first differing top-level field, or "genesis"
   39     code_identity_matches: bool     # the live code identity equals the header's
   40 
   41 
   42 def sealed_run(path: Path) -> Run:
   43     """Read `path` and refuse anything but a complete run sealed through its last tick."""
   44     run = read_run(Path(path))
   45     if not run.sealed:
   46         raise ReplayError(f"{path} is format {run.header.get('format')!r}, not a sealed run file")
   47     if not run.complete:
   48         raise ReplayError(f"{path} does not verify: {run.problems[0] if run.problems else 'incomplete'}")
   49     if run.ticks and run.last_sealed_tick != run.ticks[-1].get("tick"):
   50         raise ReplayError(f"{path} is not sealed through its last tick")
   51     return run
   52 
   53 
   54 def code_matches(run: Run) -> bool:
   55     return (run.header.get("code_identity") or {}).get("digest") == code_identity()["digest"]
   56 
   57 
   58 # Tick-line fields in the order a tick produces them, so the first difference
   59 # reported is the earliest in the causal chain rather than the first alphabetically.
   60 FIELD_ORDER = ("kind", "tick", "observations", "decisions", "inputs", "record", "record_digest", "state",
   61                "state_digest", "availability", "production", "produced_state_digest", "world", "world_digest")
   62 
   63 
   64 def first_difference(stored: dict[str, Any], replayed: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
   65     """The first top-level field, in tick order, where a stored tick line and a
   66     replayed payload differ once seals are set aside; None when byte-identical."""
   67     left, right = without_seal(stored), without_seal(replayed)
   68     if canonical_bytes(left) == canonical_bytes(right):
   69         return None
   70     keys = set(left) | set(right)
   71     for key in [name for name in FIELD_ORDER if name in keys] + sorted(keys - set(FIELD_ORDER)):
   72         if key not in left or key not in right or canonical_bytes(left[key]) != canonical_bytes(right[key]):
   73             return key
   74     return "(line)"
   75 
   76 
   77 def compare(run: Run, replayed: Iterator[dict[str, Any]]) -> ReplayResult:
   78     """Compare every tick line of `run`, in order, with the replayed payloads."""
   79     matches = code_matches(run)
   80     compared = 0
   81     for stored in run.ticks:
   82         payload = next(replayed)
   83         compared += 1
   84         field = first_difference(stored, payload)
   85         if field is not None:
   86             return ReplayResult(run.path, compared, False, stored.get("tick"), field, matches)
   87     return ReplayResult(run.path, compared, True, None, None, matches)
   88 
   89 
   90 def genesis_differs(run: Run) -> ReplayResult:
   91     return ReplayResult(run.path, 0, False, None, "genesis", code_matches(run))
   92 
   93 
   94 def replay_scenario(path: Path) -> ReplayResult:
   95     """Replay a kernel-only scenario run from its header genesis, resubmitting
   96     each tick's recorded inputs, and compare every tick line with the file."""
   97     run = sealed_run(path)
   98     if run.has_world:
   99         raise ReplayError(f"{path} is a world run; replay it with world.run --replay")
  100     try:
  101         scenario = Scenario.from_describe(run.header.get("scenario"))
  102     except ValueError as exc:
  103         raise ReplayError(f"{path} does not describe a known scenario: {exc}") from exc
  104     genesis = WorldState.from_canonical(run.header["genesis"])
  105     if scenario.genesis().digest() != genesis.digest():
  106         return genesis_differs(run)
  107 
  108     def payloads() -> Iterator[dict[str, Any]]:
  109         engine = Engine(genesis)
  110         for stored in run.ticks:
  111             proposals = [decode_input(entry) for entry in stored["inputs"]]
  112             record = engine.tick(proposals)
  113             yield tick_payload(record, engine.state, inputs=proposals)
  114 
  115     return compare(run, payloads())
  116 
  117 
  118 def replay_lines(result: ReplayResult) -> list[str]:
  119     lines = [
  120         f"file: {result.path}",
  121         f"ticks compared: {result.ticks}",
  122         f"code identity: {'same as the header' if result.code_identity_matches else 'DIFFERENT from the header'}",
  123     ]
  124     if result.identical:
  125         lines.append("replay: identical")
  126     elif result.divergent_field == "genesis":
  127         lines.append("replay: DIFFERENT, the header genesis is not what its configuration produces")
  128     else:
  129         lines.append(f"replay: DIFFERENT at tick {result.divergent_tick}, field {result.divergent_field}")
  130     return lines

===== stream/recover.py =====
    1 """Recover a cut or broken sealed run through its last verified sealed tick
    2 (slice 1c, declaration item 7), and restore private engines from sealed ticks
    3 (item 8).
    4 
    5 Recovery trusts only what the seal chain vouches for. It keeps the source's
    6 bytes through the last sealed tick line, rebuilds the state that tick left from
    7 its stored canonical content, and continues to the declared horizon in a new
    8 file; nothing past that line is read or used. The result's content lines
    9 (header, ticks, end, seals included) equal an uninterrupted run's, and only
   10 timing lines may differ.
   11 
   12 A kernel-only scenario's input generator is not in the file, so it is rebuilt
   13 from the header and run through the sealed ticks, and it must reproduce every
   14 sealed tick's recorded inputs before it generates the rest. World runs are
   15 recovered by world/recover.py with these functions, because the stream never
   16 imports the world.
   17 """
   18 
   19 from __future__ import annotations
   20 
   21 import hashlib
   22 import json
   23 import time
   24 from dataclasses import dataclass
   25 from pathlib import Path
   26 from typing import Any
   27 
   28 from kernel import Engine, WorldState, canonical_bytes
   29 
   30 from stream.run_file import RunWriter, apply_production, code_identity, encode_input, read_run
   31 from stream.scenario import ProposalGenerator, Scenario
   32 
   33 
   34 class RecoveryError(Exception):
   35     """A run file cannot be recovered: unsealed, complete, untrustworthy, or written by other code."""
   36 
   37 
   38 @dataclass(frozen=True)
   39 class SealedPrefix:
   40     source: Path
   41     source_sha256: str
   42     header: dict[str, Any]
   43     ticks: tuple[dict[str, Any], ...]      # the sealed tick lines, parsed
   44     tick_lines: tuple[bytes, ...]          # the same lines, as written
   45     prefix: bytes                          # the source's bytes through the last sealed tick line
   46 
   47     @property
   48     def last_sealed_tick(self) -> int:
   49         return self.ticks[-1]["tick"] if self.ticks else -1
   50 
   51     @property
   52     def seal(self) -> str:
   53         return self.ticks[-1]["seal"] if self.ticks else self.header["seal"]
   54 
   55     @property
   56     def next_tick(self) -> int:
   57         return self.header["genesis"]["tick"] + len(self.ticks)
   58 
   59     @property
   60     def remaining(self) -> int:
   61         return self.header["horizon"] - len(self.ticks)
   62 
   63 
   64 def sealed_prefix(path: Path) -> SealedPrefix:
   65     """The part of a cut or broken sealed run that its seals vouch for."""
   66     path = Path(path)
   67     run = read_run(path)
   68     if not run.sealed:
   69         raise RecoveryError(f"{path} is format {run.header.get('format')!r}, not a sealed run file")
   70     if run.first_break_line == 1 or run.last_sealed_tick is None:
   71         raise RecoveryError(f"{path}: the header does not verify, so nothing in the file can be trusted")
   72     if run.complete:
   73         raise RecoveryError(f"{path} is complete; there is nothing to recover")
   74     if (run.header.get("code_identity") or {}).get("digest") != code_identity()["digest"]:
   75         raise RecoveryError(f"{path} was written by other code (its code identity differs from the live "
   76                             "code), so continuing it here would not continue the same run")
   77     raw = path.read_bytes()
   78     lines = raw.splitlines(keepends=True)
   79     wanted = run.last_sealed_tick - run.header["genesis"]["tick"] + 1
   80     kept = [lines[0]]
   81     ticks: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   82     tick_lines: list[bytes] = []
   83     for line in lines[1:]:
   84         if len(ticks) == wanted:
   85             break
   86         kept.append(line)
   87         payload = json.loads(line)
   88         if payload.get("kind") == "tick":
   89             ticks.append(payload)
   90             tick_lines.append(line)
   91     if len(ticks) != wanted or (ticks and ticks[-1]["tick"] != run.last_sealed_tick):
   92         raise RecoveryError(f"{path}: the last sealed tick line could not be located")
   93     return SealedPrefix(source=path, source_sha256=hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), header=run.header,
   94                         ticks=tuple(ticks), tick_lines=tuple(tick_lines), prefix=b"".join(kept))
   95 
   96 
   97 def restored_state(header: dict[str, Any], tick: dict[str, Any] | None) -> WorldState:
   98     """The state a sealed tick left for the next one: the produced state when
   99     the tick recorded production, else the committed state, or the genesis when
  100     no tick is given. Rebuilt from stored canonical content and checked against
  101     its stored digest."""
  102     if tick is None:
  103         canonical, expected = header["genesis"], header["genesis_digest"]
  104     elif "production" in tick:
  105         canonical, expected = apply_production(tick["state"], tick["production"]), tick["produced_state_digest"]
  106     else:
  107         canonical, expected = tick["state"], tick["state_digest"]
  108     state = WorldState.from_canonical(canonical)
  109     if state.digest() != expected:
  110         raise RecoveryError("a restored state does not match its stored digest")
  111     return state
  112 
  113 
  114 def restore_engine(path: Path, tick: int) -> Engine:
  115     """A private engine on the state sealed tick `tick` left (declaration item 8).
  116     Every call builds its own state from the file, so two engines restored from
  117     one tick share nothing mutable."""
  118     run = read_run(Path(path))
  119     genesis_tick = (run.header.get("genesis") or {}).get("tick", 0)
