  120     if not run.sealed or run.last_sealed_tick is None or not genesis_tick <= tick <= run.last_sealed_tick:
  121         raise RecoveryError(f"tick {tick} is not sealed in {path}")
  122     return Engine(restored_state(run.header, run.ticks[tick - genesis_tick]))
  123 
  124 
  125 def resume_writer(out: Path, prefix: SealedPrefix) -> RunWriter:
  126     return RunWriter.resume(out, header=prefix.header, prefix=prefix.prefix, tick_lines=prefix.tick_lines,
  127                             seal=prefix.seal, next_tick=prefix.next_tick)
  128 
  129 
  130 @dataclass(frozen=True)
  131 class RecoveryResult:
  132     source: Path
  133     source_sha256: str
  134     last_sealed_tick: int
  135     live_holds_at_cut: int              # reservations in the state recovery resumed from
  136     resumed: tuple[int, int] | None     # first and last tick recovery wrote, None when none were left
  137     out: Path
  138     trail_digest: str
  139     final_seal: str
  140 
  141 
  142 def recovery_result(prefix: SealedPrefix, out: Path, trail: str, final_seal: str, holds: int) -> RecoveryResult:
  143     first = prefix.next_tick
  144     last = prefix.header["genesis"]["tick"] + prefix.header["horizon"] - 1
  145     return RecoveryResult(source=prefix.source, source_sha256=prefix.source_sha256,
  146                           last_sealed_tick=prefix.last_sealed_tick, live_holds_at_cut=holds,
  147                           resumed=(first, last) if first <= last else None, out=Path(out),
  148                           trail_digest=trail, final_seal=final_seal)
  149 
  150 
  151 def recover_scenario(source: Path, out: Path) -> RecoveryResult:
  152     """Recover a kernel-only scenario run into the new file `out`."""
  153     prefix = sealed_prefix(source)
  154     if "world" in prefix.header:
  155         raise RecoveryError(f"{source} is a world run; recover it with world.run --recover")
  156     try:
  157         scenario = Scenario.from_describe(prefix.header.get("scenario"))
  158     except ValueError as exc:
  159         raise RecoveryError(f"{source} does not describe a known scenario: {exc}") from exc
  160     generator = ProposalGenerator(scenario)
  161     previous = prefix.header["genesis"]
  162     for payload in prefix.ticks:
  163         live = {action: held["actor"] for action, held in previous["reservations"].items()}
  164         proposals = generator.tick(payload["tick"], live)
  165         if canonical_bytes([encode_input(p) for p in proposals]) != canonical_bytes(payload["inputs"]):
  166             raise RecoveryError(f"the scenario generator does not reproduce the sealed inputs of tick {payload['tick']}")
  167         previous = apply_production(payload["state"], payload["production"]) if "production" in payload else payload["state"]
  168     engine = Engine(restored_state(prefix.header, prefix.ticks[-1] if prefix.ticks else None))
  169     holds = len(engine.state.reservations)
  170     with resume_writer(out, prefix) as writer:
  171         for _ in range(prefix.remaining):
  172             live = {action: held.actor for action, held in engine.state.reservations.items()}
  173             proposals = generator.tick(engine.state.tick, live)
  174             started = time.perf_counter_ns()
  175             record = engine.tick(proposals)
  176             writer.record(record, engine.state, inputs=proposals, elapsed_ns=time.perf_counter_ns() - started)
  177         trail = writer.close()
  178     return recovery_result(prefix, out, trail, writer.seal, holds)
  179 
  180 
  181 def recovery_lines(result: RecoveryResult) -> list[str]:
  182     resumed = f"ticks {result.resumed[0]} to {result.resumed[1]}" if result.resumed else "no ticks (end line only)"
  183     return [
  184         f"source: {result.source}",
  185         f"source sha256: {result.source_sha256}",
  186         f"last sealed tick: {result.last_sealed_tick} (live holds at the cut: {result.live_holds_at_cut})",
  187         f"resumed: {resumed}",
  188         f"written: {result.out}",
  189         f"trail_digest: {result.trail_digest}",
  190         f"final_seal: {result.final_seal}",
  191     ]

===== stream/run.py =====
    1 """Run a seeded scenario, write its record stream, and check it reproduces.
    2 
    3     py -3 -B -m stream.run --seed 7 --ticks 200
    4     py -3 -B -m stream.run --seed 7 --ticks 200 --twice     # determinism check
    5     py -3 -B -m stream.run --seed 7 --ticks 200 --html      # also render the viewer
    6     py -3 -B -m stream.run --replay runs/<file>.jsonl        # slice 1c: replay against the file
    7     py -3 -B -m stream.run --recover runs/<cut>.jsonl --out runs/<new>.jsonl   # slice 1c: recovery
    8 
    9 Output goes to runs/<run_id>.jsonl (and .html). runs/ is ignored by git: a
   10 checkpoint run is exploration output, not evidence. The per-tick timing lines
   11 are the visible cost signal until slice 1d measures cost. Every run file is
   12 sealed (v3.stream.3) and records the proposals submitted each tick.
   13 """
   14 
   15 from __future__ import annotations
   16 
   17 import argparse
   18 import sys
   19 import time
   20 from pathlib import Path
   21 
   22 from kernel import Engine
   23 
   24 from stream.run_file import RunFileError, RunWriter, read_run
   25 from stream.scenario import ProposalGenerator, Scenario
   26 
   27 DEFAULT_RUNS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "runs"
   28 
   29 
   30 def run_id_for(scenario: Scenario, ticks: int) -> str:
   31     return f"{scenario.describe()['name']}-seed{scenario.seed}-ticks{ticks}"
   32 
   33 
   34 def run_scenario(scenario: Scenario, ticks: int, path: Path) -> dict:
   35     """Run `ticks` ticks from the scenario genesis, streaming every record."""
   36     engine = Engine(scenario.genesis())
   37     generator = ProposalGenerator(scenario)
   38     elapsed: list[int] = []
   39     with RunWriter(path, run_id=run_id_for(scenario, ticks), genesis=engine.state, scenario=scenario.describe(),
   40                    horizon=ticks) as writer:
   41         for _ in range(ticks):
   42             live = {action: reservation.actor for action, reservation in engine.state.reservations.items()}
   43             proposals = generator.tick(engine.state.tick, live)
   44             started = time.perf_counter_ns()
   45             record = engine.tick(proposals)
   46             cost = time.perf_counter_ns() - started
   47             elapsed.append(cost)
   48             writer.record(record, engine.state, inputs=proposals, elapsed_ns=cost)
   49         trail = writer.close()
   50     ordered = sorted(elapsed) or [0]
   51     return {
   52         "path": path, "trail_digest": trail, "ticks": ticks,
   53         "final_state_digest": engine.state.digest(),
   54         "tick_ms_mean": sum(ordered) / len(ordered) / 1e6,
   55         "tick_ms_p95": ordered[min(len(ordered) - 1, int(len(ordered) * 0.95))] / 1e6,
   56         "tick_ms_max": ordered[-1] / 1e6,
   57     }
   58 
   59 
   60 def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
   61     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Run a seeded checkpoint scenario and write its record stream.")
   62     parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=None, help="Required unless --replay is given.")
   63     parser.add_argument("--ticks", type=int, default=100)
   64     parser.add_argument("--actors", type=int, default=6)
   65     parser.add_argument("--sources", type=int, default=2)
   66     parser.add_argument("--out", default=None, help="Run file path. Default: runs/<run_id>.jsonl")
   67     parser.add_argument("--twice", action="store_true", help="Run again to a second file and compare trail digests.")
   68     parser.add_argument("--html", action="store_true", help="Render the viewer next to the run file.")
   69     parser.add_argument("--replay", default=None, metavar="FILE",
   70                         help="Replay a sealed scenario run from its header genesis and recorded inputs, "
   71                              "checked against the file (slice 1c).")
   72     parser.add_argument("--recover", default=None, metavar="FILE",
   73                         help="Recover a cut or broken sealed scenario run through its last sealed tick "
   74                              "into the new file given by --out (slice 1c).")
   75     return parser
   76 
   77 
   78 def recover_main(path: Path, out: str | None) -> int:
   79     from stream.recover import RecoveryError, recover_scenario, recovery_lines
   80     if not out:
   81         sys.stderr.write("error: --recover needs --out for the recovered file\n")
   82         return 2
   83     try:
   84         result = recover_scenario(path, Path(out))
   85     except (RecoveryError, RunFileError, ValueError, FileExistsError) as exc:
   86         sys.stderr.write(f"error: {exc}\n")
   87         return 2
   88     checked = read_run(Path(out))
   89     sys.stdout.write("\n".join(recovery_lines(result) + [f"file_verifies: {'yes' if checked.complete else 'no'}"]) + "\n")
   90     return 0 if checked.complete else 1
   91 
   92 
   93 def replay_main(path: Path) -> int:
   94     from stream.replay import ReplayError, replay_lines, replay_scenario
   95     try:
   96         result = replay_scenario(path)
   97     except (ReplayError, RunFileError, ValueError) as exc:
   98         sys.stderr.write(f"error: {exc}\n")
   99         return 2
  100     sys.stdout.write("\n".join(replay_lines(result)) + "\n")
  101     return 0 if result.identical else 1
  102 
  103 
  104 def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
  105     args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
  106     if args.recover:
  107         return recover_main(Path(args.recover), args.out)
  108     if args.replay:
  109         return replay_main(Path(args.replay))
  110     if args.seed is None:
  111         sys.stderr.write("error: --seed is required unless --replay or --recover is given\n")
  112         return 2
  113     if args.ticks < 1 or args.actors < 1 or args.sources < 1:
  114         sys.stderr.write("error: ticks, actors and sources must be positive\n")
  115         return 2
  116     scenario = Scenario(seed=args.seed, actors=args.actors, sources=args.sources)
  117     run_id = run_id_for(scenario, args.ticks)
  118     path = Path(args.out) if args.out else DEFAULT_RUNS_DIR / f"{run_id}.jsonl"
  119     if path.exists():
  120         sys.stderr.write(f"error: {path} exists; a run file is never overwritten\n")
  121         return 2
  122     result = run_scenario(scenario, args.ticks, path)
  123     checked = read_run(path)
  124     lines = [
  125         "V3 checkpoint run",
  126         f"run_id: {run_id}",
  127         f"file: {path}",
  128         f"ticks: {result['ticks']}",
  129         f"trail_digest: {result['trail_digest']}",
  130         f"final_state_digest: {result['final_state_digest']}",
  131         f"file_verifies: {'yes' if checked.complete else 'no'}",
  132         f"tick_ms_mean: {result['tick_ms_mean']:.3f}",
  133         f"tick_ms_p95: {result['tick_ms_p95']:.3f}",
  134         f"tick_ms_max: {result['tick_ms_max']:.3f}",
  135     ]
  136     for problem in checked.problems:
  137         lines.append(f"problem: {problem}")
  138     if args.twice:
  139         second_path = path.with_name(path.stem + ".rerun" + path.suffix)
  140         if second_path.exists():
  141             sys.stderr.write(f"error: {second_path} exists\n")
  142             return 2
  143         second = run_scenario(scenario, args.ticks, second_path)
  144         identical = second["trail_digest"] == result["trail_digest"]
  145         lines.append(f"rerun_file: {second_path}")
  146         lines.append(f"determinism: {'identical' if identical else 'DIFFERENT'}")
  147         if not identical:
  148             sys.stdout.write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
  149             return 1
  150     if args.html:
  151         from stream.viewer import render_html
  152         html_path = path.with_suffix(".html")
  153         html_path.write_text(render_html(checked), encoding="utf-8")
  154         lines.append(f"viewer: {html_path}")
  155     lines.append("note: a run file is evidence only where a stage record cites it; never acceptance")
  156     sys.stdout.write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
  157     return 0 if checked.complete else 1
  158 
  159 
  160 if __name__ == "__main__":
  161     raise SystemExit(main())

===== world/run.py =====
    1 """Run a seeded world, stream every tick, and check it reproduces.
    2 
    3     py -3 -B -m world.run --seed 7 --ticks 300
    4     py -3 -B -m world.run --seed 7 --ticks 300 --twice --html
    5     py -3 -B -m world.run --seed 7 --ticks 300 --renewal-every 2   # a different configuration
    6 
    7 One tick:
    8   1. every living person observes (world/observe.py)
    9   2. every living person decides (world/decide.py); eat and claim become
   10      kernel proposals, moves are kept for the process step
   11   3. the kernel settles the proposals: this is the only place food moves
   12   4. world processes run (world/process.py): movement, hunger, death, renewal
   13   5. the tick line records the kernel record and state, the proposals
   14      submitted, the overlay, every decision, every observation, and any
   15      production, sealed (v3.stream.3); a timing line records wall-clock cost
   16 
   17 Steps 1-4 are `world_step`, the one path both a run and its replay take.
   18     py -3 -B -m world.run --replay runs/<file>.jsonl   # slice 1c: replay against the file
   19     py -3 -B -m world.run --recover runs/<cut>.jsonl --out runs/<new>.jsonl   # slice 1c: recovery
   20 
   21 Output goes to runs/<run_id>.jsonl (and .html). runs/ is ignored by git: a
   22 checkpoint run is exploration output, not evidence.
   23 """
   24 
   25 from __future__ import annotations
   26 
   27 import argparse
   28 import sys
   29 import time
   30 from dataclasses import dataclass
   31 from pathlib import Path
   32 from typing import Any
   33 
   34 from kernel import Engine, Proposal, TickRecord, WorldState, claim, consume
   35 
   36 from stream.run_file import RunFileError, RunWriter, read_run
   37 from world.config import FOOD_SOURCE, WorldConfig, genesis
   38 from world.decide import CLAIM, EAT, Decision, decide
   39 from world.observe import Observation, observe
   40 from world.overlay import Overlay
   41 
   42 from world.process import Processed, advance
   43 
   44 DEFAULT_RUNS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "runs"
   45 
   46 
   47 def run_id_for(config: WorldConfig, ticks: int) -> str:
   48     mode = "on" if config.yield_on else "off"
   49     return f"{config.name}-seed{config.seed}-ticks{ticks}-yield{mode}" + ("-scoringon" if config.scoring_on else "")
   50 
   51 
   52 def proposals_for(decisions: dict[str, Decision], tick: int) -> list[Proposal]:
   53     out: list[Proposal] = []
   54     for actor in sorted(decisions):
   55         decision = decisions[actor]
   56         pid = f"t{tick}-{actor}"
   57         if decision.kind == EAT:
   58             out.append(consume(pid, actor, 0, amount=decision.amount))
   59         elif decision.kind == CLAIM:
   60             out.append(claim(pid, actor, 0, sources={FOOD_SOURCE: decision.amount}))
   61     return out
   62 
   63 
   64 @dataclass(frozen=True)
   65 class WorldRun:
   66     path: Path
   67     ticks: int
   68     trail_digest: str
   69     final_state_digest: str
   70     final_overlay_digest: str
   71     survivors: int
   72     deaths: int
   73     tick_ms_mean: float
   74     tick_ms_p95: float
   75     tick_ms_max: float
   76 
   77 
   78 @dataclass(frozen=True)
   79 class WorldStep:
   80     """One tick of the world loop, before anything is written."""
   81 
   82     views: dict[str, Observation]
   83     decisions: dict[str, Decision]
   84     proposals: list[Proposal]
   85     record: TickRecord
   86     committed: WorldState          # what settlement committed
   87     processed: Processed           # the world processes applied after it
   88     engine: Engine                 # the engine the next tick runs on
   89 
   90     def line_fields(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   91         """The world blocks of this tick's line, as the writer and replay take them."""
   92         return {
   93             "world": self.processed.overlay.canonical(),
   94             "decisions": {actor: d.canonical() for actor, d in self.decisions.items()},
   95             "observations": {actor: view.compact() for actor, view in self.views.items()},
   96             "production": list(self.processed.production) or None,
   97             "produced_state": self.processed.ledger,
   98         }
   99 
  100 
  101 def world_step(engine: Engine, overlay: Overlay, config: WorldConfig) -> WorldStep:
  102     """Observe, decide, settle, process: one tick from `engine`'s state and `overlay`."""
  103     state = engine.state
  104     views = {actor: observe(actor, state, overlay, config) for actor in overlay.living}
  105     decisions = {actor: decide(views[actor], config) for actor in overlay.living}
  106     proposals = proposals_for(decisions, state.tick)
  107     record = engine.tick(proposals)
  108     committed = engine.state
  109     processed = advance(overlay, decisions, record, committed, config)
  110     next_engine = Engine(processed.ledger) if processed.production else engine
  111     return WorldStep(views=views, decisions=decisions, proposals=proposals, record=record,
  112                      committed=committed, processed=processed, engine=next_engine)
  113 
  114 
  115 def run_world(config: WorldConfig, ticks: int, path: Path) -> WorldRun:
  116     ledger, overlay = genesis(config)
  117     engine = Engine(ledger)
  118     elapsed: list[int] = []
  119     with RunWriter(path, run_id=run_id_for(config, ticks), genesis=ledger, scenario=config.describe(),
  120                    world=overlay.canonical(), horizon=ticks) as writer:
  121         for _ in range(ticks):
  122             started = time.perf_counter_ns()
  123             step = world_step(engine, overlay, config)
  124             cost = time.perf_counter_ns() - started
  125             elapsed.append(cost)
  126             writer.record(step.record, step.committed, inputs=step.proposals, elapsed_ns=cost,
  127                           **step.line_fields())
  128             overlay, engine = step.processed.overlay, step.engine
  129         trail = writer.close()
  130     ordered = sorted(elapsed) or [0]
  131     return WorldRun(
  132         path=path, ticks=ticks, trail_digest=trail,
  133         final_state_digest=engine.state.digest(), final_overlay_digest=overlay.digest(),
  134         survivors=len(overlay.living), deaths=len(overlay.died_at),
  135         tick_ms_mean=sum(ordered) / len(ordered) / 1e6,
  136         tick_ms_p95=ordered[min(len(ordered) - 1, int(len(ordered) * 0.95))] / 1e6,
  137         tick_ms_max=ordered[-1] / 1e6,
  138     )
  139 
  140 
  141 LEVERS = ("width", "height", "actors", "starting_food", "source_stock", "source_cap", "renewal_every",
  142           "renewal_amount", "claim_amount", "hunger_rate", "satiation", "hungry_at", "emergency_at", "death_at",
  143           "perception_radius")
  144 
  145 
  146 def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
  147     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Run a seeded one-source grid world and write its record stream.")
  148     parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=None, help="Required unless --replay is given.")
  149     parser.add_argument("--ticks", type=int, default=300)
  150     for lever in LEVERS:
  151         parser.add_argument(f"--{lever.replace('_', '-')}", type=int, default=None, help=f"world lever (default from WorldConfig)")
  152     parser.add_argument("--yield", dest="yield_mode", choices=("on", "off"), default="on",
  153                         help="crowd-yield trait on (default) or off (every yield_at = actors+1)")
  154     parser.add_argument("--out", default=None, help="Run file path. Default: runs/<run_id>.jsonl")
  155     parser.add_argument("--scoring", choices=("on", "off"), default="off",
  156                         help="score eligible personal actions (opt in); food allocation is unchanged")
  157     parser.add_argument("--twice", action="store_true", help="Run again to a second file and compare trail digests.")
  158     parser.add_argument("--html", action="store_true", help="Render the map viewer next to the run file.")
  159     parser.add_argument("--replay", default=None, metavar="FILE",
  160                         help="Replay a sealed world run from its header, recomputing every decision, "
  161                              "checked against the file (slice 1c).")
  162     parser.add_argument("--recover", default=None, metavar="FILE",
  163                         help="Recover a cut or broken sealed world run through its last sealed tick "
  164                              "into the new file given by --out (slice 1c).")
  165     return parser
  166 
  167 
  168 def recover_main(path: Path, out: str | None) -> int:
  169     from stream.recover import RecoveryError, recovery_lines
  170     from world.recover import recover_world
  171     if not out:
  172         sys.stderr.write("error: --recover needs --out for the recovered file\n")
  173         return 2
  174     try:
  175         result = recover_world(path, Path(out))
  176     except (RecoveryError, RunFileError, ValueError, FileExistsError) as exc:
  177         sys.stderr.write(f"error: {exc}\n")
  178         return 2
  179     checked = read_run(Path(out))
  180     sys.stdout.write("\n".join(recovery_lines(result) + [f"file_verifies: {'yes' if checked.complete else 'no'}"]) + "\n")
  181     return 0 if checked.complete else 1
  182 
  183 
  184 def replay_main(path: Path) -> int:
  185     from stream.replay import ReplayError, replay_lines
  186     from world.replay import replay_world
  187     try:
  188         result = replay_world(path)
  189     except (ReplayError, RunFileError, ValueError) as exc:
  190         sys.stderr.write(f"error: {exc}\n")
  191         return 2
  192     sys.stdout.write("\n".join(replay_lines(result)) + "\n")
  193     return 0 if result.identical else 1
  194 
  195 
  196 def config_from(args: argparse.Namespace) -> WorldConfig:
  197     levers: dict[str, Any] = {lever: getattr(args, lever) for lever in LEVERS if getattr(args, lever) is not None}
  198     levers["yield_on"] = args.yield_mode == "on"
  199     levers["scoring_on"] = args.scoring == "on"
  200     return WorldConfig(seed=args.seed, **levers)
  201 
  202 
  203 def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
  204     args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
  205     if args.recover:
  206         return recover_main(Path(args.recover), args.out)
  207     if args.replay:
  208         return replay_main(Path(args.replay))
  209     if args.seed is None:
  210         sys.stderr.write("error: --seed is required unless --replay or --recover is given\n")
  211         return 2
  212     if args.ticks < 1:
  213         sys.stderr.write("error: ticks must be positive\n")
  214         return 2
  215     try:
  216         config = config_from(args)
  217     except ValueError as exc:
  218         sys.stderr.write(f"error: {exc}\n")
  219         return 2
  220     run_id = run_id_for(config, args.ticks)
  221     path = Path(args.out) if args.out else DEFAULT_RUNS_DIR / f"{run_id}.jsonl"
  222     if path.exists():
  223         sys.stderr.write(f"error: {path} exists; a run file is never overwritten\n")
  224         return 2
  225     result = run_world(config, args.ticks, path)
  226     checked = read_run(path)
  227     lines = [
  228         "V3 checkpoint run (world)",
  229         f"run_id: {run_id}",
  230         f"file: {path}",
  231         f"ticks: {result.ticks}",
  232         f"trail_digest: {result.trail_digest}",
  233         f"final_state_digest: {result.final_state_digest}",
  234         f"final_overlay_digest: {result.final_overlay_digest}",
  235         f"survivors: {result.survivors}  deaths: {result.deaths}",
  236         f"yield: {config.describe()['yield']}",
  237         f"scoring: {config.describe()['scoring']}",
  238         f"yield_events: {sum(1 for tick in checked.ticks for d in tick.get('decisions', {}).values() if d.get('kind') == 'yield')}",
  239         f"file_verifies: {'yes' if checked.complete else 'no'}",
  240         f"tick_ms_mean: {result.tick_ms_mean:.3f}",
  241         f"tick_ms_p95: {result.tick_ms_p95:.3f}",
  242         f"tick_ms_max: {result.tick_ms_max:.3f}",
  243     ]
  244     for problem in checked.problems:
  245         lines.append(f"problem: {problem}")
  246     if args.twice:
  247         second_path = path.with_name(path.stem + ".rerun" + path.suffix)
  248         if second_path.exists():
  249             sys.stderr.write(f"error: {second_path} exists\n")
  250             return 2
  251         second = run_world(config, args.ticks, second_path)
  252         identical = second.trail_digest == result.trail_digest
  253         lines.append(f"rerun_file: {second_path}")
  254         lines.append(f"determinism: {'identical' if identical else 'DIFFERENT'}")
  255         if not identical:
  256             sys.stdout.write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
  257             return 1
  258     if args.html:
  259         from world.viewer import render_html
  260         html_path = path.with_suffix(".html")
  261         html_path.write_text(render_html(checked), encoding="utf-8")
  262         lines.append(f"viewer: {html_path}")
  263     lines.append("note: a run file is evidence only where a stage record cites it; never acceptance")
  264     sys.stdout.write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
  265     return 0 if checked.complete else 1
  266 
  267 
  268 if __name__ == "__main__":
  269     raise SystemExit(main())

===== world/replay.py =====
    1 """Replay a sealed world run against its own file (slice 1c, declaration item 6).
    2 
    3 A world run's proposals are not external input: every one comes from a
    4 decision the live code makes from the run's own state. Replay therefore
    5 rebuilds the configuration and genesis from the header and recomputes
    6 observations, decisions, proposals, settlement and the world processes tick
    7 by tick through `world_step`, the same path a run takes, comparing every tick
    8 line with the file, byte for byte without its seal.
    9 
   10 This lives in world/ because the stream never imports the world; it uses the
   11 stream's file functions and comparison.
   12 """
   13 
   14 from __future__ import annotations
   15 
   16 from collections.abc import Iterator
   17 from pathlib import Path
   18 from typing import Any
   19 
   20 from kernel import Engine
   21 
   22 from stream.replay import ReplayError, ReplayResult, compare, genesis_differs, sealed_run
   23 from stream.run_file import tick_payload
   24 from world.config import WorldConfig, genesis
   25 from world.run import world_step
   26 
   27 
   28 def replay_world(path: Path) -> ReplayResult:
   29     """Replay a world run from its header configuration and genesis, recomputing
   30     every decision with the live code, and compare every tick line with the file."""
   31     run = sealed_run(path)
   32     if not run.has_world:
   33         raise ReplayError(f"{path} is not a world run; replay it with stream.run --replay")
   34     try:
   35         config = WorldConfig.from_describe(run.header.get("scenario"))
   36     except ValueError as exc:
   37         raise ReplayError(f"{path} does not describe this world: {exc}") from exc
   38     ledger, overlay = genesis(config)
   39     if ledger.canonical() != run.header.get("genesis") or overlay.canonical() != run.header.get("world"):
   40         return genesis_differs(run)
   41 
   42     def payloads() -> Iterator[dict[str, Any]]:
   43         engine, current = Engine(ledger), overlay
   44         for _ in run.ticks:
   45             step = world_step(engine, current, config)
   46             yield tick_payload(step.record, step.committed, inputs=step.proposals, **step.line_fields())
   47             engine, current = step.engine, step.processed.overlay
   48 
   49     return compare(run, payloads())

===== world/recover.py =====
    1 """Recover a cut or broken sealed world run (slice 1c, declaration item 7).
    2 
    3 A world's state is the kernel ledger plus the overlay, both stored in full on
    4 every sealed tick line, and every later proposal comes from a decision the
    5 live code makes from them. Recovery therefore restores both from the last
    6 sealed tick and continues through `world_step`, the same path a run takes,
    7 with the stream's file functions. This lives in world/ because the stream
    8 never imports the world.
    9 """
   10 
   11 from __future__ import annotations
   12 
   13 import time
   14 from pathlib import Path
   15 
   16 from kernel import Engine
   17 
   18 from stream.recover import (RecoveryError, RecoveryResult, recovery_result, restored_state, resume_writer,
   19                             sealed_prefix)
   20 from world.config import WorldConfig
   21 from world.overlay import Overlay
   22 from world.run import world_step
   23 
   24 
   25 def recover_world(source: Path, out: Path) -> RecoveryResult:
   26     """Recover a world run into the new file `out`."""
   27     prefix = sealed_prefix(source)
   28     if "world" not in prefix.header:
   29         raise RecoveryError(f"{source} is not a world run; recover it with stream.run --recover")
   30     try:
   31         config = WorldConfig.from_describe(prefix.header.get("scenario"))
   32     except ValueError as exc:
   33         raise RecoveryError(f"{source} does not describe this world: {exc}") from exc
   34     last = prefix.ticks[-1] if prefix.ticks else None
   35     engine = Engine(restored_state(prefix.header, last))
   36     overlay = Overlay.from_canonical(last["world"] if last else prefix.header["world"])
   37     if overlay.digest() != (last["world_digest"] if last else prefix.header["world_digest"]):
   38         raise RecoveryError("the restored overlay does not match its stored digest")
   39     holds = len(engine.state.reservations)
   40     with resume_writer(out, prefix) as writer:
   41         for _ in range(prefix.remaining):
   42             started = time.perf_counter_ns()
   43             step = world_step(engine, overlay, config)
   44             cost = time.perf_counter_ns() - started
   45             writer.record(step.record, step.committed, inputs=step.proposals, elapsed_ns=cost, **step.line_fields())
   46             overlay, engine = step.processed.overlay, step.engine
   47         trail = writer.close()
   48     return recovery_result(prefix, out, trail, writer.seal, holds)

===== kernel/state.py =====
    1 """World state, account addressing, and the read-only views handed to observers.
    2 
    3 State is immutable. A tick does not edit a world; it produces the next one. That
    4 is what makes observation inert: a view or a state taken earlier keeps reporting
    5 what it reported, because the object it points at is never written to again.
    6 
    7 Three account kinds exist. `actor:<id>` is an actor's exclusive holding.
    8 `source:<id>` is a shared source with a declared set of authorised claimants.
    9 `sink:consumed` is the single declared consumption sink, which is credit only:
   10 consumed units leave circulation but stay inside the conserved total, so loss
   11 can never be confused with leakage.
   12 """
   13 
   14 from __future__ import annotations
   15 
   16 from collections.abc import Mapping
   17 from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   18 from types import MappingProxyType
   19 from typing import Any
   20 
   21 from kernel.canonical import digest as canonical_digest
   22 from kernel.units import is_integer, is_valid_balance
   23 from kernel.version import SCHEMA_VERSION
   24 
   25 ACTOR_PREFIX = "actor:"
   26 SOURCE_PREFIX = "source:"
   27 SINK_ACCOUNT = "sink:consumed"
   28 
   29 
   30 def actor_account(actor_id: str) -> str:
   31     return ACTOR_PREFIX + actor_id
   32 
   33 
   34 def source_account(source_id: str) -> str:
   35     return SOURCE_PREFIX + source_id
   36 
   37 
   38 def is_actor_account(account: str) -> bool:
   39     return account.startswith(ACTOR_PREFIX)
   40 
   41 
   42 def is_source_account(account: str) -> bool:
   43     return account.startswith(SOURCE_PREFIX)
   44 
   45 
   46 def actor_of(account: str) -> str:
   47     return account[len(ACTOR_PREFIX) :]
   48 
   49 
   50 def source_of(account: str) -> str:
   51     return account[len(SOURCE_PREFIX) :]
   52 
   53 
   54 def _canonical_map(data: Any, keys: frozenset[str], what: str) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
   55     """Refuse anything but a mapping with exactly the canonical keys of `what`.
   56 
   57     The `from_canonical` constructors below rebuild objects from stored
   58     canonical forms (slice 1c: replay, recovery, restored instances). They
   59     add no rule of their own: they check the shape, then build through the
   60     ordinary constructors, so the same rails guard a restored object as a
   61     live one.
   62     """
   63     if not isinstance(data, Mapping) or set(data) != keys:
   64         raise ValueError(f"a canonical {what} needs exactly the keys {sorted(keys)}")
   65     return data
   66 
   67 
   68 @dataclass(frozen=True)
   69 class Effect:
   70     """One signed movement against one account. Never applied on its own."""
   71 
   72     account: str
   73     delta: int
   74 
   75     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
   76         if not isinstance(self.account, str) or not self.account:
   77             raise ValueError("an effect needs a non-empty account address")
   78         if not is_integer(self.delta):
   79             raise ValueError(f"an effect delta must be an integer, got {self.delta!r}")
   80 
   81     def canonical(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   82         return {"account": self.account, "delta": self.delta}
   83 
   84     @classmethod
   85     def from_canonical(cls, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> "Effect":
   86         data = _canonical_map(data, frozenset({"account", "delta"}), "effect")
   87         return cls(account=data["account"], delta=data["delta"])
   88 
   89 
   90 @dataclass(frozen=True)
   91 class Source:
   92     """A shared source, claimable only by the actors named in `authorised`."""
   93 
   94     stock: int
   95     authorised: frozenset[str] = frozenset()
   96 
   97     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
   98         if not is_valid_balance(self.stock):
   99             raise ValueError(f"a source stock must be an integer of zero or more, got {self.stock!r}")
  100         authorised = frozenset(self.authorised)
  101         for actor_id in authorised:
  102             if not isinstance(actor_id, str) or not actor_id:
  103                 raise ValueError("an authorised claimant needs a non-empty identity")
  104         object.__setattr__(self, "authorised", authorised)
  105 
  106     def permits(self, actor_id: str) -> bool:
  107         return actor_id in self.authorised
  108 
  109     def canonical(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  110         return {"stock": self.stock, "authorised": sorted(self.authorised)}
  111 
  112     @classmethod
  113     def from_canonical(cls, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> "Source":
  114         data = _canonical_map(data, frozenset({"stock", "authorised"}), "source")
  115         authorised = data["authorised"]
  116         if not isinstance(authorised, (list, tuple)) or any(not isinstance(actor_id, str) for actor_id in authorised):
  117             raise ValueError("a canonical source needs a list of claimant identities")
  118         if list(authorised) != sorted(set(authorised)):
  119             raise ValueError("a canonical source lists its claimants sorted and once each")
  120         return cls(stock=data["stock"], authorised=frozenset(authorised))
  121 
  122 
  123 @dataclass(frozen=True)
  124 class SourceView:
  125     """What an observer is told about a source."""
  126 
  127     source_id: str
  128     stock: int
  129     authorised: tuple[str, ...]
  130     reserved: int = 0
  131 
  132     @property
  133     def available_stock(self) -> int:
  134         return self.stock - self.reserved
  135 
  136     def permits(self, actor_id: str) -> bool:
  137         return actor_id in self.authorised
  138 
  139 
  140 @dataclass(frozen=True)
  141 class Reservation:
  142     """A frozen balanced plan; its debits are held inside existing accounts."""
  143 
  144     action_id: str
  145     actor: str
  146     created_tick: int
  147     operation: str
  148     effects: tuple[Effect, ...]
  149 
  150     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
  151         for value in (self.action_id, self.actor, self.operation):
  152             if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
  153                 raise ValueError("a reservation needs non-empty identities and operation")
  154         if not is_integer(self.created_tick) or self.created_tick < 0:
  155             raise ValueError("a reservation creation tick must be a nonnegative integer")
  156         effects = tuple(self.effects)
  157         if not effects or any(not isinstance(effect, Effect) for effect in effects):
  158             raise ValueError("a reservation needs effects")
  159         if sum(effect.delta for effect in effects) != 0 or not any(effect.delta < 0 for effect in effects):
  160             raise ValueError("a reservation needs a balanced plan with debits")
  161         object.__setattr__(self, "effects", tuple(sorted(effects, key=lambda e: (e.account, e.delta))))
  162 
  163     def held(self) -> dict[str, int]:
  164         amounts: dict[str, int] = {}
  165         for effect in self.effects:
  166             if effect.delta < 0:
  167                 amounts[effect.account] = amounts.get(effect.account, 0) - effect.delta
  168         return amounts
  169 
  170     def canonical(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  171         return {
  172             "action_id": self.action_id, "actor": self.actor,
  173             "created_tick": self.created_tick, "operation": self.operation,
  174             "effects": [effect.canonical() for effect in self.effects],
  175         }
  176 
  177     @classmethod
  178     def from_canonical(cls, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> "Reservation":
  179         data = _canonical_map(
  180             data, frozenset({"action_id", "actor", "created_tick", "operation", "effects"}), "reservation")
  181         effects = data["effects"]
  182         if not isinstance(effects, (list, tuple)):
  183             raise ValueError("a canonical reservation needs a list of effects")
  184         return cls(action_id=data["action_id"], actor=data["actor"], created_tick=data["created_tick"],
  185                    operation=data["operation"], effects=tuple(Effect.from_canonical(effect) for effect in effects))
  186 
  187 
  188 @dataclass(frozen=True)
  189 class WorldView:
  190     """One actor's read-only view of the tick-start state.
  191 
  192     Slice 1a gives every actor the whole boundary, because there is no geometry
  193     or perception to bound it with yet. Stage 2 narrows this to a bounded view;
  194     the type exists now so that decisions are already taken against a frozen
  195     boundary rather than against live state.
  196     """
  197 
  198     actor: str
  199     tick: int
  200     balances: Mapping[str, int]
  201     sources: Mapping[str, SourceView]
  202     roster: tuple[str, ...]
  203     reservations: Mapping[str, Reservation] = field(default_factory=lambda: MappingProxyType({}))
  204 
  205     @property
  206     def own_balance(self) -> int:
  207         return self.balances[self.actor]
  208 
  209     @property
  210     def own_available(self) -> int:
  211         return self.own_balance - sum(
  212             reservation.held().get(actor_account(self.actor), 0)
  213             for reservation in self.reservations.values()
  214         )
  215 
  216 
  217 @dataclass(frozen=True)
  218 class WorldState:
  219     """The whole canonical world at one tick boundary."""
  220 
  221     tick: int
  222     balances: Mapping[str, int]
  223     sources: Mapping[str, Source]
  224     consumed: int = 0
  225     reservations: Mapping[str, Reservation] = field(default_factory=dict)
  226 
  227     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
  228         if not is_integer(self.tick) or self.tick < 0:
  229             raise ValueError(f"a tick must be an integer of zero or more, got {self.tick!r}")
  230         if not is_valid_balance(self.consumed):
  231             raise ValueError(f"the consumption sink must hold an integer of zero or more, got {self.consumed!r}")
  232 
  233         raw_balances = dict(self.balances)
  234         for actor_id in raw_balances:
  235             if not isinstance(actor_id, str) or not actor_id:
  236                 raise ValueError("an actor needs a non-empty identity")
  237         balances = {actor_id: raw_balances[actor_id] for actor_id in sorted(raw_balances)}
  238         for actor_id, amount in balances.items():
  239             if not is_valid_balance(amount):
  240                 raise ValueError(f"the balance of {actor_id!r} must be an integer of zero or more, got {amount!r}")
  241 
  242         raw_sources = dict(self.sources)
  243         for source_id in raw_sources:
  244             if not isinstance(source_id, str) or not source_id:
  245                 raise ValueError("a source needs a non-empty identity")
  246         sources = {source_id: raw_sources[source_id] for source_id in sorted(raw_sources)}
  247         for source_id, source in sources.items():
  248             if not isinstance(source, Source):
  249                 raise ValueError(f"{source_id!r} is not a Source")
  250 
  251         object.__setattr__(self, "balances", MappingProxyType(balances))
  252         object.__setattr__(self, "sources", MappingProxyType(sources))
  253         reservations = dict(self.reservations)
  254         for action_id, reservation in reservations.items():
  255             if not isinstance(reservation, Reservation) or action_id != reservation.action_id:
  256                 raise ValueError("reservation key must match its action identity")
  257             if reservation.actor not in balances or reservation.created_tick >= self.tick:
  258                 raise ValueError("a reservation needs an existing actor and an earlier creation tick")
  259             for effect in reservation.effects:
  260                 account = effect.account
  261                 if account == SINK_ACCOUNT:
  262                     if effect.delta < 0:
  263                         raise ValueError("a reservation cannot debit consumed stock")
  264                 elif is_actor_account(account):
  265                     if actor_of(account) not in balances:
  266                         raise ValueError("a reservation names an unknown actor")
  267                     if effect.delta < 0 and actor_of(account) != reservation.actor:
  268                         raise ValueError("a reservation cannot debit another actor")
  269                 elif is_source_account(account):
  270                     if source_of(account) not in sources:
  271                         raise ValueError("a reservation names an unknown source")
  272                     if effect.delta < 0 and not sources[source_of(account)].permits(reservation.actor):
  273                         raise ValueError("a reservation needs source authority")
  274                 else:
  275                     raise ValueError("a reservation names an unknown account")
  276         object.__setattr__(self, "reservations", MappingProxyType(dict(sorted(reservations.items()))))
  277         if any(amount < 0 for amount in self.availability().values()):
  278             raise ValueError("reserved stock exceeds its account balance")
  279 
  280     @classmethod
  281     def genesis(
  282         cls,
  283         *,
  284         tick: int = 0,
  285         balances: Mapping[str, int] | None = None,
  286         sources: Mapping[str, Source] | None = None,
  287         consumed: int = 0,
  288         reservations: Mapping[str, Reservation] | None = None,
  289     ) -> "WorldState":
  290         return cls(
  291             tick=tick,
  292             balances=dict(balances or {}),
  293             sources=dict(sources or {}),
  294             consumed=consumed,
  295             reservations=dict(reservations or {}),
  296         )
  297 
  298     @classmethod
  299     def from_canonical(cls, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> "WorldState":
  300         """Rebuild a state from `canonical()`, as replay, recovery and restored
  301         instances need. Only this schema version is accepted, and the result is
  302         built through the constructor, so every rail that guards a live state
  303         guards a restored one. `from_canonical(s.canonical()).digest() == s.digest()`."""
  304         data = _canonical_map(
  305             data, frozenset({"schema_version", "tick", "balances", "sources", "consumed", "reservations"}), "state")
  306         if data["schema_version"] != SCHEMA_VERSION:
  307             raise ValueError(f"a canonical state of schema {data['schema_version']!r} is not {SCHEMA_VERSION}")
  308         for name in ("balances", "sources", "reservations"):
  309             if not isinstance(data[name], Mapping):
  310                 raise ValueError(f"a canonical state needs a mapping of {name}")
  311         return cls(
  312             tick=data["tick"],
  313             balances=dict(data["balances"]),
  314             sources={source_id: Source.from_canonical(source) for source_id, source in data["sources"].items()},
  315             consumed=data["consumed"],
  316             reservations={action_id: Reservation.from_canonical(reservation)
  317                           for action_id, reservation in data["reservations"].items()},
  318         )
  319 
  320     @property
  321     def roster(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
  322         """The tick-start actor identities, sorted."""
  323         return tuple(sorted(self.balances))
  324 
  325     def total(self) -> int:
  326         """The conserved total: everything held, everything in a source, everything consumed."""
  327         return (
  328             sum(self.balances.values())
  329             + sum(source.stock for source in self.sources.values())
  330             + self.consumed
  331         )
  332 
  333     def availability(self) -> dict[str, int]:
  334         """A detached account map of free stock; holds are counted only once."""
  335         available = {actor_account(actor): amount for actor, amount in self.balances.items()}
  336         available.update({source_account(name): source.stock for name, source in self.sources.items()})
  337         available[SINK_ACCOUNT] = 0
  338         for reservation in self.reservations.values():
  339             for account, amount in reservation.held().items():
  340                 available[account] -= amount
  341         return available
  342 
  343     def view_for(self, actor_id: str) -> WorldView:
  344         if actor_id not in self.balances:
  345             raise KeyError(f"no such actor: {actor_id!r}")
  346         available = self.availability()
  347         return WorldView(
  348             actor=actor_id,
  349             tick=self.tick,
  350             balances=MappingProxyType(dict(self.balances)),
  351             sources=MappingProxyType(
  352                 {
  353                     source_id: SourceView(
  354                         source_id=source_id,
  355                         stock=source.stock,
  356                         authorised=tuple(sorted(source.authorised)),
  357                         reserved=source.stock - available[source_account(source_id)],
  358                     )
  359                     for source_id, source in self.sources.items()
  360                 }
  361             ),
  362             roster=self.roster,
  363             reservations=MappingProxyType(dict(self.reservations)),
  364         )
  365 
  366     def canonical(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  367         return {
  368             "schema_version": SCHEMA_VERSION,
  369             "tick": self.tick,
  370             "balances": dict(self.balances),
  371             "sources": {source_id: source.canonical() for source_id, source in self.sources.items()},
  372             "consumed": self.consumed,
  373             "reservations": {action_id: reservation.canonical() for action_id, reservation in self.reservations.items()},
  374         }
  375 
  376     def digest(self) -> str:
  377         return canonical_digest(self.canonical())

===== world/overlay.py =====
    1 """The world state the kernel does not hold: homes, positions, hunger, deaths.
    2 
    3 Immutable and canonical like the kernel's WorldState; replaced, never edited.
    4 Food units are not here on purpose: they live in the kernel ledger, and the
    5 overlay only ever reads them through the kernel's own views.
    6 """
    7 
    8 from __future__ import annotations
    9 
   10 from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   11 from types import MappingProxyType
   12 from typing import Any, Mapping
   13 
   14 from kernel import digest as canonical_digest
   15 
   16 Position = tuple[int, int]
   17 
   18 
   19 def _positions(raw: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Mapping[str, Position]:
   20     out: dict[str, Position] = {}
   21     for actor in sorted(raw):
   22         x, y = raw[actor]
   23         if type(x) is not int or type(y) is not int or x < 0 or y < 0:
   24             raise ValueError(f"{actor!r} needs a non-negative integer position, got {raw[actor]!r}")
   25         out[actor] = (x, y)
   26     return MappingProxyType(out)
   27 
   28 
   29 def _positive_ints(raw: Mapping[str, Any], *, roster: set[str], name: str) -> Mapping[str, int]:
   30     if set(raw) != roster:
   31         raise ValueError(f"{name} must name the same people as homes")
   32     out = {actor: raw[actor] for actor in sorted(raw)}
   33     for actor, value in out.items():
   34         if type(value) is not int or value < 1:
   35             raise ValueError(f"{name} of {actor!r} must be a positive integer, got {value!r}")
   36     return MappingProxyType(out)
   37 
   38 
   39 @dataclass(frozen=True)
   40 class Overlay:
   41     tick: int
   42     homes: Mapping[str, Position]
   43     positions: Mapping[str, Position]
   44     hunger: Mapping[str, int]
   45     yield_at: Mapping[str, int]
   46     died_at: Mapping[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
   47 
   48     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
   49         if type(self.tick) is not int or self.tick < 0:
   50             raise ValueError("a tick must be an integer of zero or more")
   51         homes = _positions(self.homes)
   52         positions = _positions(self.positions)
   53         if set(homes) != set(positions) or set(positions) != set(self.hunger):
   54             raise ValueError("homes, positions and hunger must name the same people")
   55         hunger = {actor: self.hunger[actor] for actor in sorted(self.hunger)}
   56         for actor, value in hunger.items():
   57             if type(value) is not int or value < 0:
   58                 raise ValueError(f"hunger of {actor!r} must be an integer of zero or more")
   59         died = {actor: self.died_at[actor] for actor in sorted(self.died_at)}
   60         for actor, when in died.items():
   61             if actor not in positions or type(when) is not int or when < 0 or when > self.tick:
   62                 raise ValueError(f"death of {actor!r} must name a known person and an earlier tick")
   63         yield_at = _positive_ints(self.yield_at, roster=set(homes), name="yield_at")
   64         object.__setattr__(self, "homes", homes)
   65         object.__setattr__(self, "positions", positions)
   66         object.__setattr__(self, "hunger", MappingProxyType(hunger))
   67         object.__setattr__(self, "died_at", MappingProxyType(died))
   68         object.__setattr__(self, "yield_at", yield_at)
   69 
   70     @property
   71     def roster(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   72         return tuple(sorted(self.positions))
   73 
   74     def alive(self, actor: str) -> bool:
   75         return actor not in self.died_at
   76 
   77     @property
   78     def living(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   79         return tuple(actor for actor in self.roster if self.alive(actor))
   80 
   81     def canonical(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   82         return {
   83             "tick": self.tick,
   84             "homes": {actor: list(pos) for actor, pos in self.homes.items()},
   85             "positions": {actor: list(pos) for actor, pos in self.positions.items()},
   86             "hunger": dict(self.hunger),
   87             "yield_at": dict(self.yield_at),
   88             "died_at": dict(self.died_at),
   89         }
   90 
   91     @classmethod
   92     def from_canonical(cls, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> "Overlay":
   93         """Rebuild an overlay from `canonical()` (slice 1c: replay, recovery).
   94         The shape is checked here; every value is validated by the constructor."""
   95         keys = {"tick", "homes", "positions", "hunger", "yield_at", "died_at"}
   96         if not isinstance(data, Mapping) or set(data) != keys:
   97             raise ValueError(f"a canonical overlay needs exactly the keys {sorted(keys)}")
   98         for name in sorted(keys - {"tick"}):
   99             if not isinstance(data[name], Mapping):
  100                 raise ValueError(f"a canonical overlay needs a mapping of {name}")
  101 
  102         def cells(raw: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Position]:
  103             out: dict[str, Position] = {}
  104             for actor, cell in raw.items():
  105                 if not isinstance(cell, (list, tuple)) or len(cell) != 2:
  106                     raise ValueError(f"{actor!r} needs a two-integer position, got {cell!r}")
  107                 out[actor] = (cell[0], cell[1])
  108             return out
  109 
  110         return cls(tick=data["tick"], homes=cells(data["homes"]), positions=cells(data["positions"]),
  111                    hunger=dict(data["hunger"]), yield_at=dict(data["yield_at"]), died_at=dict(data["died_at"]))
  112 
  113     def digest(self) -> str:
  114         return canonical_digest(self.canonical())

===== world/config.py =====
    1 """The declared world levers and the seeded genesis.
    2 
    3 Levers are the ones ROADMAP Stage 2 allows: geometry, distribution, renewal,
    4 initial supplies, perception radius, consumption rates, and the crowd-yield
    5 trait set. Changing one is a new configuration, and every value is written
    6 into the run header so a run is readable on its own.
    7 
    8 Genesis uses one named deterministic generator, `homes-uniform-v1+yield-v1`:
    9 homes are drawn without replacement from every cell except the source cell
   10 using `random.Random(seed)`, then `yield_at` is drawn from the same RNG
   11 instance. Home draws are unchanged from `homes-uniform-v1`. Nothing else in a
   12 run uses randomness; decisions and processes are pure rules (DOCTRINE: no
   13 runtime randomness through Stage 3).
   14 """
   15 
   16 from __future__ import annotations
   17 
   18 import random
   19 from collections.abc import Mapping
   20 from dataclasses import dataclass
   21 from typing import Any
   22 
   23 from kernel import Source, WorldState
   24 
   25 from world.overlay import Overlay
   26 
   27 GENESIS_GENERATOR = "homes-uniform-v1+yield-v1"
   28 FOOD_SOURCE = "food"
   29 DISTANCE_METRIC = "chebyshev"
   30 PERCEPTION_BOUNDARY = "distance <= radius"
   31 DEFAULT_YIELD_SET = (1, 2, 3)
   32 INTEGER_LEVERS = ("seed", "width", "height", "actors", "starting_food", "source_stock", "source_cap",
   33                   "renewal_every", "renewal_amount", "claim_amount", "hunger_rate", "satiation",
   34                   "hungry_at", "emergency_at", "death_at", "perception_radius")
   35 
   36 
   37 @dataclass(frozen=True)
   38 class WorldConfig:
   39     seed: int
   40     width: int = 12
   41     height: int = 12
   42     actors: int = 6
   43     starting_food: int = 1        # units each person holds at genesis
   44     source_stock: int = 4         # units in the source at genesis
   45     source_cap: int = 8           # renewal never lifts stock above this
   46     renewal_every: int = 3        # ticks between renewals
   47     renewal_amount: int = 2       # units added per renewal, capped
   48     claim_amount: int = 2         # most units one claim asks for
   49     hunger_rate: int = 1          # hunger added per tick while alive
   50     satiation: int = 6            # hunger removed per unit eaten
   51     hungry_at: int = 5            # hunger at which a person seeks food
   52     emergency_at: int = 10        # hunger at which the state is an emergency
   53     death_at: int = 16            # hunger at which a person dies
   54     perception_radius: int = 3    # Chebyshev cells; self is always in view
   55     yield_set: tuple[int, ...] = DEFAULT_YIELD_SET
   56     yield_on: bool = True         # False assigns yield_at = actors + 1 so the rule never fires
   57     scoring_on: bool = False      # opt in; OFF preserves the leg-5 selector and decision shape
   58 
   59     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
   60         checks = {
   61             "width": self.width >= 3, "height": self.height >= 3, "actors": self.actors >= 1,
   62             "starting_food": self.starting_food >= 0, "source_stock": self.source_stock >= 0,
   63             "source_cap": self.source_cap >= self.source_stock, "renewal_every": self.renewal_every >= 1,
   64             "renewal_amount": self.renewal_amount >= 0, "claim_amount": self.claim_amount >= 1,
   65             "hunger_rate": self.hunger_rate >= 0, "satiation": self.satiation >= 1,
   66             "hungry_at": 0 <= self.hungry_at, "emergency_at": self.hungry_at <= self.emergency_at,
   67             "death_at": self.emergency_at < self.death_at,
   68             "perception_radius": self.perception_radius >= 0,
   69             "yield_set": (
   70                 len(self.yield_set) >= 1
   71                 and all(type(value) is int and value >= 1 for value in self.yield_set)
   72             ),
   73             "capacity": self.actors <= self.width * self.height - 1,
   74         }
   75         bad = [name for name, ok in checks.items() if not ok]
   76         if bad:
   77             raise ValueError(f"invalid world configuration: {', '.join(bad)}")
   78         object.__setattr__(self, "yield_set", tuple(self.yield_set))
   79 
   80     @property
   81     def name(self) -> str:
   82         return "one-source-grid"
   83 
   84     @property
   85     def source_position(self) -> tuple[int, int]:
   86         return (self.width // 2, self.height // 2)
   87 
   88     def actor_ids(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   89         return tuple(f"p{index:02d}" for index in range(1, self.actors + 1))
   90 
   91     def describe(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   92         return {
   93             "name": self.name,
   94             "genesis_generator": GENESIS_GENERATOR,
   95             "seed": self.seed,
   96             "width": self.width, "height": self.height, "actors": self.actors,
   97             "source": FOOD_SOURCE, "source_position": list(self.source_position),
   98             "starting_food": self.starting_food, "source_stock": self.source_stock,
   99             "source_cap": self.source_cap, "renewal_every": self.renewal_every,
  100             "renewal_amount": self.renewal_amount, "claim_amount": self.claim_amount,
  101             "hunger_rate": self.hunger_rate, "satiation": self.satiation,
  102             "hungry_at": self.hungry_at, "emergency_at": self.emergency_at, "death_at": self.death_at,
  103             "distance_metric": DISTANCE_METRIC,
  104             "perception_radius": self.perception_radius,
  105             "perception_boundary": PERCEPTION_BOUNDARY,
  106             "perception": (
  107                 "self always; others in radius: identity, position, free food "
  108                 "(not hunger, home, or decision); source position is a known landmark; "
  109                 "source stock only when the source cell is in view (absent otherwise, never stale)"
  110             ),
  111             "dead_are_not_seen": "dead people are neither seen nor counted",
  112             "yield_set": list(self.yield_set),
  113             "yield": "on" if self.yield_on else "off",
  114             "scoring": "on" if self.scoring_on else "off",
  115             "scoring_formula": {
  116                 "eat": "(2, 0)", "claim": "(1, 0)", "wait": "(1, 0)",
  117                 "go": "(0, 2 * (hunger - hungry_at) + 1)",
  118                 "yield": "(0, 2 * (seen_crowd - yield_at + 1))",
  119                 "home": "(0, 0)", "rest": "(0, 0)",
  120             },
  121             "scoring_order": "lexicographic (tier, pressure), greatest first; eligibility unchanged",
  122             "scoring_scale": (
  123                 "one hunger point and one observed person each add 2 pressure units; "
  124                 "equal weighting is a modelling assumption, not an empirical scale"
  125             ),
  126             "scoring_crossover": "GO > YIELD iff hunger >= hungry_at + seen_crowd - yield_at + 1 (both eligible)",
  127             "scoring_crossover_configured": f"hunger >= seen_crowd - yield_at + {self.hungry_at + 1} (both eligible)",
  128             "scoring_ties": "GO odd, YIELD even: never equal; CLAIM/WAIT and HOME/REST mutually exclusive; no additional tie policy",
  129             "food_allocation": "kernel sorted full tick-start roster (including inactive actors), rotated by tick mod actor_count; personal scores confer no priority",
  130             "movement": "one step per tick along the longer axis (x on ties), four neighbours, co-location allowed",
  131             "decision": (
  132                 ("score existing eligible actions; " if self.scoring_on else "fixed priority: ")
  133                 + "eat if hungry and holding; claim if hungry at the source; wait if hungry at an empty source; "
  134                 "yield if hungry, not emergency, off the source, source in view, crowd >= yield_at and stock < crowd; "
  135                 "walk to the source if hungry; else walk home"
  136             ),
  137         }
  138 
  139     @classmethod
  140     def from_describe(cls, described: Mapping[str, Any]) -> "WorldConfig":
  141         """The configuration a run header describes (slice 1c replay and recovery).
  142 
  143         Refused unless `describe()` of the result equals the description exactly:
  144         the world name, the genesis generator, every lever and every line of
  145         declared rule text must be what this code writes, so a header from
  146         another generator or rule set cannot be replayed as this one.
  147         """
  148         if not isinstance(described, Mapping):
  149             raise ValueError("a world description must be a mapping")
  150         if described.get("name") != "one-source-grid" or described.get("genesis_generator") != GENESIS_GENERATOR:
  151             raise ValueError(f"unknown world or genesis generator: "
  152                              f"{described.get('name')!r} {described.get('genesis_generator')!r}")
  153         values: dict[str, Any] = {}
  154         for name in INTEGER_LEVERS:
  155             value = described.get(name)
  156             if type(value) is not int:
  157                 raise ValueError(f"world lever {name} must be an integer, got {value!r}")
  158             values[name] = value
  159         yield_set = described.get("yield_set")
  160         if not isinstance(yield_set, list):
  161             raise ValueError(f"yield_set must be a list, got {yield_set!r}")
  162         switches = {"on": True, "off": False}
  163         if any(not isinstance(described.get(name), str) or described.get(name) not in switches
  164                for name in ("yield", "scoring")):
  165             raise ValueError("yield and scoring must each be 'on' or 'off'")
  166         config = cls(**values, yield_set=tuple(yield_set), yield_on=switches[described["yield"]],
  167                      scoring_on=switches[described["scoring"]])
  168         if config.describe() != dict(described):
  169             raise ValueError("the world description does not round-trip exactly")
  170         return config
  171 
  172 
  173 def _homes_from(rng: random.Random, config: WorldConfig) -> dict[str, tuple[int, int]]:
  174     """First RNG draw: distinct cells for every person, never the source cell."""
  175     cells = [(x, y) for y in range(config.height) for x in range(config.width) if (x, y) != config.source_position]
  176     picks = rng.sample(cells, config.actors)
  177     return dict(zip(config.actor_ids(), picks))
  178 
  179 
  180 def _yield_from(rng: random.Random, config: WorldConfig) -> dict[str, int]:
  181     """Second RNG draw, after homes. OFF assigns actor_count + 1 so the rule cannot fire."""
  182     actors = config.actor_ids()
  183     if not config.yield_on:
  184         return {actor: config.actors + 1 for actor in actors}
  185     bag: list[int] = []
  186     while len(bag) < config.actors:
  187         bag.extend(config.yield_set)
  188     bag = bag[:config.actors]
  189     rng.shuffle(bag)
  190     return dict(zip(actors, bag))
  191 
  192 
  193 def homes_for(config: WorldConfig) -> dict[str, tuple[int, int]]:
  194     """Home placement: the first draw of `homes-uniform-v1+yield-v1`, unchanged from `homes-uniform-v1`."""
  195     return _homes_from(random.Random(config.seed), config)
  196 
  197 
  198 def yield_at_for(config: WorldConfig) -> dict[str, int]:
  199     rng = random.Random(config.seed)
  200     _homes_from(rng, config)
  201     return _yield_from(rng, config)
  202 
  203 
  204 def genesis(config: WorldConfig) -> tuple[WorldState, Overlay]:
  205     """The saved initial state: a kernel ledger and the overlay beside it."""
  206     actors = config.actor_ids()
  207     ledger = WorldState.genesis(
  208         balances={actor: config.starting_food for actor in actors},
  209         sources={FOOD_SOURCE: Source(stock=config.source_stock, authorised=frozenset(actors))},
  210     )
  211     rng = random.Random(config.seed)
  212     homes = _homes_from(rng, config)
  213     overlay = Overlay(
  214         tick=0,
  215         homes=homes,
  216         positions=dict(homes),
  217         hunger={actor: 0 for actor in actors},
  218         yield_at=_yield_from(rng, config),
  219         died_at={},
  220     )
  221     return ledger, overlay

===== stream/scenario.py =====
    1 """A named, versioned, seeded input generator for checkpoint runs.
    2 
    3 The engine uses no runtime randomness. This module is the declared input side:
    4 given a seed it produces the same genesis and the same proposals every time
    5 (CPython's `random.Random` is stable across platforms for a fixed version). It
    6 exercises everything the kernel has so far: claims, transfers, consumption,
    7 reservations, completion and cancellation, plus enough invalid submissions
    8 that the record shows denials with their reasons. It is exploration input, not
    9 a world model: nothing here is a need, a place or a behaviour.
   10 """
   11 
   12 from __future__ import annotations
   13 
   14 import random
   15 from collections.abc import Mapping
   16 from dataclasses import dataclass
   17 from typing import Any
   18 
   19 from kernel import Proposal, Source, WorldState
   20 
   21 SCENARIO_NAME = "contention-and-holds"
   22 SCENARIO_VERSION = "v3.scenario.contention-and-holds.1"
   23 
   24 
   25 @dataclass(frozen=True)
   26 class Scenario:
   27     seed: int
   28     actors: int = 6
   29     sources: int = 2
   30     starting_balance: int = 3
   31     source_stock: int = 60  # the second source holds half; nothing is produced, so every run eventually starves
   32     max_proposals_per_tick: int = 8
   33 
   34     def describe(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   35         return {
   36             "name": SCENARIO_NAME, "version": SCENARIO_VERSION, "seed": self.seed,
   37             "actors": self.actors, "sources": self.sources,
   38             "starting_balance": self.starting_balance, "source_stock": self.source_stock,
   39             "max_proposals_per_tick": self.max_proposals_per_tick,
   40         }
   41 
   42     @classmethod
   43     def from_describe(cls, described: Mapping[str, Any]) -> "Scenario":
   44         """The scenario a run header describes (slice 1c replay and recovery).
   45         Refused unless `describe()` of the result equals the description exactly,
   46         so a header from another generator or version cannot pass as this one."""
   47         if not isinstance(described, Mapping):
   48             raise ValueError("a scenario description must be a mapping")
   49         if described.get("name") != SCENARIO_NAME or described.get("version") != SCENARIO_VERSION:
   50             raise ValueError(f"not a {SCENARIO_VERSION} description: "
   51                              f"{described.get('name')!r} {described.get('version')!r}")
   52         values: dict[str, int] = {}
   53         for name in ("seed", "actors", "sources", "starting_balance", "source_stock", "max_proposals_per_tick"):
   54             value = described.get(name)
   55             if type(value) is not int:
   56                 raise ValueError(f"scenario {name} must be an integer, got {value!r}")
   57             values[name] = value
   58         scenario = cls(**values)
   59         if scenario.describe() != dict(described):
   60             raise ValueError("the scenario description does not round-trip exactly")
   61         return scenario
   62 
   63     def actor_ids(self) -> list[str]:
   64         return [f"p{index + 1}" for index in range(self.actors)]
   65 
   66     def source_ids(self) -> list[str]:
   67         return [f"s{index + 1}" for index in range(self.sources)]
   68 
   69     def genesis(self) -> WorldState:
   70         """Every actor may claim from every source; sources alternate stock so
   71         contention differs between them."""
   72         actors = self.actor_ids()
   73         sources = {
   74             name: Source(self.source_stock if index % 2 == 0 else self.source_stock // 2, tuple(actors))
   75             for index, name in enumerate(self.source_ids())
   76         }
   77         return WorldState.genesis(balances={actor: self.starting_balance for actor in actors}, sources=sources)
   78 
   79 
   80 class ProposalGenerator:
   81     """Stateful only in its seeded PRNG and in the reservation IDs it has seen."""
   82 
   83     def __init__(self, scenario: Scenario) -> None:
   84         self.scenario = scenario
   85         self.rng = random.Random(scenario.seed)
   86         self.actors = scenario.actor_ids()
   87         self.sources = scenario.source_ids()
   88 
   89     def tick(self, tick: int, live_actions: dict[str, str]) -> list[Proposal]:
   90         """Proposals for one tick. `live_actions` maps action ID to owner, from
   91         the previous committed state, so terminal requests can target real holds."""
   92         rng = self.rng
   93         proposals: list[Proposal] = []
   94         count = rng.randrange(0, self.scenario.max_proposals_per_tick + 1)
   95         orders: dict[str, int] = {}
   96         for index in range(count):
   97             actor = rng.choice(self.actors)
   98             order = orders.get(actor, 0)
   99             orders[actor] = order + 1
  100             if rng.random() < 0.04:  # a deliberate duplicate order, denied for both
  101                 order = max(order - 1, 0)
  102             proposal_id = f"t{tick}-{index}"
  103             amount = rng.choice([1, 1, 1, 2, 2, 3])
  104             kind = rng.choices(
  105                 ["claim", "transfer", "consume", "reserve", "close", "bad"],
  106                 weights=[30, 20, 20, 15, 10, 5],
  107             )[0]
  108             if kind == "close":
  109                 owned = [action for action, owner in live_actions.items() if owner == actor]
  110                 if owned and rng.random() < 0.9:
  111                     action = rng.choice(sorted(owned))
  112                 else:
  113                     action = rng.choice(sorted(live_actions) or ["action:none"])  # a foreign or stale ID
  114                 operation = "complete" if rng.random() < 0.7 else "cancel"
  115                 proposals.append(Proposal(proposal_id, actor, order, operation, {"action_id": action}))
  116                 continue
  117             if kind == "bad":
  118                 bad = rng.choice([
  119                     ("consume", {"amount": 0}), ("consume", {"amount": True}),
  120                     ("transfer", {"to": actor, "amount": 1}), ("transfer", {"to": "nobody", "amount": 1}),
  121                     ("claim", {"sources": {"missing": 1}}), ("dance", {"amount": 1}),
  122                 ])
  123                 proposals.append(Proposal(proposal_id, actor, order, bad[0], bad[1]))
  124                 continue
  125             if kind in ("claim", "reserve") and (kind == "claim" or rng.random() < 0.5):
  126                 chosen = rng.sample(self.sources, rng.randrange(1, len(self.sources) + 1))
  127                 inner, params = "claim", {"sources": {source: amount for source in chosen}}
  128             elif kind == "transfer" or (kind == "reserve" and rng.random() < 0.5):
  129                 target = rng.choice([other for other in self.actors if other != actor])
  130                 inner, params = "transfer", {"to": target, "amount": amount}
  131             else:
  132                 inner, params = "consume", {"amount": amount}
  133             if kind == "reserve":
  134                 proposals.append(Proposal(proposal_id, actor, order, "reserve", {"operation": inner, "params": params}))
  135             else:
  136                 proposals.append(Proposal(proposal_id, actor, order, inner, params))
  137         return proposals

===== tests/test_sealed_stream.py =====
    1 """Slice 1c: the sealed evidence stream, v3.stream.3 (declaration items 1-4).
    2 
    3 A sealed file vouches for itself. The header and every tick line are chained
    4 by seals; the reader names the first line that fails any check and reports
    5 the last tick that line and every line before it still vouch for. These are
    6 focused transaction tests on the kernel-only scenario and small worlds; they
    7 are not runs and not behavioural evidence.
    8 """
    9 
   10 from __future__ import annotations
   11 
   12 import hashlib
   13 import json
   14 from pathlib import Path
   15 
   16 import pytest
   17 
   18 from kernel import Engine, WorldState, canonical_bytes, consume, digest
   19 from kernel.proposals import Proposal
   20 from stream.run import run_scenario
   21 from stream.run_file import (STREAM_FORMAT, RunFileError, RunWriter, code_identity, decode_input, encode_input,
   22                              header_seal, read_run, tick_seal)
   23 from stream.scenario import ProposalGenerator, Scenario
   24 from world.config import WorldConfig
   25 from world.run import run_world
   26 
   27 
   28 def lines_of(path: Path) -> list[bytes]:
   29     return path.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)
   30 
   31 
   32 def tick_indexes(lines: list[bytes]) -> list[int]:
   33     return [index for index, line in enumerate(lines) if b'"kind":"tick"' in line]
   34

   36 @pytest.fixture
   37 def scenario_file(tmp_path: Path) -> Path:
   38     path = tmp_path / "scenario.jsonl"
   39     run_scenario(Scenario(seed=7), 30, path)
   40     return path

   43 # --- a fresh file ----------------------------------------------------------------

   45 def test_a_fresh_scenario_run_is_sealed_through_its_last_tick(scenario_file: Path):
   46     run = read_run(scenario_file)
   47     assert run.complete and run.sealed and run.problems == ()
   48     assert run.header["format"] == STREAM_FORMAT == "v3.stream.3"
   49     assert run.last_sealed_tick == 29 and run.first_break_line is None
   50     assert run.header["seal"] == header_seal(run.header)
   51     previous = run.header["seal"]
   52     for tick in run.ticks:
   53         assert tick["seal"] == tick_seal(previous, tick)
   54         previous = tick["seal"]
   55     assert run.end["final_seal"] == previous
   56     assert run.end["ticks"] == run.header["horizon"] == 30

   61     run_world(WorldConfig(seed=7, width=7, height=7, actors=4), 30, path)
   62     run = read_run(path)
   63     assert run.complete and run.sealed and run.last_sealed_tick == 29
   64     assert all("inputs" in tick and "seal" in tick for tick in run.ticks)
