   67 def test_the_header_names_code_configuration_and_horizon_and_nothing_host_specific(scenario_file: Path):
   68     header = read_run(scenario_file).header
   69     assert header["code_identity"] == code_identity()
   70     files = header["code_identity"]["files"]
   71     assert {"kernel/state.py", "stream/run_file.py", "stream/replay.py", "world/run.py"} <= set(files)
   72     assert all(name.split("/")[0] in {"kernel", "stream", "world"} for name in files)
   73     assert header["code_identity"]["digest"] == digest(files)
   74     assert header["config_identity"] == digest(header["scenario"])
   75     assert header["horizon"] == 30
   76     assert header["engine_version"] == "0.2.0-stage1b" and header["schema_version"] == "v3.kernel.1b.1"
   77     assert not [key for key in header if any(word in key for word in ("git", "host", "time", "clock"))]

   80 def test_code_identity_reads_crlf_as_lf(tmp_path: Path):
   81     for text in (b"x = 1\n", b"x = 1\r\n"):
   82         root = tmp_path / ("crlf" if b"\r" in text else "lf")
   83         for package in ("kernel", "stream", "world"):
   84             (root / package).mkdir(parents=True)
   85             (root / package / "m.py").write_bytes(text)
   86     assert code_identity(tmp_path / "lf") == code_identity(tmp_path / "crlf")

   91 def test_inputs_are_the_proposals_the_runner_submitted_in_order(tmp_path: Path):
   92     scenario = Scenario(seed=7)
   93     engine = Engine(scenario.genesis())
   94     generator = ProposalGenerator(scenario)
   95     submitted: list[list[Proposal]] = []
   96     path = tmp_path / "inputs.jsonl"
   97     with RunWriter(path, run_id="inputs", genesis=engine.state, scenario=scenario.describe(), horizon=120) as writer:
   98         for _ in range(120):
   99             live = {action: held.actor for action, held in engine.state.reservations.items()}
  100             proposals = generator.tick(engine.state.tick, live)
  101             submitted.append(proposals)
  102             record = engine.tick(proposals)
  103             writer.record(record, engine.state, inputs=proposals)
  104     run = read_run(path)
  105     assert run.complete
  106     assert [[decode_input(entry) for entry in tick["inputs"]] for tick in run.ticks] == submitted

  109 def test_empty_ticks_and_params_without_a_canonical_form_round_trip(tmp_path: Path):
  110     genesis = WorldState.genesis(balances={"A": 3, "B": 0})
  111     engine = Engine(genesis)
  112     ticks = [
  113         [],
  114         [Proposal("t1-0", "A", 0, "consume", {"amount": True}),
  115          Proposal("t1-1", "A", 1, "transfer", {"to": "B", "amount": 1})],
  116         [Proposal("t2-0", "B", 0, "consume", {"amount": 1.0}),
  117          Proposal("t2-1", "A", 0, "claim", {"sources": {1: 2}}),
  118          Proposal("t2-2", "A", 1, "dance", {"steps": [1, "two", None], "nested": {"k": (True, 3)}})],
  119     ]
  120     path = tmp_path / "typed.jsonl"
  121     with RunWriter(path, run_id="typed", genesis=genesis, scenario={"name": "hand-built"}, horizon=len(ticks)) as writer:
  122         for proposals in ticks:
  123             record = engine.tick(proposals)
  124             writer.record(record, engine.state, inputs=proposals)
  125     run = read_run(path)
  126     assert run.complete and run.last_sealed_tick == 2
  127     assert [[decode_input(entry) for entry in tick["inputs"]] for tick in run.ticks] == ticks
  128     assert run.ticks[0]["inputs"] == []
  129     typed = {entry["proposal_id"] for tick in run.ticks for entry in tick["inputs"] if "params_typed" in entry}
  130     assert typed == {"t1-0", "t2-0", "t2-1", "t2-2"}
  131     assert run.ticks[1]["inputs"][1]["params"] == {"amount": 1, "to": "B"}
  132     assert encode_input(ticks[1][0])["params_typed"] == ["map", [[["str", "amount"], ["bool", 1]]]]

  135 def test_the_writer_refuses_inputs_it_cannot_encode_or_the_record_did_not_settle(tmp_path: Path):
  136     genesis = WorldState.genesis(balances={"A": 1})
  137     engine = Engine(genesis)
  138     odd = Proposal("x", "A", 0, "consume", {"amount": {1, 2}})
  139     record = engine.tick([odd])
  140     writer = RunWriter(tmp_path / "w.jsonl", run_id="w", genesis=genesis, scenario={}, horizon=1)
  141     with pytest.raises(RunFileError, match="no declared input encoding"):
  142         writer.record(record, engine.state, inputs=[odd])
  143     with pytest.raises(RunFileError, match="not the proposals"):
  144         writer.record(record, engine.state, inputs=[])
  145     with pytest.raises(RunFileError, match="not the proposals"):
  146         writer.record(record, engine.state, inputs=[consume("y", "A", 0, amount=1)])
  147     writer.abort()
  148     assert read_run(tmp_path / "w.jsonl").end is None

  153 def test_a_run_closed_or_stopped_before_its_horizon_has_no_end_line(tmp_path: Path):
  154     genesis = WorldState.genesis(balances={"A": 3})
  155     closed = tmp_path / "closed.jsonl"
  156     writer = RunWriter(closed, run_id="c", genesis=genesis, scenario={}, horizon=3)
  157     engine = Engine(genesis)
  158     writer.record(engine.tick([]), engine.state, inputs=[])
  159     with pytest.raises(RunFileError, match="1 of 3"):
  160         writer.close()
  161     stopped = tmp_path / "stopped.jsonl"
  162     engine = Engine(genesis)
  163     with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
  164         with RunWriter(stopped, run_id="s", genesis=genesis, scenario={}, horizon=3) as writer:
  165             writer.record(engine.tick([]), engine.state, inputs=[])
  166             raise RuntimeError("stopped")
  167     for path in (closed, stopped):
  168         run = read_run(path)
  169         assert run.end is None and not run.complete
  170         assert run.last_sealed_tick == 0 and run.first_break_line is None

  173 def test_the_writer_refuses_ticks_out_of_order_or_past_the_horizon(tmp_path: Path):
  174     genesis = WorldState.genesis(balances={"A": 3})
  175     engine = Engine(genesis)
  176     first = engine.tick([])
  177     second = engine.tick([])
  178     writer = RunWriter(tmp_path / "w.jsonl", run_id="w", genesis=genesis, scenario={}, horizon=1)
  179     with pytest.raises(RunFileError, match="expected a record for tick 0"):
  180         writer.record(second, engine.state, inputs=[])
  181     replay = Engine(genesis)
  182     writer.record(replay.tick([]), replay.state, inputs=[])
  183     with pytest.raises(RunFileError, match="horizon"):
  184         writer.record(replay.tick([]), replay.state, inputs=[])
  185     writer.close()
  186     assert first.tick == 0 and read_run(tmp_path / "w.jsonl").complete

  189 # --- damage --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  190 
  191 def damage(path: Path, how: str, target: int) -> int:
  192     """Damage the line of tick `target` and return the 1-based number of the
  193     first line the reader should find broken."""
  194     lines = lines_of(path)
  195     ticks = tick_indexes(lines)
  196     index = ticks[target]
  197     first_broken = index + 1
  198     if how == "edit-content":
  199         payload = json.loads(lines[index])
  200         account = sorted(payload["availability"])[0]
  201         payload["availability"][account] += 1
  202         lines[index] = canonical_bytes(payload) + b"\n"
  203     elif how == "edit-seal-byte":
  204         line = bytearray(lines[index])
  205         at = line.index(b'"seal":"') + len(b'"seal":"')
  206         line[at] = ord("1") if line[at] == ord("0") else ord("0")
  207         lines[index] = bytes(line)
  208     elif how == "delete":
  209         first_broken = ticks[target + 1]   # the next tick line moves up one place
  210         del lines[index]
  211     elif how == "swap":
  212         lines[index], lines[ticks[target + 1]] = lines[ticks[target + 1]], lines[index]
  213     elif how == "cut-mid-line":
  214         lines = lines[:index] + [lines[index][: len(lines[index]) // 2]]
  215     else:
  216         raise AssertionError(how)
  217     path.write_bytes(b"".join(lines))
  218     return first_broken
  219 
  220 
  221 @pytest.mark.parametrize("how", ["edit-content", "edit-seal-byte", "delete", "swap", "cut-mid-line"])
  222 def test_damage_breaks_the_chain_at_its_line_and_nothing_after_it_counts(scenario_file: Path, how: str):
  223     first_broken = damage(scenario_file, how, 12)
  224     run = read_run(scenario_file)
  225     assert not run.complete
  226     assert run.first_break_line == first_broken
  227     assert run.last_sealed_tick == 11
  228 
  229 
  230 def test_an_edited_header_breaks_every_seal(scenario_file: Path):
  231     lines = lines_of(scenario_file)
  232     header = json.loads(lines[0])
  233     header["run_id"] = "renamed"
  234     lines[0] = canonical_bytes(header) + b"\n"
  235     scenario_file.write_bytes(b"".join(lines))
  236     run = read_run(scenario_file)
  237     assert run.first_break_line == 1 and run.last_sealed_tick == -1
  238     assert any("header seal does not verify" in problem for problem in run.problems)
  239 
  240 
  241 def test_the_end_line_must_carry_the_final_seal(scenario_file: Path):
  242     lines = lines_of(scenario_file)
  243     end = json.loads(lines[-1])
  244     end["final_seal"] = "0" * 64
  245     lines[-1] = canonical_bytes(end) + b"\n"
  246     scenario_file.write_bytes(b"".join(lines))
  247     run = read_run(scenario_file)
  248     assert not run.complete and run.first_break_line == len(lines)
  249     assert run.last_sealed_tick == 29

  251 
  252 def test_v3_stream_2_files_stay_readable_without_seal_checks(scenario_file: Path, tmp_path: Path):
  253     """Strip the 1c additions from a sealed file and label it v3.stream.2: the
  254     earlier rules still verify it, and no seal is looked for."""
  255     trail = hashlib.sha256()
  256     out: list[bytes] = []
  257     for raw in lines_of(scenario_file):
  258         payload = json.loads(raw)
  259         if payload["kind"] == "header":
  260             for key in ("seal", "horizon", "code_identity", "config_identity"):
  261                 payload.pop(key)
  262             payload["format"] = "v3.stream.2"
  263         elif payload["kind"] == "tick":
  264             payload.pop("seal")
  265             payload.pop("inputs")
  266             raw = canonical_bytes(payload) + b"\n"
  267             trail.update(raw)
  268             out.append(raw)
  269             continue
  270         elif payload["kind"] == "end":
  271             payload.pop("final_seal")
  272             payload["trail_digest"] = trail.hexdigest()
  273         out.append(canonical_bytes(payload) + b"\n")
  274     old = tmp_path / "old.jsonl"
  275     old.write_bytes(b"".join(out))
  276     run = read_run(old)
  277     assert run.complete and not run.sealed and run.last_sealed_tick is None

===== tests/test_replay.py =====
    1 """Slice 1c: canonical reconstruction and replay against the file
    2 (declaration items 5 and 6).
    3 
    4 Replay re-executes from the header genesis with the recorded inputs (scenario
    5 runs) or with recomputed decisions (world runs) and compares every tick line,
    6 without its seal, byte for byte. A file edited and then re-sealed is exactly
    7 what the seal cannot catch, so that is what replay is tested against.
    8 World runs are the declaration's optional targets.
    9 """
   10 
   11 from __future__ import annotations
   12 
   13 import hashlib
   14 import json
   15 from pathlib import Path
   16 
   17 import pytest
   18 
   19 from kernel import (Effect, Engine, Reservation, Source, WorldState, canonical_bytes, claim, complete, consume,
   20                     digest, reserve, transfer)
   21 from stream.replay import ReplayError, replay_scenario
   22 from stream.run import main as stream_main
   23 from stream.run import run_scenario
   24 from stream.run_file import header_seal, read_run, tick_seal
   25 from stream.scenario import ProposalGenerator, Scenario
   26 from world.config import WorldConfig
   27 from world.overlay import Overlay
   28 from world.replay import replay_world
   29 from world.run import main as world_main
   30 from world.run import run_world
   31 
   32 
   33 def reseal(path: Path) -> None:
   34     """Recompute every seal, the trail and the end line after an edit, as a
   35     forger or a changed engine would: the seals then vouch for the edit."""
   36     out: list[bytes] = []
   37     trail = hashlib.sha256()
   38     seal = None
   39     for raw in path.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True):
   40         payload = json.loads(raw)
   41         if payload["kind"] == "header":
   42             payload["seal"] = header_seal(payload)
   43             seal = payload["seal"]
   44         elif payload["kind"] == "tick":
   45             payload["seal"] = tick_seal(seal, payload)
   46             seal = payload["seal"]
   47             raw = canonical_bytes(payload) + b"\n"
   48             trail.update(raw)
   49             out.append(raw)
   50             continue
   51         elif payload["kind"] == "end":
   52             payload["trail_digest"] = trail.hexdigest()
   53             payload["final_seal"] = seal
   54         out.append(canonical_bytes(payload) + b"\n")
   55     path.write_bytes(b"".join(out))
   56 
   57 
   58 def edit_tick(path: Path, tick: int, change) -> None:
   59     lines = path.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)
   60     for index, raw in enumerate(lines):
   61         payload = json.loads(raw)
   62         if payload["kind"] == "tick" and payload["tick"] == tick:
   63             change(payload)
   64             lines[index] = canonical_bytes(payload) + b"\n"
   65     path.write_bytes(b"".join(lines))
   66     reseal(path)
   67     assert read_run(path).complete, "the resealed edit should verify; only replay can catch it"
   68 
   69 
   70 # --- reconstruction --------------------------------------------------------------
   71 
   72 def scenario_states(ticks: int) -> list[WorldState]:
   73     scenario = Scenario(seed=7)
   74     engine = Engine(scenario.genesis())
   75     generator = ProposalGenerator(scenario)
   76     states = [engine.state]
   77     for _ in range(ticks):
   78         live = {action: held.actor for action, held in engine.state.reservations.items()}
   79         engine.tick(generator.tick(engine.state.tick, live))
   80         states.append(engine.state)
   81     return states
   82 
   83 
   84 def fixture_states() -> list[WorldState]:
   85     """The 1a contention and credit fixtures and a 1b reserve-then-complete
   86     lifecycle, as states, built through the kernel's own operations."""
   87     states: list[WorldState] = []
   88     contention = Engine(WorldState.genesis(balances={"A": 0, "B": 0}, sources={"s": Source(1, frozenset({"A", "B"}))}))
   89     states.append(contention.state)
   90     contention.tick([claim("pA", "A", 0, sources={"s": 1}), claim("pB", "B", 0, sources={"s": 1})])
   91     states.append(contention.state)
   92     credit = Engine(WorldState.genesis(balances={"A": 1, "B": 0}))
   93     credit.tick([transfer("t", "A", 0, to="B", amount=1), consume("c", "B", 0, amount=1)])
   94     states.append(credit.state)
   95     credit.tick([consume("c2", "B", 0, amount=1)])
   96     states.append(credit.state)
   97     holds = Engine(WorldState.genesis(balances={"A": 2, "B": 0}, sources={"s": Source(3, frozenset({"A"}))}))
   98     record = holds.tick([reserve("r", "A", 0, operation="claim", params={"sources": {"s": 2}})])
   99     states.append(holds.state)
  100     action = next(outcome.action_id for outcome in record.outcomes if outcome.action_id)
  101     holds.tick([complete("k", "A", 0, action_id=action)])
  102     states.append(holds.state)
  103     assert any(state.reservations for state in states)
  104     return states
  105 
  106 
  107 @pytest.mark.parametrize("source", ["fixtures", "scenario-200"])
  108 def test_every_state_rebuilds_to_its_own_canonical_bytes(source: str):
  109     states = fixture_states() if source == "fixtures" else scenario_states(200)
  110     assert any(state.reservations for state in states)
  111     for state in states:
  112         rebuilt = WorldState.from_canonical(state.canonical())
  113         assert canonical_bytes(rebuilt.canonical()) == canonical_bytes(state.canonical())
  114         assert rebuilt.digest() == state.digest()
  115         for held in state.reservations.values():
  116             assert Reservation.from_canonical(held.canonical()) == held
  117             for effect in held.effects:
  118                 assert Effect.from_canonical(effect.canonical()) == effect
  119         for pool in state.sources.values():
  120             assert Source.from_canonical(pool.canonical()) == pool
  121     # a stored (JSON) copy rebuilds too, not only the live dicts
  122     last = states[-1]
  123     assert WorldState.from_canonical(json.loads(canonical_bytes(last.canonical()))).digest() == last.digest()
  124 
  125 
  126 @pytest.mark.parametrize("damage", ["negative-balance", "unknown-reserving-actor", "string-balance", "bool-balance",
  127                                     "float-stock", "missing-key", "extra-key", "old-schema", "unsorted-authorised",
  128                                     "repeated-authorised", "list-for-balances", "string-authorised"])
  129 def test_malformed_canonical_states_are_refused(damage: str):
  130     state = next(state for state in scenario_states(60) if state.reservations)
  131     data = json.loads(canonical_bytes(state.canonical()))
  132     actor = sorted(data["balances"])[0]
  133     source = sorted(data["sources"])[0]
  134     if damage == "negative-balance":
  135         data["balances"][actor] = -1
  136     elif damage == "unknown-reserving-actor":
  137         next(iter(data["reservations"].values()))["actor"] = "nobody"
  138     elif damage == "string-balance":
  139         data["balances"][actor] = "1"
  140     elif damage == "bool-balance":
  141         data["balances"][actor] = True
  142     elif damage == "float-stock":
  143         data["sources"][source]["stock"] = 1.0
  144     elif damage == "missing-key":
  145         del data["consumed"]
  146     elif damage == "extra-key":
  147         data["extra"] = 0
  148     elif damage == "old-schema":
  149         data["schema_version"] = "v3.kernel.1a.1"
  150     elif damage == "unsorted-authorised":
  151         data["sources"][source]["authorised"].reverse()
  152     elif damage == "repeated-authorised":
  153         data["sources"][source]["authorised"].append(data["sources"][source]["authorised"][-1])
  154     elif damage == "list-for-balances":
  155         data["balances"] = sorted(data["balances"].items())
  156     elif damage == "string-authorised":
  157         data["sources"][source]["authorised"] = "p1"
  158     with pytest.raises(ValueError):
  159         WorldState.from_canonical(data)
  160 
  161 
  162 def test_every_overlay_of_a_world_run_rebuilds_and_malformed_ones_are_refused(tmp_path: Path):
  163     path = tmp_path / "w.jsonl"
  164     run_world(WorldConfig(seed=7), 60, path)
  165     run = read_run(path)
  166     for stored in [run.header["world"]] + [tick["world"] for tick in run.ticks]:
  167         overlay = Overlay.from_canonical(stored)
  168         assert overlay.canonical() == stored and overlay.digest() == digest(stored)
  169     good = run.ticks[30]["world"]
  170     actor = sorted(good["positions"])[0]
  171     for mutate in (lambda d: d["positions"].__setitem__(actor, [1, 2, 3]),
  172                    lambda d: d["positions"].__setitem__(actor, "ab"),
  173                    lambda d: d["hunger"].__setitem__(actor, -1),
  174                    lambda d: d["died_at"].__setitem__(actor, d["tick"] + 1),
  175                    lambda d: d.pop("yield_at"),
  176                    lambda d: d.__setitem__("homes", [])):
  177         broken = json.loads(json.dumps(good))
  178         mutate(broken)
  179         with pytest.raises(ValueError):
  180             Overlay.from_canonical(broken)
  181 
  182 
  183 def test_scenario_and_world_descriptions_round_trip_exactly():
  184     for scenario in (Scenario(seed=7), Scenario(seed=11, actors=4, sources=3, max_proposals_per_tick=5)):
  185         assert Scenario.from_describe(json.loads(json.dumps(scenario.describe()))) == scenario
  186     for config in (WorldConfig(seed=7), WorldConfig(seed=11, yield_on=False, scoring_on=True, renewal_every=2),
  187                    WorldConfig(seed=23, yield_set=(2, 3), perception_radius=5)):
  188         assert WorldConfig.from_describe(json.loads(json.dumps(config.describe()))) == config
  189 
  190 
  191 @pytest.mark.parametrize("damage", ["name", "version", "extra", "missing", "string-seed"])
  192 def test_a_scenario_description_from_elsewhere_is_refused(damage: str):
  193     described = Scenario(seed=7).describe()
  194     if damage == "name":
  195         described["name"] = "other"
  196     elif damage == "version":
  197         described["version"] = "v3.scenario.contention-and-holds.2"
  198     elif damage == "extra":
  199         described["extra"] = 1
  200     elif damage == "missing":
  201         del described["actors"]
  202     else:
  203         described["seed"] = "7"
  204     with pytest.raises(ValueError):
  205         Scenario.from_describe(described)
  206

  208 @pytest.mark.parametrize("damage", ["generator", "name", "rule-text", "lever-type", "switch", "unhashable-switch"])

  219     elif damage == "switch":
  220         described["yield"] = "maybe"
  221     else:
  222         described["scoring"] = ["on"]

  229 @pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", [7, 11, 23])
  230 def test_scenario_runs_replay_identically(tmp_path: Path, seed: int):
  231     path = tmp_path / f"scenario-{seed}.jsonl"
  232     run_scenario(Scenario(seed=seed), 120, path)
  233     result = replay_scenario(path)
  234     assert result.identical and result.ticks == 120 and result.code_identity_matches
  235 
  236 
  237 def accepted_input(payload: dict) -> dict | None:
  238     accepted = {o["proposal_id"] for o in payload["record"]["outcomes"] if o["accepted"]}
  239     for entry in payload["inputs"]:
  240         if entry["proposal_id"] in accepted and "params" in entry and "amount" in entry["params"]:
  241             return entry
  242     return None
  243 
  244 
  245 def test_a_resealed_altered_input_replays_different_at_exactly_that_tick(tmp_path: Path):
  246     path = tmp_path / "scenario.jsonl"
  247     run_scenario(Scenario(seed=7), 120, path)
  248     target = next(tick["tick"] for tick in read_run(path).ticks if tick["tick"] >= 20 and accepted_input(tick))
  249 
  250     def change(payload: dict) -> None:
  251         accepted_input(payload)["params"]["amount"] += 1
  252 
  253     edit_tick(path, target, change)
  254     result = replay_scenario(path)
  255     assert not result.identical
  256     assert result.divergent_tick == target and result.divergent_field == "record"
  257 
  258 
  259 def test_a_resealed_altered_outcome_replays_different_at_exactly_that_tick(tmp_path: Path):
  260     path = tmp_path / "scenario.jsonl"
  261     run_scenario(Scenario(seed=7), 120, path)
  262     target = next(tick["tick"] for tick in read_run(path).ticks if tick["tick"] >= 30 and accepted_input(tick))
  263 
  264     def change(payload: dict) -> None:
  265         outcome = next(o for o in payload["record"]["outcomes"] if o["accepted"])
  266         outcome["reason"] = "denied_insufficient_balance"
  267         outcome["accepted"] = 0
  268         payload["record_digest"] = digest(payload["record"])
  269 
  270     edit_tick(path, target, change)
  271     result = replay_scenario(path)
  272     assert not result.identical
  273     assert result.divergent_tick == target and result.divergent_field == "record"
  274

  276 def test_replay_refuses_files_it_cannot_vouch_for(tmp_path: Path):
  277     scenario_path = tmp_path / "scenario.jsonl"

  279     world_path = tmp_path / "world.jsonl"
  280     run_world(WorldConfig(seed=7, width=7, height=7, actors=4), 20, world_path)
  281     with pytest.raises(ReplayError, match="world run"):
  282         replay_scenario(world_path)
  283     with pytest.raises(ReplayError, match="not a world run"):
  284         replay_world(scenario_path)
  285     cut = tmp_path / "cut.jsonl"
  286     cut.write_bytes(b"".join(scenario_path.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)[:-3]))
  287     with pytest.raises(ReplayError, match="does not verify"):
  288         replay_scenario(cut)

  291 def test_a_header_whose_genesis_its_configuration_does_not_produce_is_reported(tmp_path: Path):
  292     path = tmp_path / "scenario.jsonl"
  293     run_scenario(Scenario(seed=7), 10, path)
  294     lines = path.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)
  295     header = json.loads(lines[0])
  296     header["scenario"]["starting_balance"] = 4   # the scenario genesis does not depend on the seed
  297     header["config_identity"] = digest(header["scenario"])
  298     lines[0] = canonical_bytes(header) + b"\n"
  299     path.write_bytes(b"".join(lines))
  300     reseal(path)
  301     result = replay_scenario(path)
  302     assert not result.identical and result.divergent_field == "genesis"

===== tests/test_recovery.py =====
    1 """Slice 1c: recovery through the last verified sealed tick (declaration item 7)
    2 and private instance isolation (item 8).
    3 
    4 Recovery keeps only what the seal chain vouches for and continues from the
    5 state the last sealed tick left. The check is the strongest one available:
    6 the recovered file's content lines (header, ticks, end, seals included) must
    7 equal an uninterrupted run's, with only timing lines allowed to differ. World
    8 runs are the declaration's optional targets.
    9 """
   10 
   11 from __future__ import annotations
   12 
   13 import hashlib
   14 import json
   15 from pathlib import Path
   16 
   17 import pytest
   18 
   19 import stream.recover as recover_module
   20 from kernel import canonical_bytes, digest
   21 from stream.recover import RecoveryError, recover_scenario, restore_engine
   22 from stream.run import main as stream_main
   23 from stream.run import run_scenario
   24 from stream.run_file import decode_input, header_seal, read_run, tick_seal
   25 from stream.scenario import Scenario
   26 from world.config import WorldConfig
   27 from world.recover import recover_world
   28 from world.run import main as world_main
   29 from world.run import run_world
   30 
   31 
   32 def content(path: Path) -> list[bytes]:
   33     """Every line except timing lines: what recovery must reproduce byte for byte."""
   34     return [line for line in path.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True) if b'"kind":"timing"' not in line]
   35 
   36 
   37 def index_of_tick(lines: list[bytes], tick: int) -> int:
   38     return next(i for i, line in enumerate(lines) if b'"kind":"tick"' in line and json.loads(line)["tick"] == tick)
   39 
   40 
   41 def cut_after(source: Path, tick: int, dest: Path) -> Path:
   42     """A copy of `source` that stops right after tick `tick`'s line, as if the
   43     writing process died there."""
   44     lines = source.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)
   45     dest.write_bytes(b"".join(lines[: index_of_tick(lines, tick) + 1]))
   46     return dest
   47 
   48 
   49 def reseal(path: Path) -> None:
   50     """Recompute every seal (and any end line) after an edit, as a forger would."""
   51     out: list[bytes] = []
   52     trail = hashlib.sha256()
   53     seal = None
   54     for raw in path.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True):
   55         payload = json.loads(raw)
   56         if payload["kind"] == "header":
   57             payload["seal"] = header_seal(payload)
   58             seal = payload["seal"]
   59         elif payload["kind"] == "tick":
   60             payload["seal"] = tick_seal(seal, payload)
   61             seal = payload["seal"]
   62             raw = canonical_bytes(payload) + b"\n"
   63             trail.update(raw)
   64             out.append(raw)
   65             continue
   66         elif payload["kind"] == "end":
   67             payload["trail_digest"], payload["final_seal"] = trail.hexdigest(), seal
   68         out.append(canonical_bytes(payload) + b"\n")
   69     path.write_bytes(b"".join(out))
   70 
   71 
   72 def declared_cut(path: Path) -> int:
   73     """The declaration's cut rule: the first tick at or after 40 whose state
   74     holds at least one live reservation."""
   75     return next(t["tick"] for t in read_run(path).ticks if t["tick"] >= 40 and t["state"]["reservations"])
   76 
   77 
   78 @pytest.fixture(scope="module")
   79 def uninterrupted(tmp_path_factory) -> Path:
   80     path = tmp_path_factory.mktemp("recovery") / "scenario-seed7.jsonl"
   81     run_scenario(Scenario(seed=7), 120, path)
   82     return path
   83 
   84 
   85 @pytest.fixture(scope="module")
   86 def uninterrupted_world(tmp_path_factory) -> Path:
   87     path = tmp_path_factory.mktemp("recovery-world") / "world-seed7.jsonl"
   88     run_world(WorldConfig(seed=7), 120, path)
   89     return path
   90 
   91 
   92 # --- recovery: scenario runs (essential) --------------------------------------------
   93 
   94 def test_recovery_from_a_cut_with_live_reservations_matches_the_uninterrupted_run(uninterrupted, tmp_path):
   95     cut_tick = declared_cut(uninterrupted)
   96     cut = cut_after(uninterrupted, cut_tick, tmp_path / "cut.jsonl")
   97     assert read_run(cut).last_sealed_tick == cut_tick and not read_run(cut).complete
   98     result = recover_scenario(cut, tmp_path / "recovered.jsonl")
   99     assert result.last_sealed_tick == cut_tick and result.live_holds_at_cut >= 1
  100     assert result.resumed == (cut_tick + 1, 119)
  101     assert content(tmp_path / "recovered.jsonl") == content(uninterrupted)
  102     assert read_run(tmp_path / "recovered.jsonl").complete
  103     assert result.final_seal == read_run(uninterrupted).end["final_seal"]
  104 
  105 
  106 def test_recovery_from_a_line_cut_in_half(uninterrupted, tmp_path):
  107     lines = uninterrupted.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)
  108     index = index_of_tick(lines, 61)
  109     cut = tmp_path / "half.jsonl"
  110     cut.write_bytes(b"".join(lines[:index]) + lines[index][: len(lines[index]) // 2])
  111     result = recover_scenario(cut, tmp_path / "recovered.jsonl")
  112     assert result.last_sealed_tick == 60
  113     assert content(tmp_path / "recovered.jsonl") == content(uninterrupted)
  114 
  115 
  116 def test_recovery_from_a_broken_last_seal_resumes_from_the_tick_before_it(uninterrupted, tmp_path):
  117     cut = cut_after(uninterrupted, 70, tmp_path / "cut.jsonl")
  118     lines = cut.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)
  119     last = json.loads(lines[-1])
  120     last["seal"] = "0" * 64
  121     lines[-1] = canonical_bytes(last) + b"\n"
  122     cut.write_bytes(b"".join(lines))
  123     assert read_run(cut).last_sealed_tick == 69
  124     result = recover_scenario(cut, tmp_path / "recovered.jsonl")
  125     assert result.last_sealed_tick == 69
  126     assert content(tmp_path / "recovered.jsonl") == content(uninterrupted)
  127 
  128 
  129 def test_recovery_never_uses_an_unsealed_line(uninterrupted, tmp_path):
  130     """An edited line and everything after it are dropped, not carried forward."""
  131     lines = uninterrupted.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)
  132     index = index_of_tick(lines, 80)
  133     edited = json.loads(lines[index])
  134     actor = sorted(edited["state"]["balances"])[0]
  135     edited["state"]["balances"][actor] += 5
  136     lines[index] = canonical_bytes(edited) + b"\n"
  137     broken = tmp_path / "broken.jsonl"
  138     broken.write_bytes(b"".join(lines[:-1]))
  139     assert read_run(broken).last_sealed_tick == 79
  140     result = recover_scenario(broken, tmp_path / "recovered.jsonl")
  141     assert result.last_sealed_tick == 79
  142     assert content(tmp_path / "recovered.jsonl") == content(uninterrupted)
  143 
  144 
  145 def test_recovery_from_the_header_alone_starts_at_genesis(uninterrupted, tmp_path):
  146     cut = tmp_path / "header.jsonl"
  147     cut.write_bytes(uninterrupted.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)[0])
  148     result = recover_scenario(cut, tmp_path / "recovered.jsonl")
  149     assert result.last_sealed_tick == -1 and result.resumed == (0, 119)
  150     assert content(tmp_path / "recovered.jsonl") == content(uninterrupted)
  151 
  152 
  153 def test_recovery_of_a_file_cut_only_before_its_end_line(uninterrupted, tmp_path):
  154     cut = cut_after(uninterrupted, 119, tmp_path / "cut.jsonl")
  155     result = recover_scenario(cut, tmp_path / "recovered.jsonl")
  156     assert result.last_sealed_tick == 119 and result.resumed is None
  157     assert content(tmp_path / "recovered.jsonl") == content(uninterrupted)

  160 def test_recovery_refuses_what_it_cannot_trust(uninterrupted, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
  161     with pytest.raises(RecoveryError, match="complete"):
  162         recover_scenario(uninterrupted, tmp_path / "a.jsonl")
  163     cut = cut_after(uninterrupted, 50, tmp_path / "cut.jsonl")
  164     lines = cut.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)
  165     header = json.loads(lines[0])
  166     header["run_id"] = "renamed"
  167     bad_header = tmp_path / "bad-header.jsonl"
  168     bad_header.write_bytes(canonical_bytes(header) + b"\n" + b"".join(lines[1:]))
  169     with pytest.raises(RecoveryError, match="header"):
  170         recover_scenario(bad_header, tmp_path / "b.jsonl")
  171     live = recover_module.code_identity()
  172     monkeypatch.setattr(recover_module, "code_identity", lambda: {**live, "digest": "0" * 64})
  173     with pytest.raises(RecoveryError, match="other code"):
  174         recover_scenario(cut, tmp_path / "c.jsonl")
  175     monkeypatch.undo()
  176     world = tmp_path / "world.jsonl"
  177     run_world(WorldConfig(seed=7, width=7, height=7, actors=4), 30, world)
  178     with pytest.raises(RecoveryError, match="world run"):
  179         recover_scenario(cut_after(world, 10, tmp_path / "world-cut.jsonl"), tmp_path / "d.jsonl")
  180     with pytest.raises(RecoveryError, match="not a world run"):
  181         recover_world(cut, tmp_path / "e.jsonl")
  182     assert not [name for name in "abcde" if (tmp_path / f"{name}.jsonl").exists()]
  183 
  184 
  185 def test_recovery_refuses_when_the_generator_does_not_reproduce_the_sealed_inputs(uninterrupted, tmp_path):
  186     cut = cut_after(uninterrupted, 30, tmp_path / "cut.jsonl")
  187     lines = cut.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)
  188     target = next(t["tick"] for t in read_run(cut).ticks if t["tick"] >= 12 and t["inputs"])
  189     index = index_of_tick(lines, target)
  190     payload = json.loads(lines[index])
  191     payload["inputs"][0]["order"] += 7          # the record still settles the same identities
  192     lines[index] = canonical_bytes(payload) + b"\n"
  193     cut.write_bytes(b"".join(lines))
  194     reseal(cut)
  195     assert read_run(cut).last_sealed_tick == 30, "the resealed prefix verifies; only the generator check can catch it"
  196     with pytest.raises(RecoveryError, match=f"sealed inputs of tick {target}"):
  197         recover_scenario(cut, tmp_path / "recovered.jsonl")
  198     assert not (tmp_path / "recovered.jsonl").exists()
  199

  201 # --- recovery: world runs (optional targets) ---------------------------------------

  203 @pytest.mark.parametrize("production", [True, False])
  204 def test_world_recovery_matches_the_uninterrupted_run(uninterrupted_world, tmp_path, production: bool):
  205     cut_tick = next(t["tick"] for t in read_run(uninterrupted_world).ticks
  206                     if t["tick"] >= 30 and ("production" in t) == production)
  207     cut = cut_after(uninterrupted_world, cut_tick, tmp_path / "cut.jsonl")
  208     result = recover_world(cut, tmp_path / "recovered.jsonl")
  209     assert result.last_sealed_tick == cut_tick and result.resumed == (cut_tick + 1, 119)
  210     assert content(tmp_path / "recovered.jsonl") == content(uninterrupted_world)

  213 # --- isolation --------------------------------------------------------------------
  214 
  215 def test_two_engines_restored_from_one_sealed_tick_share_no_mutable_state(uninterrupted):
  216     tick = declared_cut(uninterrupted)
  217     a, b = restore_engine(uninterrupted, tick), restore_engine(uninterrupted, tick)
  218     assert a is not b and a.state is not b.state and a.state.digest() == b.state.digest()
  219     assert a.state.reservations, "the declared cut holds live reservations"
  220     for name in ("balances", "sources", "reservations"):
  221         assert getattr(a.state, name) is not getattr(b.state, name)
  222     assert all(a.state.sources[s] is not b.state.sources[s] for s in a.state.sources)
  223     assert all(a.state.reservations[r] is not b.state.reservations[r] for r in a.state.reservations)
  224     before = b.state.digest()
  225     following = next(t for t in read_run(uninterrupted).ticks if t["tick"] == tick + 1)
  226     proposals = [decode_input(entry) for entry in following["inputs"]]
  227 
  228     a.tick(proposals)
  229     assert a.state.digest() == following["state_digest"]      # a continues the run exactly
  230     assert b.state.digest() == before                         # b is untouched
  231 
  232     copy = a.state.canonical()
  233     copy["balances"][sorted(copy["balances"])[0]] += 100
  234     copy["reservations"].clear()
  235     copy["sources"][sorted(copy["sources"])[0]]["authorised"].append("intruder")
  236     assert a.state.digest() == following["state_digest"] and b.state.digest() == before
  237     with pytest.raises(TypeError):
  238         a.state.balances["p1"] = 0
  239 
  240     b.tick(proposals)
  241     assert b.state.digest() == a.state.digest()               # same inputs, same result, independently
  242 
  243 
  244 def test_restore_refuses_a_tick_the_file_does_not_vouch_for(uninterrupted, tmp_path):
  245     cut = cut_after(uninterrupted, 20, tmp_path / "cut.jsonl")
  246     assert restore_engine(cut, 20).state.tick == 21
  247     with pytest.raises(RecoveryError):
  248         restore_engine(cut, 21)
  249     assert digest(restore_engine(cut, 0).state.canonical()) == read_run(cut).ticks[0]["state_digest"]
  250 
  251 
  252 # --- commands ------------------------------------------------------------------------
  253 
  254 def test_the_runner_commands_recover(uninterrupted, uninterrupted_world, tmp_path, capsys):
  255     cut = cut_after(uninterrupted, 45, tmp_path / "cut.jsonl")
  256     assert stream_main(["--recover", str(cut), "--out", str(tmp_path / "r.jsonl")]) == 0
  257     out = capsys.readouterr().out
  258     assert "last sealed tick: 45" in out and "file_verifies: yes" in out
  259     assert content(tmp_path / "r.jsonl") == content(uninterrupted)
  260     assert stream_main(["--recover", str(cut)]) == 2
  261     assert stream_main(["--recover", str(cut), "--out", str(tmp_path / "r.jsonl")]) == 2   # never overwrites
  262     world_cut = cut_after(uninterrupted_world, 20, tmp_path / "world-cut.jsonl")
  263     assert world_main(["--recover", str(world_cut), "--out", str(tmp_path / "wr.jsonl")]) == 0
  264     assert content(tmp_path / "wr.jsonl") == content(uninterrupted_world)

===== tests/test_reference_results.py =====
    1 """Slice 1c, declaration item 11: recorded reference results stay byte-identical.
    2 
    3 The recorded 1b fixture output is the before-capture of kernel canonical bytes:
    4 its state and record digests are sha256 over them, so an unchanged output means
    5 unchanged canonical forms. The head-b305783 world decisions fixture is checked
    6 by test_perception.py and test_yield.py. Nothing here is regenerated.
    7 """
    8 
    9 from __future__ import annotations
   10 
   11 import runpy
   12 from pathlib import Path
   13 
   14 ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
   15 
   16 
   17 def normalised(text: str) -> str:
   18     return text.replace("\r\n", "\n").strip()
   19 
   20 
   21 def test_fixture_digests_reproduce_the_recorded_1b_output(capsys):
   22     namespace = runpy.run_path(str(ROOT / "evidence" / "stage-01" / "instrument" / "fixture_digests.py"))
   23     assert namespace["main"]() == 0
   24     produced = capsys.readouterr().out
   25     recorded = (ROOT / "evidence" / "stage-01" / "slice-1b" / "fixtures-stage1b.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
   26     assert normalised(produced) == normalised(recorded)

===== tests/test_dependency_direction.py =====
    1 """A9 - module dependency direction, required by doctrine principle 9.
    2 
    3 State, proposal shape, ordering, resolution, evidence and diagnostics are
    4 separate responsibilities, and the import graph has to say so. In particular
    5 settlement must not be able to see the diagnostics channel at all, which is what
    6 makes optional capture structurally unable to influence a decision.
    7 """
    8 
    9 from __future__ import annotations
   10 
   11 import ast
   12 import pathlib
   13 
   14 KERNEL = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "kernel"
   15 
   16 ALLOWED = {
   17     "version": set(),
   18     "canonical": set(),
   19     "reasons": set(),
   20     "units": set(),
   21     "ordering": set(),
   22     "diagnostics": {"canonical"},
   23     "state": {"version", "canonical", "units"},
   24     "proposals": {"units", "reasons", "state"},
   25     "outcomes": {"version", "canonical", "reasons", "state"},
   26     "settlement": {"units", "reasons", "ordering", "state", "proposals", "outcomes", "canonical"},
   27     "engine": {"version", "canonical", "state", "proposals", "outcomes", "settlement", "diagnostics"},
   28 }
   29 
   30 
   31 def kernel_imports(path: pathlib.Path) -> set[str]:
   32     tree = ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), filename=str(path))
   33     found: set[str] = set()
   34     for node in ast.walk(tree):
   35         if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) and node.module and node.module.startswith("kernel"):
   36             parts = node.module.split(".")
   37             if len(parts) > 1:
   38                 found.add(parts[1])
   39             else:
   40                 found.update(alias.name for alias in node.names)
   41         elif isinstance(node, ast.Import):
   42             for alias in node.names:
   43                 parts = alias.name.split(".")
   44                 if parts[0] == "kernel" and len(parts) > 1:
   45                     found.add(parts[1])
   46     return found
   47 
   48 
   49 def module_paths() -> dict[str, pathlib.Path]:
   50     return {
   51         path.stem: path
   52         for path in sorted(KERNEL.glob("*.py"))
   53         if path.stem != "__init__"
   54     }
   55 
   56 
   57 def test_every_kernel_module_has_a_declared_layer():
   58     assert set(module_paths()) == set(ALLOWED)
   59 
   60 
   61 def test_no_module_imports_above_its_layer():
   62     offences = []
   63     for name, path in module_paths().items():
   64         forbidden = kernel_imports(path) - ALLOWED[name] - {name}
   65         if forbidden:
   66             offences.append((name, sorted(forbidden)))
   67     assert offences == []
   68 
   69 
   70 def test_settlement_cannot_see_the_diagnostics_channel():
   71     assert "diagnostics" not in kernel_imports(module_paths()["settlement"])
   72 
   73 
   74 def test_nothing_inside_the_kernel_imports_the_engine():
   75     importers = [
   76         name
   77         for name, path in module_paths().items()
   78         if name != "engine" and "engine" in kernel_imports(path)
   79     ]
   80     assert importers == []
   81 
   82 
   83 def test_state_does_not_import_resolution_or_evidence():
   84     seen = kernel_imports(module_paths()["state"])
   85     assert not seen & {"settlement", "engine", "outcomes", "proposals"}
   86 
   87 
   88 def test_the_declared_layering_is_acyclic():
   89     visiting: set[str] = set()
   90     done: set[str] = set()
   91 
   92     def walk(name: str, trail: tuple[str, ...]) -> None:
   93         if name in done:
   94             return
   95         assert name not in visiting, f"import cycle: {' -> '.join(trail + (name,))}"
   96         visiting.add(name)
   97         for dependency in sorted(ALLOWED[name]):
   98             walk(dependency, trail + (name,))
   99         visiting.discard(name)
  100         done.add(name)
  101 
  102     for module in sorted(ALLOWED):
  103         walk(module, ())
  104 
  105 
  106 # Presentation track (OD-012): viewer/ reads saved run files and imports nothing
  107 # from the kernel, the world, or the stream (which imports the kernel).
  108 
  109 VIEWER = KERNEL.parent / "viewer"
  110 VIEWER_FORBIDDEN = {"kernel", "world", "stream"}
  111 
  112 
  113 def top_level_imports(path: pathlib.Path) -> set[str]:
  114     tree = ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), filename=str(path))
  115     found: set[str] = set()
  116     for node in ast.walk(tree):
  117         if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) and node.module:
  118             found.add(node.module.split(".")[0])
  119         elif isinstance(node, ast.Import):
  120             found.update(alias.name.split(".")[0] for alias in node.names)
  121     return found
  122 
  123 
  124 def test_viewer_package_exists_and_imports_nothing_from_kernel_world_or_stream():
  125     modules = sorted(VIEWER.glob("*.py"))
  126     assert [m.name for m in modules if m.name != "__init__.py"], "viewer/ has no modules"
  127     offences = [(m.name, sorted(top_level_imports(m) & VIEWER_FORBIDDEN)) for m in modules
  128                 if top_level_imports(m) & VIEWER_FORBIDDEN]
  129     assert offences == []
  130 
  131 
  132 # Slice 1c (declaration item 9): the simulation packages depend downward only,
  133 # kernel <- stream <- world, and none imports the viewer, the tooling or the
  134 # tests. Replay and recovery of world runs therefore live in world/.
  135 
  136 LAYERS_ABOVE = {
  137     "kernel": {"stream", "world", "viewer", "automation", "tests"},
  138     "stream": {"world", "viewer", "automation", "tests"},
  139     "world": {"viewer", "automation", "tests"},
  140 }
  141 
  142 
  143 def test_simulation_packages_import_only_downward():
  144     offences = []
  145     for package, forbidden in LAYERS_ABOVE.items():
  146         modules = sorted((KERNEL.parent / package).glob("*.py"))
  147         assert modules, package
  148         for module in modules:
  149             bad = top_level_imports(module) & forbidden
  150             if bad:
  151                 offences.append((f"{package}/{module.name}", sorted(bad)))
  152     assert offences == []

===== tests/test_stream.py =====
    1 """Record stream and viewer (slice 1c as re-scoped by OD-009).
    2 
    3 These check that the stream is a faithful, verifiable, reproducible write-down
    4 of what the engine did, and that the viewer renders from it alone. They are
    5 not evidence of any world property; the scenario is exploration input.
    6 """
    7 
    8 from __future__ import annotations
    9 
   10 import ast
   11 import json
   12 import re
   13 from pathlib import Path
   14 
   15 import pytest
   16 
   17 from kernel import Engine, Source, WorldState, consume
   18 from stream.run import run_scenario
   19 from stream.run_file import STREAM_FORMAT, RunFileError, RunWriter, read_run
   20 from stream.scenario import ProposalGenerator, Scenario
   21 from stream.viewer import render_html, render_text
   22 
   23 REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
   24 
   25 
   26 def content_lines(path: Path) -> list[bytes]:
   27     return [line for line in path.read_bytes().splitlines() if b'"kind":"timing"' not in line]
   28 
   29 
   30 def test_two_runs_of_one_seed_are_byte_identical_apart_from_timing(tmp_path: Path):
   31     scenario = Scenario(seed=11, actors=4, sources=2)
   32     first = run_scenario(scenario, 60, tmp_path / "a.jsonl")
   33     second = run_scenario(scenario, 60, tmp_path / "b.jsonl")
   34     assert first["trail_digest"] == second["trail_digest"]
   35     assert first["final_state_digest"] == second["final_state_digest"]
   36     assert content_lines(tmp_path / "a.jsonl") == content_lines(tmp_path / "b.jsonl")
   37     assert Scenario(seed=12, actors=4, sources=2) != scenario
   38     third = run_scenario(Scenario(seed=12, actors=4, sources=2), 60, tmp_path / "c.jsonl")
   39     assert third["trail_digest"] != first["trail_digest"]
   40 
   41 
   42 def test_run_file_verifies_and_reads_back_what_the_engine_committed(tmp_path: Path):
   43     scenario = Scenario(seed=3, actors=3, sources=1)
   44     path = tmp_path / "run.jsonl"
   45     result = run_scenario(scenario, 25, path)
   46     run = read_run(path)
   47     assert run.complete and run.problems == ()
   48     assert run.header["format"] == STREAM_FORMAT and run.header["scenario"]["seed"] == 3
   49     assert len(run.ticks) == 25 and run.end["ticks"] == 25
   50     assert run.trail_digest == result["trail_digest"] == run.end["trail_digest"]
   51     assert run.ticks[-1]["state_digest"] == result["final_state_digest"]
   52     assert [t["tick"] for t in run.ticks] == list(range(25))
   53     assert set(run.timings) == set(range(25))
   54     # the engine's own availability map is stored, never recomputed here
   55     for entry in run.ticks:
   56         state = WorldState.genesis(**{k: entry["state"][k] for k in ("tick", "balances", "consumed")},
   57                                    sources={k: Source(v["stock"], tuple(v["authorised"])) for k, v in entry["state"]["sources"].items()})
   58         if not entry["state"]["reservations"]:
   59             assert entry["availability"] == state.availability()
   60 
   61 
   62 def test_writer_refuses_to_overwrite_and_checks_the_record_state_pair(tmp_path: Path):
   63     path = tmp_path / "run.jsonl"
   64     genesis = WorldState.genesis(balances={"A": 1})
   65     with RunWriter(path, run_id="x", genesis=genesis, scenario={}, horizon=1) as writer:
   66         engine = Engine(genesis)
   67         proposal = consume("p", "A", 0, amount=1)
   68         record = engine.tick([proposal])
   69         with pytest.raises(RunFileError):
   70             writer.record(record, genesis, inputs=[proposal])  # wrong state for this record
   71         writer.record(record, engine.state, inputs=[proposal])
   72     with pytest.raises(FileExistsError):
   73         RunWriter(path, run_id="x", genesis=genesis, scenario={}, horizon=1)
   74     assert read_run(path).complete
   75 
   76 
   77 @pytest.mark.parametrize("damage", ["flip-balance", "drop-tick", "truncate", "reorder"])
   78 def test_tampered_or_cut_run_files_do_not_verify(tmp_path: Path, damage: str):
   79     path = tmp_path / "run.jsonl"
   80     run_scenario(Scenario(seed=5, actors=3, sources=1), 12, path)
   81     lines = path.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)
   82     ticks = [k for k, line in enumerate(lines) if b'"kind":"tick"' in line]
   83     if damage == "flip-balance":
   84         target = ticks[4]
   85         payload = json.loads(lines[target])
   86         actor = sorted(payload["state"]["balances"])[0]
   87         payload["state"]["balances"][actor] += 1
   88         lines[target] = (json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True) + "\n").encode()
   89     elif damage == "drop-tick":
   90         del lines[ticks[3]]
   91     elif damage == "truncate":
   92         lines = lines[: ticks[6]]
   93     else:
   94         lines[ticks[2]], lines[ticks[3]] = lines[ticks[3]], lines[ticks[2]]
   95     path.write_bytes(b"".join(lines))
   96     run = read_run(path)
   97     assert not run.complete and run.problems, damage
   98 
   99 
  100 def test_viewer_renders_every_tick_from_the_file_with_no_external_resources(tmp_path: Path):
  101     path = tmp_path / "run.jsonl"
  102     run_scenario(Scenario(seed=8, actors=4, sources=2), 30, path)
  103     run = read_run(path)
  104     page = render_html(run)
  105     assert page.count("<script") == 2 and "src=" not in page and "href=" not in page
  106     assert not re.search(r"https?://", page)
  107     embedded = json.loads(re.search(r'<script id="run-data" type="application/json">(.*?)</script>', page, re.S).group(1))
  108     assert len(embedded["ticks"]) == 30 and embedded["header"]["genesis_digest"] == run.header["genesis_digest"]
  109     assert "file verifies" in page and "not evidence" in page
  110     text = render_text(run, 10)
  111     assert "after tick 10" in text and "actors:" in text and "outcomes" in text
  112     for actor in run.ticks[10]["state"]["balances"]:
  113         assert actor in text

  115 
  116 def test_stream_is_downstream_of_the_kernel():
  117     """The kernel must not know the stream exists; the stream reads kernel objects only."""
  118     def imports(module: Path) -> set[str]:
  119         tree = ast.parse(module.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
  120         names: set[str] = set()
  121         for node in ast.walk(tree):
  122             if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
  123                 names.add((node.module or "").split(".")[0])
  124             elif isinstance(node, ast.Import):
  125                 names.update(alias.name.split(".")[0] for alias in node.names)
  126         return names
  127 
  128     for module in (REPO_ROOT / "kernel").glob("*.py"):
  129         assert "stream" not in imports(module), module.name
  130     for module in (REPO_ROOT / "stream").glob("*.py"):
  131         assert not imports(module) & {"automation", "tests"}, module.name
  132 
  133 
  134 def test_scenario_is_reproducible_and_produces_lifecycle_input():
  135     scenario = Scenario(seed=21, actors=5, sources=2)
  136     a, b = ProposalGenerator(scenario), ProposalGenerator(scenario)
  137     live = {"action:" + "a" * 64: "p1", "action:" + "b" * 64: "p2"}
  138     kinds = set()
  139     for tick in range(40):
  140         first, second = a.tick(tick, live), b.tick(tick, live)
  141         assert first == second
  142         kinds.update(p.operation for p in first)
  143     assert {"claim", "transfer", "consume", "reserve", "complete", "cancel"} <= kinds

===== tests/test_world.py =====
    1 """Stage 2 first step: position, movement, one renewable source, hunger.
    2 
    3 What is checked here is the part OD-009 keeps for every leg: seeded runs
    4 reproduce byte for byte, the run file verifies and detects tampering with the
    5 new blocks, the kernel is still the only place food moves, the world rules do
    6 what their declarations say, and the viewer renders from the file alone.
    7 """
    8 
    9 from __future__ import annotations
   10 
   11 import ast
   12 import json
   13 import re
   14 from pathlib import Path
   15 
   16 import pytest
   17 
   18 from kernel import Engine, claim
   19 from stream.run_file import RunFileError, RunWriter, apply_production, read_run
   20 from world.config import FOOD_SOURCE, WorldConfig, genesis, homes_for
   21 from world.decide import CLAIM, EAT, GO, HOME, REST, WAIT, candidates, decide, step_toward
   22 from world.observe import Observation, observe
   23 from world.overlay import Overlay
   24 from world.process import advance
   25 from world.run import run_world
   26 from world.viewer import render_html, render_text
   27 
   28 ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
   29 
   30 
   31 def small(**levers) -> WorldConfig:
   32     base = dict(seed=3, width=7, height=7, actors=4)
   33     base.update(levers)
   34     return WorldConfig(**base)
   35 
   36 
   37 def obs(**fields) -> Observation:
   38     base = dict(actor="p01", tick=0, alive=True, position=(0, 0), home=(0, 0), hunger=0, food=0, source=(3, 3), source_food=4)
   39     base.update(fields)
   40     return Observation(**base)
   41 
   42 
   43 # --- genesis and configuration -------------------------------------------------
   44 
   45 def test_genesis_is_seeded_saved_and_identity_neutral():
   46     a, b = genesis(small()), genesis(small())
   47     assert a[0].digest() == b[0].digest() and a[1].digest() == b[1].digest()
   48     assert genesis(small(seed=4))[1].digest() != a[1].digest()
   49     homes = homes_for(small())
   50     assert len(set(homes.values())) == len(homes) and small().source_position not in homes.values()
   51     ledger, overlay = a
   52     assert overlay.positions == overlay.homes and all(h == 0 for h in overlay.hunger.values())
   53     assert set(overlay.yield_at) == set(overlay.homes) and all(v >= 1 for v in overlay.yield_at.values())
   54     assert ledger.sources[FOOD_SOURCE].authorised == frozenset(ledger.roster)
   55 
   56 
   57 @pytest.mark.parametrize("bad", [dict(actors=49), dict(source_cap=1, source_stock=4), dict(death_at=5, emergency_at=10),
   58                                  dict(claim_amount=0), dict(renewal_every=0), dict(satiation=0),
   59                                  dict(perception_radius=-1), dict(yield_set=()), dict(yield_set=(0, 1))])
   60 def test_configuration_rejects_incoherent_levers(bad):
   61     with pytest.raises(ValueError):
   62         small(**bad)

   66 
   67 def test_step_toward_moves_one_cell_along_the_longer_axis_x_on_ties():
   68     assert step_toward((0, 0), (3, 1)) == (1, 0)
   69     assert step_toward((0, 0), (1, 3)) == (0, 1)
   70     assert step_toward((0, 0), (2, 2)) == (1, 0)
   71     assert step_toward((5, 5), (2, 5)) == (4, 5)
   72     assert step_toward((2, 2), (2, 2)) == (2, 2)
   73 
   74 
   75 def test_selection_rule_follows_the_declared_priority():
   76     cfg = small()
   77     d = decide(obs(hunger=cfg.hungry_at, food=2, position=(3, 3)), cfg)
   78     assert d.kind == EAT and d.amount == 1 and CLAIM in d.candidates
   79     d = decide(obs(hunger=cfg.hungry_at, food=0, position=(3, 3), source_food=1), cfg)
   80     assert d.kind == CLAIM and d.amount == 1                      # never asks for more than it saw
   81     d = decide(obs(hunger=cfg.emergency_at, food=0, position=(3, 3), source_food=0), cfg)
   82     assert d.kind == WAIT and d.reason.startswith("emergency")
   83     d = decide(obs(hunger=cfg.hungry_at, food=0, position=(0, 0)), cfg)
   84     assert d.kind == GO and d.step == (1, 0)
   85     d = decide(obs(hunger=cfg.hungry_at - 1, food=0, position=(1, 0), home=(0, 0)), cfg)
   86     assert d.kind == HOME and d.step == (0, 0)
   87     d = decide(obs(hunger=0, food=0), cfg)
   88     assert d.kind == REST and d.step is None
   89     assert candidates(obs(alive=False, hunger=99), cfg) == () and decide(obs(alive=False), cfg).kind == "dead"
   90

   92 # --- processes -----------------------------------------------------------------
   93 
   94 def test_hunger_rises_every_tick_only_settled_eating_lowers_it_and_death_freezes():
   95     cfg = small(hunger_rate=2, satiation=6, death_at=8, emergency_at=7, hungry_at=3, starting_food=1)
   96     ledger, overlay = genesis(cfg)
   97     engine = Engine(ledger)
   98     # nobody proposes: hunger rises by the rate for everyone
   99     processed = advance(overlay, {}, engine.tick([]), engine.state, cfg)
  100     assert all(h == 2 for h in processed.overlay.hunger.values()) and not processed.died
  101     # p01 eats one unit: hunger falls by satiation, floored at zero; the rest keep rising
  102     overlay = processed.overlay
  103     d = decide(obs(actor="p01", hunger=4, food=1, position=overlay.positions["p01"], home=overlay.homes["p01"]), cfg)
  104     assert d.kind == EAT
  105     from world.run import proposals_for
  106     record = engine.tick(proposals_for({"p01": d}, 1))
  107     assert record.outcomes[0].accepted
  108     processed = advance(overlay, {"p01": d}, record, engine.state, cfg)
  109     assert processed.overlay.hunger["p01"] == 0 and processed.overlay.hunger["p02"] == 4 and processed.eaten == {"p01": 1}
  110     # a rejected eat (no food) counts for nothing: hunger keeps rising
  111     overlay = processed.overlay
  112     record = engine.tick(proposals_for({"p01": d}, 2))
  113     assert not record.outcomes[0].accepted
  114     processed = advance(overlay, {"p01": d}, record, engine.state, cfg)
  115     assert processed.overlay.hunger["p01"] == 2 and processed.overlay.hunger["p02"] == 6
  116     # one more tick kills p02..p04 at death_at; their hunger and positions freeze and the ledger keeps their units
  117     overlay = processed.overlay
  118     processed = advance(overlay, {}, engine.tick([]), engine.state, cfg)
  119     assert set(processed.died) == {"p02", "p03", "p04"} and processed.overlay.hunger["p02"] == 8
  120     frozen = processed.overlay
  121     later = advance(frozen, {}, engine.tick([]), engine.state, cfg)
  122     assert later.overlay.hunger["p02"] == 8 and later.overlay.died_at["p02"] == 4 and later.overlay.living == ("p01",)
  123     assert engine.state.balances["p02"] == 1 and engine.state.total() == later.ledger.total() - sum(e["amount"] for e in later.production)
  124 
  125 
  126 def test_renewal_is_the_only_production_and_respects_cap_and_cadence():
  127     cfg = small(source_stock=4, source_cap=5, renewal_every=2, renewal_amount=3)
  128     ledger, overlay = genesis(cfg)
  129     engine = Engine(ledger)
  130     first = advance(overlay, {}, engine.tick([]), engine.state, cfg)      # tick 1: no renewal
  131     assert first.production == () and first.ledger.digest() == engine.state.digest()
  132     engine = Engine(first.ledger)
  133     second = advance(first.overlay, {}, engine.tick([]), engine.state, cfg)  # tick 2: capped to +1
  134     assert second.production == ({"source": FOOD_SOURCE, "amount": 1},)
  135     assert second.ledger.sources[FOOD_SOURCE].stock == 5 and second.ledger.total() == engine.state.total() + 1
  136     assert second.ledger.balances == engine.state.balances and second.ledger.tick == engine.state.tick
  137     engine = Engine(second.ledger)
  138     third = advance(second.overlay, {}, engine.tick([]), engine.state, cfg)
  139     engine = Engine(third.ledger)
  140     fourth = advance(third.overlay, {}, engine.tick([]), engine.state, cfg)
  141     assert third.production == () and fourth.production == ()                # at cap: nothing produced
  142 
  143 
  144 def test_movement_takes_one_cell_per_tick_and_contention_is_settled_by_the_kernel(tmp_path: Path):
  145     cfg = small(seed=11, starting_food=0, source_stock=1, renewal_amount=0, hungry_at=0, death_at=60, emergency_at=30)
  146     run_world(cfg, 12, tmp_path / "w.jsonl")
  147     run = read_run(tmp_path / "w.jsonl")
  148     assert run.complete
  149     previous = run.header["world"]["positions"]
  150     for tick in run.ticks:
  151         for actor, pos in tick["world"]["positions"].items():
  152             assert abs(pos[0] - previous[actor][0]) + abs(pos[1] - previous[actor][1]) <= 1
  153         previous = tick["world"]["positions"]
  154     accepted = [o for t in run.ticks for o in t["record"]["outcomes"] if o["operation"] == "claim" and o["accepted"]]
  155     denied = [o for t in run.ticks for o in t["record"]["outcomes"] if o["operation"] == "claim" and not o["accepted"]]
  156     assert len(accepted) == 1 and all(o["reason"] == "denied_insufficient_source" for o in denied)
  157     assert run.ticks[-1]["state"]["sources"][FOOD_SOURCE]["stock"] == 0
  158     # once the source is empty, a hungry person standing on it waits rather than claiming, and hunger keeps rising
  159     waited = [(a, d) for t in run.ticks for a, d in t["decisions"].items() if d["kind"] == "wait"]
  160     assert waited and all(d["reason"].endswith("source empty") for _, d in waited)
  161     assert all(h > 0 for h in run.ticks[-1]["world"]["hunger"].values())
  162

  164 # --- run files -----------------------------------------------------------------
  165 
  166 def test_two_runs_of_one_seed_are_byte_identical_apart_from_timing(tmp_path: Path):
  167     cfg = small()
  168     a = run_world(cfg, 80, tmp_path / "a.jsonl")
  169     b = run_world(cfg, 80, tmp_path / "b.jsonl")
  170     assert a.trail_digest == b.trail_digest and a.final_overlay_digest == b.final_overlay_digest
  171     strip = lambda p: [l for l in p.read_bytes().splitlines() if b'"kind":"timing"' not in l]
  172     assert strip(tmp_path / "a.jsonl") == strip(tmp_path / "b.jsonl")
  173     assert run_world(small(seed=5), 80, tmp_path / "c.jsonl").trail_digest != a.trail_digest
  174

  176 def test_world_run_file_verifies_and_chains_through_production(tmp_path: Path):
  177     cfg = small(renewal_every=1, renewal_amount=1, source_cap=9)
  178     run_world(cfg, 30, tmp_path / "w.jsonl")
  179     run = read_run(tmp_path / "w.jsonl")
  180     assert run.complete and run.has_world and run.header["world_digest"]
  181     produced = [t for t in run.ticks if "production" in t]
  182     assert produced, "a renewal every tick must show up"
  183     for earlier, later in zip(run.ticks, run.ticks[1:]):
  184         expected = earlier["produced_state_digest"] if "production" in earlier else earlier["state_digest"]
  185         assert later["record"]["prior_state_digest"] == expected
  186     # every decision belongs to a person who was alive when the tick started
  187     living_before = set(run.header["world"]["positions"]) - set(run.header["world"]["died_at"])
  188     for tick in run.ticks:
  189         assert set(tick["decisions"]) == living_before
  190         living_before = set(tick["world"]["positions"]) - set(tick["world"]["died_at"])

  193 def test_writer_refuses_production_that_did_not_happen(tmp_path: Path):
  194     cfg = small()
  195     ledger, overlay = genesis(cfg)
  196     engine = Engine(ledger)
  197     record = engine.tick([])
  198     writer = RunWriter(tmp_path / "w.jsonl", run_id="x", genesis=ledger, scenario=cfg.describe(),
  199                        world=overlay.canonical(), horizon=1)
  200     with pytest.raises(RunFileError):
  201         writer.record(record, engine.state, inputs=[], production=[{"source": FOOD_SOURCE, "amount": 1}],
  202                       produced_state=engine.state)
  203     with pytest.raises(RunFileError):
  204         writer.record(record, engine.state, inputs=[], production=[{"source": FOOD_SOURCE, "amount": 1}])
  205     writer.record(record, engine.state, inputs=[])
  206     writer.close()
  207     with pytest.raises(RunFileError):
  208         apply_production(engine.state.canonical(), [{"source": "nowhere", "amount": 1}])
  209     with pytest.raises(RunFileError):
  210         apply_production(engine.state.canonical(), [{"source": FOOD_SOURCE, "amount": 0}])

  213 @pytest.mark.parametrize("damage", ["world", "production", "produced_digest", "decision"])
  214 def test_tampering_with_world_blocks_does_not_verify(tmp_path: Path, damage: str):
  215     cfg = small(renewal_every=1, renewal_amount=1, source_cap=9)
  216     run_world(cfg, 10, tmp_path / "w.jsonl")
  217     lines = (tmp_path / "w.jsonl").read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)
  218     index = next(i for i, l in enumerate(lines) if b'"kind":"tick"' in l and b'"production"' in l)
  219     tick = json.loads(lines[index])
  220     if damage == "world":
  221         actor = sorted(tick["world"]["hunger"])[0]
  222         tick["world"]["hunger"][actor] += 1
  223     elif damage == "production":
  224         tick["production"][0]["amount"] += 1
  225     elif damage == "produced_digest":
  226         tick["produced_state_digest"] = "0" * 64
  227     else:
  228         actor = sorted(tick["decisions"])[0]
  229         tick["decisions"][actor]["kind"] = "eat"
  230     from kernel import canonical_bytes
  231     lines[index] = canonical_bytes(tick) + b"\n"
  232     (tmp_path / "w.jsonl").write_bytes(b"".join(lines))
  233     run = read_run(tmp_path / "w.jsonl")
  234     assert not run.complete and any("trail" in p for p in run.problems)
  235     if damage == "world":
  236         assert any("world digest" in p for p in run.problems)
  237     if damage in {"production", "produced_digest"}:
  238         assert any("production rule" in p for p in run.problems)

  241 # --- ownership -----------------------------------------------------------------
  242 
  243 def test_world_is_downstream_of_the_kernel_and_the_kernel_knows_nothing_of_it():
  244     def imports(module: Path) -> set[str]:
  245         names: set[str] = set()
  246         for node in ast.walk(ast.parse(module.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))):
  247             if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) and node.module:
  248                 names.add(node.module.split(".")[0])
  249             elif isinstance(node, ast.Import):
  250                 names.update(alias.name.split(".")[0] for alias in node.names)
  251         return names
  252     for module in (ROOT / "kernel").glob("*.py"):
  253         assert not imports(module) & {"world", "stream"}, module.name
  254     for module in (ROOT / "stream").glob("*.py"):
  255         assert "world" not in imports(module), module.name
  256     for module in (ROOT / "world").glob("*.py"):
  257         assert not imports(module) & {"automation", "tests"}, module.name
  258     # only run.py talks to the engine and the run file; observe/decide/process are pure rules
  259     for name in ("observe", "decide", "process", "overlay", "config"):
  260         assert "stream" not in imports(ROOT / "world" / f"{name}.py"), name
  261     observe_tree = ast.parse((ROOT / "world" / "observe.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
  262     observe_modules = {
  263         node.module for node in ast.walk(observe_tree)
  264         if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) and node.module
  265     } | {
  266         alias.name for node in ast.walk(observe_tree) if isinstance(node, ast.Import) for alias in node.names
  267     }
  268     assert "stream" not in {name.split(".")[0] for name in observe_modules}
  269     assert "world.run" not in observe_modules and "run" not in observe_modules

  272 def test_food_only_moves_through_the_kernel(tmp_path: Path):
  273     """Every change in a person's balance or the source stock between two
  274     stored states is explained by accepted kernel effects or recorded production."""
  275     cfg = small(renewal_every=2)
  276     run_world(cfg, 60, tmp_path / "w.jsonl")
  277     run = read_run(tmp_path / "w.jsonl")
  278     before = run.header["genesis"]
  279     for tick in run.ticks:
  280         delta: dict[str, int] = {}
  281         for o in tick["record"]["outcomes"]:
  282             if o["accepted"]:
  283                 for e in o["effects"]:
  284                     delta[e["account"]] = delta.get(e["account"], 0) + e["delta"]
  285         after = tick["state"]
  286         for actor in after["balances"]:
  287             assert after["balances"][actor] - before["balances"][actor] == delta.get(f"actor:{actor}", 0)
  288         assert after["sources"][FOOD_SOURCE]["stock"] - before["sources"][FOOD_SOURCE]["stock"] == delta.get(f"source:{FOOD_SOURCE}", 0)
  289         before = apply_production(after, tick.get("production", []))
