  294 def test_viewer_renders_from_the_file_with_no_external_resources(tmp_path: Path):
  295     cfg = small()
  296     run_world(cfg, 25, tmp_path / "w.jsonl")
  297     run = read_run(tmp_path / "w.jsonl")
  298     page = render_html(run)
  299     assert "file verifies" in page and 'id="run-data"' in page
  300     assert not re.search(r'(src|href)\s*=\s*["\']?(https?:)?//', page)
  301     assert "<script src" not in page and "@import" not in page and "<link" not in page
  302     embedded = json.loads(re.search(r'<script id="run-data" type="application/json">(.*?)</script>', page, re.S).group(1))
  303     assert len(embedded["ticks"]) == 25 and embedded["header"]["world"] == run.header["world"]
  304     text = render_text(run, 25)
  305     assert "source S at" in text
  306     assert "view 25/25" in text
  307     with pytest.raises(ValueError):
  308         render_text(run, 26)

===== tests/test_world_view.py =====
    1 """Presentation track (OD-012): the world view is generated from a saved run
    2 file alone, embeds that file verbatim with its digest, references nothing
    3 outside itself, refuses shapes it does not recognise, and stays inside a
    4 declared size and per-frame cost on a synthetic 50-person, 2,000-tick input.
    5 
    6 The synthetic run below is a test input in the run-file shape. It is written
    7 to a temporary directory, never under `runs/`, and is not a world execution.
    8 """
    9 
   10 from __future__ import annotations
   11 
   12 import hashlib
   13 import json
   14 import pathlib
   15 import random
   16 import re
   17 import shutil
   18 import subprocess
   19 import sys
   20 
   21 import pytest
   22 
   23 from viewer import world_view
   24 from viewer.world_view import WorldViewError, load_run, render_html
   25 
   26 ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
   27 PAGE_SIZE_BOUND = 64 * 1024 * 1024  # declared in the OD-012 card
   28 FRAME_MS_BOUND = 4.0  # mean interpolation step on the 50 x 2,000 fixture, headless, excluding drawing
   29 
   30 EXTERNAL_REFERENCE = re.compile(r"https?://|\bsrc=|\bhref=|url\(|@import|\bfetch\(|\bimport\(", re.IGNORECASE)
   31 
   32 
   33 def _line(payload: dict) -> str:
   34     return json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
   35 
   36 
   37 def write_synthetic_run(path: pathlib.Path, *, people: int, width: int, height: int, ticks: int, seed: int) -> None:
   38     """A run file in the recognised shape, with people who walk, eat, claim,
   39     yield and sometimes die. Digests are not written: this view never checks
   40     them, and the stream reader is the instrument that does."""
   41     rng = random.Random(seed)
   42     ids = [f"p{i + 1:02d}" for i in range(people)]
   43     src = [width // 2, height // 2]
   44     homes = {p: [rng.randrange(width), rng.randrange(height)] for p in ids}
   45     positions = {p: list(homes[p]) for p in ids}
   46     hunger = {p: 0 for p in ids}
   47     balances = {p: 1 for p in ids}
   48     yield_at = {p: rng.choice([1, 2, 3]) for p in ids}
   49     died_at: dict[str, int] = {}
   50     stock = 4
   51     scenario = {"name": "synthetic-50", "seed": seed, "width": width, "height": height, "actors": people,
   52                 "source": "food", "source_position": src, "source_cap": 8, "source_stock": 4,
   53                 "hungry_at": 5, "emergency_at": 10, "death_at": 16, "perception_radius": 3, "yield": "on",
   54                 "scoring": "on"}
   55     genesis = {"tick": 0, "balances": dict(balances), "consumed": 0, "reservations": {},
   56                "schema_version": "v3.kernel.1b.1",
   57                "sources": {"food": {"authorised": list(ids), "stock": stock}}}
   58 
   59     def world() -> dict:
   60         return {"tick": 0, "positions": {p: list(v) for p, v in positions.items()}, "homes": homes,
   61                 "hunger": dict(hunger), "died_at": dict(died_at), "yield_at": yield_at}
   62 
   63     lines = [_line({"kind": "header", "format": "v3.stream.2", "run_id": f"synthetic-50-seed{seed}-ticks{ticks}",
   64                     "engine_version": "0.2.0-stage1b", "schema_version": "v3.kernel.1b.1", "scenario": scenario,
   65                     "genesis": genesis, "genesis_digest": "0" * 64, "world": world(), "world_digest": "0" * 64})]
   66     consumed = 0
   67     for t in range(ticks):
   68         decisions, outcomes, observations = {}, [], {}
   69         roster = ids[t % people:] + ids[:t % people]
   70         living = [p for p in ids if p not in died_at]
   71         for p in living:
   72             x, y = positions[p]
   73             others = [{"id": q, "at": positions[q], "food": balances[q]} for q in living
   74                       if q != p and max(abs(positions[q][0] - x), abs(positions[q][1] - y)) <= 3]
   75             obs = {"others": others}
   76             if max(abs(src[0] - x), abs(src[1] - y)) <= 3:
   77                 obs["source_food"] = stock
   78             observations[p] = obs
   79             h = hunger[p]
   80             if h >= 5 and balances[p] > 0:
   81                 decisions[p] = {"kind": "eat", "amount": 1, "reason": f"hungry, holding {balances[p]}", "candidates": ["eat", "go"],
   82                                 "scores": {"eat": [2, 0], "go": [0, 2 * (h - 5) + 1]}}
   83             elif h >= 5 and [x, y] == src:
   84                 decisions[p] = {"kind": "claim", "amount": 2, "reason": "hungry, at source", "candidates": ["claim"], "scores": {"claim": [1, 0]}}
   85             elif h >= 5 and h < 10 and len(others) >= yield_at[p] and rng.random() < 0.15:
   86                 decisions[p] = {"kind": "yield", "reason": f"hungry, saw {len(others)} near, yield_at {yield_at[p]}",
   87                                 "candidates": ["go", "yield"], "scores": {"go": [0, 2 * (h - 5) + 1], "yield": [0, 2 * (len(others) - yield_at[p] + 1)]}}
   88             elif h >= 5:
   89                 decisions[p] = {"kind": "go", "reason": "hungry, walking to source", "candidates": ["go"], "scores": {"go": [0, 2 * (h - 5) + 1]}}
   90             elif [x, y] == homes[p]:
   91                 decisions[p] = {"kind": "rest", "reason": "fed, at home", "candidates": ["rest"], "scores": {"rest": [0, 0]}}
   92             else:
   93                 decisions[p] = {"kind": "home", "reason": "fed, walking home", "candidates": ["home"], "scores": {"home": [0, 0]}}
   94         # settle in roster order: eats always succeed, claims while stock lasts
   95         for p in roster:
   96             d = decisions.get(p)
   97             if not d:
   98                 continue
   99             if d["kind"] == "eat":
  100                 balances[p] -= 1
  101                 consumed += 1
  102                 outcomes.append({"proposal_id": f"t{t}-{p}", "actor": p, "operation": "consume", "accepted": 1, "reason": "accepted",
  103                                  "effects": [{"account": f"actor:{p}", "delta": -1}, {"account": "sink:consumed", "delta": 1}], "sequence": 0})
  104                 hunger[p] = max(0, hunger[p] - 6)
  105             elif d["kind"] == "claim":
  106                 if stock >= 2:
  107                     stock -= 2
  108                     balances[p] += 2
  109                     outcomes.append({"proposal_id": f"t{t}-{p}", "actor": p, "operation": "claim", "accepted": 1, "reason": "accepted",
  110                                      "effects": [{"account": f"actor:{p}", "delta": 2}, {"account": "source:food", "delta": -2}], "sequence": 0})
  111                 else:
  112                     outcomes.append({"proposal_id": f"t{t}-{p}", "actor": p, "operation": "claim", "accepted": 0,
  113                                      "reason": "denied_insufficient_source", "effects": [], "sequence": 0})
  114         # movement, hunger, death
  115         for p in living:
  116             d = decisions[p]
  117             target = src if d["kind"] in ("go",) else homes[p] if d["kind"] == "home" else None
  118             if target is not None:
  119                 x, y = positions[p]
  120                 if abs(target[0] - x) >= abs(target[1] - y) and target[0] != x:
  121                     x += 1 if target[0] > x else -1
  122                 elif target[1] != y:
  123                     y += 1 if target[1] > y else -1
  124                 positions[p] = [x, y]
  125             if d["kind"] != "eat":
  126                 hunger[p] += 1
  127             if hunger[p] >= 16:
  128                 died_at[p] = t + 1
  129         state = {"tick": t + 1, "balances": dict(balances), "consumed": consumed, "reservations": {},
  130                  "schema_version": "v3.kernel.1b.1", "sources": {"food": {"authorised": list(ids), "stock": stock}}}
  131         availability = {f"actor:{p}": balances[p] for p in ids}
  132         availability["source:food"] = stock
  133         availability["sink:consumed"] = consumed
  134         tick = {"kind": "tick", "tick": t, "record": {"tick": t, "prior_state_digest": "0" * 64, "next_state_digest": "0" * 64,
  135                                                        "rotated_roster": roster, "outcomes": outcomes},
  136                 "record_digest": "0" * 64, "state": state, "state_digest": "0" * 64, "availability": availability,
  137                 "world": {**world(), "tick": t + 1}, "world_digest": "0" * 64, "decisions": decisions, "observations": observations}
  138         if t % 3 == 2 and stock < 8:
  139             grant = min(2, 8 - stock)
  140             stock += grant
  141             tick["production"] = [{"source": "food", "amount": grant}]
  142             tick["produced_state_digest"] = "0" * 64
  143         lines.append(_line(tick))
  144         lines.append(_line({"kind": "timing", "tick": t, "elapsed_ns": 100000 + rng.randrange(50000)}))
  145     lines.append(_line({"kind": "end", "ticks": ticks, "trail_digest": "0" * 64}))
  146     path.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
  147

  163 def embedded_text(page: str) -> str:
  164     match = re.search(r'<script id="run-jsonl" type="application/json">(.*?)</script>', page, re.DOTALL)
  165     assert match, "page has no embedded run data"
  166     return json.loads(match.group(1))

  169 def test_embedded_run_data_is_the_file_verbatim_and_carries_its_digest(small_run):
  170     run = load_run(small_run)
  171     page = render_html(run)
  172     file_digest = hashlib.sha256(small_run.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
  173     assert run.sha256 == file_digest
  174     assert f'<meta name="source-sha256" content="{file_digest}">' in page
  175     assert hashlib.sha256(embedded_text(page).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() == file_digest

  178 def test_page_references_nothing_outside_itself(small_run):
  179     page = render_html(load_run(small_run))
  180     assert not EXTERNAL_REFERENCE.search(page), EXTERNAL_REFERENCE.search(page).group(0)
  181     assert page.count("<script") == 3  # run data, core, ui: all inline

  184 def test_generator_refuses_unrecognised_headers(tmp_path, small_run):
  185     lines = small_run.read_text(encoding="utf-8").split("\n")
  186     header = json.loads(lines[0])
  187 
  188     def variant(name: str, mutate) -> pathlib.Path:
  189         h = json.loads(json.dumps(header))
  190         mutate(h)
  191         p = tmp_path / f"{name}.jsonl"
  192         p.write_text("\n".join([_line(h), *lines[1:]]), encoding="utf-8")
  193         return p
  194 
  195     cases = {
  196         "format": lambda h: h.__setitem__("format", "v3.stream.9"),
  197         "schema": lambda h: h.__setitem__("schema_version", "v4.kernel.0"),
  198         "no-world": lambda h: h.pop("world"),
  199         "no-homes": lambda h: h["world"].pop("homes"),
  200         "no-thresholds": lambda h: h["scenario"].pop("emergency_at"),
  201         "no-source": lambda h: h["genesis"]["sources"].pop("food"),
  202         "not-header": lambda h: h.__setitem__("kind", "tick"),
  203     }
  204     for name, mutate in cases.items():
  205         with pytest.raises(WorldViewError) as info:
  206             load_run(variant(name, mutate))
  207         assert "unrecognised" in str(info.value), name
  208     cut = tmp_path / "cut.jsonl"
  209     cut.write_text("\n".join(lines[:-2]) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
  210     with pytest.raises(WorldViewError, match="no end record"):
  211         load_run(cut)
  212     assert world_view.main([str(cut)]) == 2

  218     assert out.is_file()

  221     assert embedded_text(out.read_bytes().decode("utf-8")) == small_run.read_bytes().decode("utf-8")

  247 NODE_HARNESS = r"""
  248 const core = require(process.argv[2]);
  249 const text = require('fs').readFileSync(process.argv[3], 'utf8');
  250 const parsed = core.parseLines(text);
  251 const m = core.buildModel(parsed.header, parsed.ticks);
  252 const out = core.newFrame(m.P);
  253 // exactness: at progress 0 the frame is the recorded state
  254 for (const t of [0, 1, 777, m.n]) { core.frame(m, t, 0, out); for (let i = 0; i < m.P; i++) {
  255   if (Math.abs(out.x[i] - out.x[i]) > 0) throw new Error('nan');
  256   const k = t * m.P + i; if (Math.abs((out.x[i] - m.ox[k]) - m.px[k]) > 1e-6 || Math.abs((out.y[i] - m.oy[k]) - m.py[k]) > 1e-6) throw new Error('frame at progress 0 is not the recorded state'); } }
  257 // timing: 600 frames spread over the run at 10 t/s, 60 fps
  258 const frames = 600; const t0 = process.hrtime.bigint();
  259 for (let f = 0; f < frames; f++) { const pos = (f / frames) * m.n; core.frame(m, Math.min(m.n - 1, Math.floor(pos)), pos - Math.floor(pos), out); }
  260 const ms = Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - t0) / 1e6 / frames;
  261 console.log(JSON.stringify({ people: m.P, ticks: m.n, frame_ms_mean: ms }));
  262 """
  263 
  264 
  265 def test_frame_step_is_exact_at_progress_zero_and_cheap_on_the_large_fixture(large_run, tmp_path):
  266     node = shutil.which("node")
  267     if node is None:
  268         pytest.skip("node is not installed; headless frame timing not run")
  269     core = tmp_path / "core.js"
  270     core.write_text(world_view.JS_CORE, encoding="utf-8")
  271     harness = tmp_path / "harness.js"
  272     harness.write_text(NODE_HARNESS, encoding="utf-8")
  273     subprocess.run([node, "--check", str(core)], check=True)
  274     result = subprocess.run([node, str(harness), str(core), str(large_run)], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
  275     report = json.loads(result.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1])
  276     assert report["people"] == 50 and report["ticks"] == 2000
  277     assert report["frame_ms_mean"] < FRAME_MS_BOUND, report
  278     (large_run.parent / "frame.txt").write_text(json.dumps(report) + "\n")
  279 
  280 
  281 def test_page_scripts_parse_under_node(small_run, tmp_path):
  282     node = shutil.which("node")
  283     if node is None:
  284         pytest.skip("node is not installed; JavaScript syntax check not run")
  285     page = render_html(load_run(small_run))
  286     scripts = re.findall(r"<script>(.*?)</script>", page, re.DOTALL)
  287     assert len(scripts) == 2
  288     for i, body in enumerate(scripts):
  289         js = tmp_path / f"script{i}.js"
  290         js.write_text(body, encoding="utf-8")
  291         subprocess.run([node, "--check", str(js)], check=True)
  292

  294 def test_a_sealed_world_run_opens_in_the_world_view(tmp_path):
  295     """Slice 1c: v3.stream.3 files open; the view reads what it always read."""
  296     from world.config import WorldConfig
  297     from world.run import run_world
  298 
  299     path = tmp_path / "sealed.jsonl"
  300     run_world(WorldConfig(seed=7), 20, path)
  301     loaded = load_run(path)
  302     assert loaded.header["format"] == "v3.stream.3" and loaded.tick_count == 20
  303     assert "seal" in loaded.header and render_html(loaded)

   54 What the probes establish beyond the packet:
   55 
   56 - Namespace collisions: actors named `x`, `actor:x` and `sink:consumed`, plus a
   57   source named `x`, coexist without cross-crediting; a transfer to the literal
   58   sink address or to an unknown actor is denied with nothing debited.
   59 - Atomicity with three sources where only the last is short: no leg commits.
   60 - Availability boundary across a transfer chain A→B→C in one tick, at all six
   61   rotations and both collection orders: the forwarded credit is always denied
   62   and succeeds at the next tick.
   63 - Duplicate identities across different actors deny both sharers while an
   64   unrelated proposal from one of them still settles.
   65 - R5: sparse caller orders (7, 10^30) and dense orders (0, 1) produce the same
   66   record identity; output sequences are dense.
   67 - 300 random shuffles of a nine-proposal tick (including an unknown actor and
   68   a cross-actor duplicate identity) with shuffled balance and source maps: one
   69   record digest, one state digest.
   70 - R1: a hostile diagnostics sink that re-enters `tick` is refused inside the
   71   guard, counted once, and the outer tick commits exactly once; a proposal
   72   generator that re-enters raises and leaves state untouched; the guard is
   73   released and the engine is usable afterwards.
   74 - Observation: views, state mappings, source authorised sets and records refuse
   75   mutation; editing a `canonical()` copy leaves the record digest unchanged; a
   76   view taken before two ticks still reports the earlier balances.
   77 - R4: canonical integer text equals `str()` for 500 random values up to 10^40
   78   and the nine-digit chunk boundaries; a 5,001-digit quantity serialises with
   79   the expected byte layout; booleans, floats, non-string keys and foreign
   80   objects are refused; a claim and consume of 10^5000 units conserve the total.
   81 - Fuzz: 250 ticks of random proposals (bad amounts, unknown actors, duplicate
   82   orders and identities, bogus operations) run twice with different collection
   83   orders: conserved total, no negative holding, exactly one reason per
   84   proposal, every accepted transaction zero-sum, no account debited beyond its
   85   tick-start holding within a tick, identical digest trail across both runs.
   86 - An empty roster denies everything and still advances the tick.

   40 ## Design read (settlement.py, state.py)
   41 
   42 - Holds encumber tick-start stock without moving it: `WorldState.availability()`
   43   = balance/stock minus all holds; `__post_init__` rejects any state where a
   44   hold exceeds its account, so a held unit is unspendable by ordinary
   45   proposals by construction, not by bookkeeping. Conserved total counts held
   46   units once.
   47 - Phases: cancel (0) → complete (1) → new work (2), then rotated rank, then
   48   dense sequence. Cancellation of an action always precedes its completion
   49   regardless of the owner's declared order (roadmap step 3). `available` is
   50   captured before any release, so cancelled or completed stock is not free
   51   until the next tick (roadmap: released reservations spendable at next tick
   52   start).
   53 - Completion settles exactly the frozen effects against its own hold, never
   54   against free availability; it is exactly-once because the action is removed
   55   from the tick-local reservation map on the first accepted close and later
   56   requests see `denied_unknown_action`. Same-tick completion of a new hold is
   57   impossible because validation reads tick-start reservations only.
   58 - Action IDs are `action:` + digest(tick, actor, dense sequence): deterministic,
   59   unique per tick, never reused across ticks; owner checked before any close.
   60 - Rails retained; `_commit` additionally converts reservation-state
   61   `ValueError`s into `IntegrityError`.
   62 
   63 ## Reviewer probes (9 passed on attempt 2)
   64 
   65 [test_review_probes_1b.py](test_review_probes_1b.py),
   66 [probes-attempt-2.txt](probes-attempt-2.txt). Attempt 1
   67 ([probes-attempt-1.txt](probes-attempt-1.txt)) failed only the fuzz's own
   68 coverage floor: nothing produces units in this slice, so a four-unit economy
   69 drained and later ticks were all denials; the economy was deepened, no kernel
   70 behaviour changed. Established:
   71 
   72 - An actor's declared order cannot put its spend ahead of its own completion;
   73   held units stay unspendable; phase precedes sequence in the record.
   74 - A hold survives 24 ticks of other actors' activity with the owner's free
   75   balance at zero, then completes.
   76 - Two actors reserving the last source unit at ticks 0–3, both orders: exactly
   77   one hold, decided by rotation.
   78 - A cross-actor cancel alongside the attacker's own valid completion:
   79   unauthorised and accepted respectively; the victim's hold survives.
   80 - Nested `reserve` and reserved terminal operations leave no hold; extra keys
   81   in the inner plan are tolerated exactly as 1a's expanders tolerate them.
   82 - Held units count once in `total()` and `availability()`; two holds
   83   overcommitting one account are rejected at state construction.
   84 - A reserved transfer credits the recipient only at completion, and that
   85   credit is spendable only the tick after.
   86 - Action IDs are stable across engines and change with tick, actor and dense
   87   sequence (a preceding proposal by the same actor shifts them).
   88 - Fuzz, 300 ticks × 2 runs with different collection orders, four actors, two
   89   sources, random reserve/complete/cancel/claim/transfer/consume with bad
   90   amounts, ghosts, duplicates and bogus action IDs: conserved total; no
   91   negative availability; one reason per submission; each accepted close
   92   matches its frozen plan and happens exactly once ever; phase-2 debits never
   93   exceed tick-start free stock; unclosed holds persist unchanged; identical
   94   digest trails across both runs (>60 holds created, >40 closed, >20 completed).
   95

  109 ## Findings
  110 
  111 **F1 — Blocking before commit. The working tree fails the repository's own evidence verification.**
  112 `automation/verify_evidence.py --root .` returns ERROR with three
  113 `manifest_hash_mismatch` errors for `kernel/proposals.py`, `settlement.py`
  114 and `version.py` against `repair-1/FILE_MANIFEST.json` (contract
  115 `stage-01-repair-1`, role historical), and `preflight.py` lists the same three
  116 inconsistencies ([verify-evidence-on-1b-tree.txt](verify-evidence-on-1b-tree.txt),
  117 [preflight-on-1b-tree.txt](preflight-on-1b-tree.txt)). Three tooling tests
  118 fail (`tests/test_verify_evidence.py`, 247 passed / 3 failed with the stale
  119 file set aside). The orchestrator treats these inconsistencies as blockers.
  120 Cause: a historical packet's file manifest is compared against the live tree,
  121 so any later kernel change breaks it; 1b is such a change. Options, owner's
  122 choice: (a) make the verifier compare a historical manifest against the
  123 contract's `recorded_revision` blobs (`git show rev:path`) rather than the
  124 working tree, or (b) add a Stage 1b contract and manifest and re-scope
  125 `stage-01-repair-1`'s manifest check. (a) fixes the class; (b) fixes the
  126 instance. Either way the three tests must pass on the committed tree.
  127 
  128 **F2 — Blocking for any bare `pytest` in this checkout. Stale orchestrator kills the test runner.**
  129 The untracked, pre-freeze `automation/orchestrator.py` (reviewed NEEDS FIXES,
  130 F4) probes lock liveness with `os.kill(pid, 0)`, which on Windows terminates
  131 the target process. Its own untracked test writes the pytest PID into the lock
  132 file, so `pytest` with default collection dies silently at 23%
  133 ([bare-pytest-snapshot.txt](bare-pytest-snapshot.txt),
  134 [legacy-test_orchestrator-alone.txt](legacy-test_orchestrator-alone.txt)).
  135 Consequences: the 1a mutation instrument cannot run in this checkout; the
  136 registered `py -3 -m pytest` gate would be killed. The repaired orchestrator
  137 lives on `codex/orchestrator-phase4-repair` (+ repair 2). Recommendation: an
  138 owner direction to remove these two stale files from the main checkout (or
  139 land the repaired branch), independent of any further orchestrator work.
  140 
  141 **F3 — Mutation instrument is stale for 1b (recommended, not blocking).**
  142 With F2's files and the verifier test set aside, the 1a instrument ran three
  143 mutations (rotation 9, credit-spendable 5, authority 7 tests noticed, all more
  144 than in 1a) and then stopped: the `order-by-arrival` anchor no longer exists in
  145 the phased sort ([mutations/mutation-output.txt](mutations/mutation-output.txt)).
  146 The instrument needs its anchors updated and, per doctrine 7, reservation-rule
  147 mutations added (completion checked against free stock, release spendable
  148 same tick, close without removing the hold, owner check off, phase order off).
  149 
  150 **Observations (non-blocking)**
  151 1. `ROADMAP.md`'s status banner was amended under a two-word direction. The
  152    amendment is scope-only and consistent with OD-008; the owner should
  153    confirm it is intended as part of the same commit.
  154 2. This checkout's register jumps OD-004 → OD-008; OD-005–007 exist on other
  155    branches / a published revision `810ab25` not present locally. Reconcile
  156    the register when branches merge.
  157 3. `WorldView.reservations` exposes every actor's frozen plan. Declared as the
  158    whole-boundary view of slice 1a; Stage 2 must bound it (doctrine 6).
  159 4. All state digests changed with the schema, including states with no

  162 5. The 1a review's proposed record entry (2026-09-21) is not yet in RECORD.md;
  163    the 1b card now sits above the retained 1a record.

  187 `work/stage1b-review-snapshot2-20260921` (with `.git`; stale files and the
  188 verifier test moved to `_review_set_aside/` for the mutation run).

===== kernel/engine.py =====
    1 """The tick loop: freeze the boundary, settle, commit, publish.
    2 
    3 The engine hands settlement a frozen boundary, replaces its state with the one
    4 settlement produced, and then publishes a detached diagnostics payload. A
    5 nonblocking guard spans collection through publication: callbacks cannot start
    6 another tick on this engine while that boundary is still being processed.
    7 """
    8 
    9 from __future__ import annotations
   10 
   11 from collections.abc import Iterable
   12 from threading import Lock
   13 
   14 from kernel.diagnostics import DiagnosticsSink
   15 from kernel.outcomes import TickRecord
   16 from kernel.proposals import Proposal
   17 from kernel.settlement import settle
   18 from kernel.state import WorldState, WorldView
   19 
   20 
   21 class Engine:
   22     def __init__(self, state: WorldState, diagnostics: DiagnosticsSink | None = None) -> None:
   23         if not isinstance(state, WorldState):
   24             raise TypeError(f"an engine needs a WorldState, got {type(state).__name__}")
   25         self._state = state
   26         self._diagnostics = diagnostics
   27         self._diagnostics_failures = 0
   28         self._tick_lock = Lock()
   29 
   30     @property
   31     def state(self) -> WorldState:
   32         """The current tick-start state. Immutable, and replaced rather than edited."""
   33         return self._state
   34 
   35     @property
   36     def diagnostics_failures(self) -> int:
   37         """How often the optional sink raised. Counted rather than hidden."""
   38         return self._diagnostics_failures
   39 
   40     def view_for(self, actor_id: str) -> WorldView:
   41         return self._state.view_for(actor_id)
   42 
   43     def tick(self, proposals: Iterable[Proposal] = ()) -> TickRecord:
   44         if not self._tick_lock.acquire(blocking=False):
   45             raise RuntimeError("a tick is already in progress on this engine")
   46         try:
   47             boundary = self._state
   48             settlement = settle(boundary, proposals)
   49             record = TickRecord(
   50                 tick=boundary.tick,
   51                 prior_state_digest=boundary.digest(),
   52                 next_state_digest=settlement.next_state.digest(),
   53                 rotated_roster=settlement.rotated_roster,
   54                 outcomes=settlement.outcomes,
   55             )
   56             self._state = settlement.next_state
   57             self._publish(record)
   58             return record
   59         finally:
   60             self._tick_lock.release()
   61 
   62     def _publish(self, record: TickRecord) -> None:
   63         sink = self._diagnostics
   64         if sink is None:
   65             return
   66         try:
   67             sink.record("tick", record.canonical())
   68         except Exception:
   69             # A diagnostics failure must not change canonical history, and must
   70             # not be silent either.
   71             self._diagnostics_failures += 1

===== kernel/proposals.py =====
    1 """Proposals, the operations they name, and the balanced effects they expand to.
    2 
    3 A transaction is a list of effects whose signed amounts sum to zero. An
    4 operation is a named constructor for such a list. Expansion is pure shape: it
    5 knows what an operation means, not whether the world can afford it or whether
    6 the proposer is allowed to ask. Existence, authority, availability and
    7 commitment all belong to settlement.
    8 
    9 `claim` accepts several sources because a single-debit transaction has no second
   10 leg to leave behind, and so cannot demonstrate all-or-nothing settlement.
   11 """
   12 
   13 from __future__ import annotations
   14 
   15 from collections.abc import Mapping
   16 from dataclasses import dataclass
   17 from types import MappingProxyType
   18 from typing import Any, Callable
   19 
   20 from kernel import reasons
   21 from kernel.state import SINK_ACCOUNT, Effect, actor_account, source_account
   22 from kernel.units import is_integer, is_valid_transaction_amount
   23 
   24 OP_CLAIM = "claim"
   25 OP_TRANSFER = "transfer"
   26 OP_CONSUME = "consume"
   27 OP_RESERVE = "reserve"
   28 OP_COMPLETE = "complete"
   29 OP_CANCEL = "cancel"
   30 
   31 
   32 def _freeze(value: Any) -> Any:
   33     """Copy `value` into an immutable form, so a caller cannot edit it afterwards."""
   34     if isinstance(value, Mapping):
   35         try:
   36             keys = sorted(value)
   37         except TypeError:
   38             keys = list(value)
   39         return MappingProxyType({key: _freeze(value[key]) for key in keys})
   40     if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
   41         return tuple(_freeze(item) for item in value)
   42     return value
   43 
   44 
   45 @dataclass(frozen=True)
   46 class Proposal:
   47     """One actor's request to run one transaction at one tick.
   48 
   49     `order` expresses the actor's intended ordering, not an assigned sequence.
   50     Settlement validates unique orders and assigns dense actor-local sequences
   51     from them, independently of the order proposals happen to be collected.
   52     """
   53 
   54     proposal_id: str
   55     actor: str
   56     order: int
   57     operation: str
   58     params: Mapping[str, Any]
   59 
   60     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
   61         if not isinstance(self.proposal_id, str) or not self.proposal_id:
   62             raise ValueError("a proposal needs a non-empty identity")
   63         if not isinstance(self.actor, str) or not self.actor:
   64             raise ValueError("a proposal needs a non-empty actor identity")
   65         if not is_integer(self.order) or self.order < 0:
   66             raise ValueError("an actor-local order must be an integer of zero or more")
   67         if not isinstance(self.operation, str) or not self.operation:
   68             raise ValueError("a proposal needs a non-empty operation name")
   69         if not isinstance(self.params, Mapping):
   70             raise ValueError("proposal parameters must be a mapping")
   71         for key in self.params:
   72             if not isinstance(key, str):
   73                 raise ValueError("proposal parameter names must be strings")
   74         object.__setattr__(self, "params", _freeze(self.params))
   75 
   76 
   77 def _ordered(effects: list[Effect]) -> tuple[Effect, ...]:
   78     """Effects are recorded by account, not by the order the operation built them."""
   79     return tuple(sorted(effects, key=lambda effect: effect.account))
   80 
   81 
   82 def _amount(params: Mapping[str, Any], name: str) -> int:
   83     if name not in params:
   84         raise reasons.Rejected(reasons.DENIED_MALFORMED_PARAMS)
   85     amount = params[name]
   86     if not is_integer(amount):
   87         raise reasons.Rejected(reasons.DENIED_NON_INTEGER_AMOUNT)
   88     if not is_valid_transaction_amount(amount):
   89         raise reasons.Rejected(reasons.DENIED_NON_POSITIVE_AMOUNT)
   90     return amount
   91 
   92 
   93 def expand_claim(proposal: Proposal) -> tuple[Effect, ...]:
   94     wanted = proposal.params.get("sources")
   95     if not isinstance(wanted, Mapping) or not wanted:
   96         raise reasons.Rejected(reasons.DENIED_MALFORMED_PARAMS)
   97     if any(not isinstance(source_id, str) or not source_id for source_id in wanted):
   98         raise reasons.Rejected(reasons.DENIED_MALFORMED_PARAMS)
   99 
  100     taken = 0
  101     effects: list[Effect] = []
  102     for source_id in sorted(wanted):
  103         amount = _amount(wanted, source_id)
  104         taken += amount
  105         effects.append(Effect(source_account(source_id), -amount))
  106     effects.append(Effect(actor_account(proposal.actor), taken))
  107     return _ordered(effects)
  108 
  109 
  110 def expand_transfer(proposal: Proposal) -> tuple[Effect, ...]:
  111     target = proposal.params.get("to")
  112     if not isinstance(target, str) or not target:
  113         raise reasons.Rejected(reasons.DENIED_MALFORMED_PARAMS)
  114     amount = _amount(proposal.params, "amount")
  115     if target == proposal.actor:
  116         raise reasons.Rejected(reasons.DENIED_SELF_TRANSFER)
  117     return _ordered(
  118         [
  119             Effect(actor_account(proposal.actor), -amount),
  120             Effect(actor_account(target), amount),
  121         ]
  122     )
  123 
  124 
  125 def expand_consume(proposal: Proposal) -> tuple[Effect, ...]:
  126     amount = _amount(proposal.params, "amount")
  127     return _ordered(
  128         [
  129             Effect(actor_account(proposal.actor), -amount),
  130             Effect(SINK_ACCOUNT, amount),
  131         ]
  132     )
  133 
  134 
  135 EXPANDERS: dict[str, Callable[[Proposal], tuple[Effect, ...]]] = {
  136     OP_CLAIM: expand_claim,
  137     OP_TRANSFER: expand_transfer,
  138     OP_CONSUME: expand_consume,
  139 }
  140 
  141 
  142 def expand(proposal: Proposal) -> tuple[Effect, ...]:
  143     """The effects an operation means, or `Rejected` carrying the reason it does not."""
  144     expander = EXPANDERS.get(proposal.operation)
  145     if expander is None:
  146         raise reasons.Rejected(reasons.DENIED_UNKNOWN_OPERATION)
  147     return tuple(expander(proposal))
  148 
  149 
  150 def claim(proposal_id: str, actor: str, order: int, *, sources: Mapping[str, int]) -> Proposal:
  151     """Take the named amounts from the named shared sources, as one transaction."""
  152     return Proposal(proposal_id, actor, order, OP_CLAIM, {"sources": dict(sources)})
  153 
  154 
  155 def transfer(proposal_id: str, actor: str, order: int, *, to: str, amount: int) -> Proposal:
  156     """Give `amount` of the proposer's own holding to another actor."""
  157     return Proposal(proposal_id, actor, order, OP_TRANSFER, {"to": to, "amount": amount})
  158 
  159 
  160 def consume(proposal_id: str, actor: str, order: int, *, amount: int) -> Proposal:
  161     """Spend `amount` of the proposer's own holding into the consumption sink."""
  162     return Proposal(proposal_id, actor, order, OP_CONSUME, {"amount": amount})
  163 
  164 
  165 def reserve(proposal_id: str, actor: str, order: int, *, operation: str, params: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Proposal:
  166     """Hold a claim, transfer or consumption plan for a later tick."""
  167     return Proposal(proposal_id, actor, order, OP_RESERVE, {"operation": operation, "params": params})
  168 
  169 
  170 def complete(proposal_id: str, actor: str, order: int, *, action_id: str) -> Proposal:
  171     """Commit the owner's previously reserved plan exactly once."""
  172     return Proposal(proposal_id, actor, order, OP_COMPLETE, {"action_id": action_id})
  173 
  174 
  175 def cancel(proposal_id: str, actor: str, order: int, *, action_id: str) -> Proposal:
  176     """Cancel the owner's reserved plan; its stock is free next tick."""
  177     return Proposal(proposal_id, actor, order, OP_CANCEL, {"action_id": action_id})
  178 
  179 
  180 def reserved_plan(proposal: Proposal) -> Proposal:
  181     """Validate the wrapper before using the existing operation expanders."""
  182     operation = proposal.params.get("operation")
  183     params = proposal.params.get("params")
  184     if set(proposal.params) != {"operation", "params"} or not isinstance(params, Mapping):
  185         raise reasons.Rejected(reasons.DENIED_MALFORMED_PARAMS)
  186     if not isinstance(operation, str) or operation not in (OP_CLAIM, OP_TRANSFER, OP_CONSUME):
  187         raise reasons.Rejected(reasons.DENIED_UNKNOWN_OPERATION)
  188     if any(not isinstance(key, str) for key in params):
  189         raise reasons.Rejected(reasons.DENIED_MALFORMED_PARAMS)
  190     return Proposal(proposal.proposal_id, proposal.actor, proposal.order, operation, params)
  191 
  192 
  193 def requested_action(proposal: Proposal) -> str:
  194     action_id = proposal.params.get("action_id")
  195     if set(proposal.params) != {"action_id"} or not isinstance(action_id, str) or not action_id:
  196         raise reasons.Rejected(reasons.DENIED_MALFORMED_PARAMS)
  197     return action_id

===== kernel/outcomes.py =====
    1 """Immutable records of what a tick decided.
    2 
    3 Every submitted proposal leaves the tick with exactly one explicit reason and,
    4 when accepted, the effects that were actually committed. Nothing disappears from
    5 the record.
    6 
    7 This is not yet the sealed evidence stream: slice 1c specifies and writes that,
    8 including immutable copies of the inputs and alternatives a decision used. What
    9 exists here is the in-memory record the settlement produced, which slice 1c will
   10 serialise rather than reconstruct.
   11 """
   12 
   13 from __future__ import annotations
   14 
   15 from typing import Any
   16 
   17 from dataclasses import dataclass
   18 
   19 from kernel import reasons
   20 from kernel.canonical import digest as canonical_digest
   21 from kernel.state import Effect, Reservation
   22 from kernel.version import ENGINE_VERSION, SCHEMA_VERSION
   23 
   24 
   25 @dataclass(frozen=True)
   26 class ProposalOutcome:
   27     proposal_id: str
   28     actor: str
   29     sequence: int
   30     operation: str
   31     reason: str
   32     effects: tuple[Effect, ...] = ()
   33     action_id: str | None = None
   34     reservation: Reservation | None = None
   35 
   36     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
   37         if self.reason not in reasons.ALL:
   38             raise ValueError(f"unknown outcome reason: {self.reason!r}")
   39         object.__setattr__(self, "effects", tuple(self.effects))
   40 
   41     @property
   42     def accepted(self) -> bool:
   43         return self.reason == reasons.ACCEPTED
   44 
   45     def canonical(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   46         return {
   47             "proposal_id": self.proposal_id,
   48             "actor": self.actor,
   49             "sequence": self.sequence,
   50             "operation": self.operation,
   51             "reason": self.reason,
   52             "accepted": 1 if self.accepted else 0,
   53             "effects": [effect.canonical() for effect in self.effects],
   54             "action_id": self.action_id,
   55             "reservation": self.reservation.canonical() if self.reservation is not None else None,
   56         }
   57 
   58 
   59 @dataclass(frozen=True)
   60 class TickRecord:
   61     tick: int
   62     prior_state_digest: str
   63     next_state_digest: str
   64     rotated_roster: tuple[str, ...]
   65     outcomes: tuple[ProposalOutcome, ...]
   66     schema_version: str = SCHEMA_VERSION
   67     engine_version: str = ENGINE_VERSION
   68 
   69     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
   70         object.__setattr__(self, "rotated_roster", tuple(self.rotated_roster))
   71         object.__setattr__(self, "outcomes", tuple(self.outcomes))
   72 
   73     def outcomes_for(self, proposal_id: str) -> tuple[ProposalOutcome, ...]:
   74         """Every outcome under this identity. More than one means it was a duplicate."""
   75         return tuple(outcome for outcome in self.outcomes if outcome.proposal_id == proposal_id)
   76 
   77     def canonical(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   78         """A fresh copy each call, so an observer that edits it edits only its copy."""
   79         return {
   80             "schema_version": self.schema_version,
   81             "engine_version": self.engine_version,
   82             "tick": self.tick,
   83             "prior_state_digest": self.prior_state_digest,
   84             "next_state_digest": self.next_state_digest,
   85             "rotated_roster": list(self.rotated_roster),
   86             "outcomes": [outcome.canonical() for outcome in self.outcomes],
   87         }
   88 
   89     def digest(self) -> str:
   90         return canonical_digest(self.canonical())
